| | discrete (第2次) | /dɪˈskriːt/ | adj. 分离的，离散的；<br>各别的 | discrete units 独立单元<br>discrete categories 离散类别<br>discrete steps 分步步骤 | The project was divided into several **discrete** stages. （该项目被分为几个独立的阶段。）<br>These are two **discrete** problems and should be treated separately. （这是两个互不相干的问题，应该分开处理。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：separate（分离的）· **易混**：discrete（离散的）vs discreet（谨慎的，拼写极像！）|
| | retention | /rɪˈtenʃn/ | n. 保留，保持；<br>记忆力 | retention rate 保留率<br>memory retention 记忆力<br>water retention 水分保持<br>employee retention 员工留任 | Regular review helps improve memory **retention**. （定期复习有助于提高记忆力。）<br>The company focuses on employee **retention** strategies. （公司专注于员工留任策略。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：preservation（保存）/ maintenance（维持）· **易混**：retention（保留/记忆力）vs retain（动词，保留）vs attention（注意）|
| | segregate | /ˈseɡrɪɡeɪt/ | v. 隔离，分开；<br>实行种族隔离 | segregate sb from 把某人与…隔离<br>segregate by race 按种族隔离<br>segregated schools 种族隔离学校<br>segregation n. 隔离 | The hospital **segregated** patients with infectious diseases. （医院隔离了传染病患者。）<br>In the past, schools were **segregated** by race. （过去，学校按种族实行隔离。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：separate（分开）/ isolate（隔离）· **反义**：integrate（融合）· **易混**：segregate（隔离/种族隔离）vs aggregate（聚集）|
| | naive | /naɪˈiːv/ | adj. 天真的，幼稚的；<br>轻信的 | naive belief 天真的信念<br>naive optimism 盲目的乐观<br>be naive to think 天真地认为<br>naively adv. 天真地 | It was **naive** of him to trust a stranger. （他信任一个陌生人，太天真了。）<br>Don't be so **naive** — not everyone means well. （别那么天真——不是每个人都心怀善意。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：innocent（天真的）/ gullible（轻信的）· **反义**：sophisticated（世故的）/ cunning（狡猾的）· **易混**：naive（天真幼稚）vs native（本地人/本土的）|
| | mortal | /ˈmɔːrtl/ | adj. 终有一死的；<br>致命的<br>n. 凡人 | mortal danger 致命危险<br>mortal wound 致命伤<br>mortal enemy 死敌<br>mere mortal 区区凡人 | All human beings are **mortal**. （人终有一死。）<br>He suffered a **mortal** wound in the battle. （他在战斗中受了致命伤。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：fatal（致命的）/ deadly（致命的）· **反义**：immortal（不朽的）· **易混**：mortal（致死的/凡人）vs moral（道德的）vs morale（士气）|
| | shrink | /ʃrɪŋk/ | v. 收缩，缩小；<br>退缩，畏缩<br>n. 心理医生（俚语） | shrink from 退缩，回避<br>shrink in size 尺寸缩小<br>shrink dramatically 急剧萎缩<br>not shrink from sth 不畏惧… | The sweater **shrank** after washing. （毛衣洗后缩水了。）<br>He never **shrank** from a challenge. （他从不畏惧挑战。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：contract（收缩）/ recoil（退缩）· **反义**：expand（扩张）· **易混**：shrink（收缩/退缩）vs shrug（耸肩）vs shrine（圣地）|
| | sphere | /sfɪr/ | n. 球体，球形；<br>领域，范围 | sphere of influence 势力范围<br>in the public sphere 在公共领域<br>political sphere 政治领域<br>spherical adj. 球形的 | The Earth is an oblate **sphere**. （地球是一个扁球体。）<br>His work lies within the **sphere** of public health. （他的工作属于公共卫生领域。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：domain（领域）/ field（领域）· **易混**：sphere（球体/领域）vs spear（矛）vs spare（空闲的）|
| | loop | /luːp/ | n. 圈，环；<br>循环<br>v. 使成环，绕圈 | closed loop 闭环<br>loop around 环绕<br>in the loop 在圈内，知情<br>keep sb in the loop 让某人知情 | The road **loops** around the mountain. （道路环绕着山。）<br>Please keep me in the **loop** about the project. （请随时让我了解项目进展。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：circle（圆圈）/ cycle（循环）· **易混**：loop（环/循环）vs loom（织布机/隐约出现）vs loot（抢劫）|
| | radius | /ˈreɪdiəs/ | n. 半径；<br>范围，半径范围 | within a radius of 在…半径范围内<br>blast radius 爆炸半径<br>radius of influence 影响范围<br>plural: radii | The earthquake was felt within a **radius** of 100 miles. （地震在半径100英里范围内都有震感。）<br>The **radius** of a circle is half its diameter. （圆的半径是直径的一半。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：range（范围）/ diameter（直径）· **易混**：radius（半径）vs radium（镭，化学元素）vs ratio（比率）|
| | monument | /ˈmɑːnjumənt/ | n. 纪念碑，纪念物；<br>历史遗迹 | historical monument 历史遗迹<br>national monument 国家纪念碑<br>monument to …的纪念碑<br>monumental adj. 巨大的；纪念碑的 | The **monument** was built to honor the war heroes. （这座纪念碑是为了纪念战争英雄而建。）<br>The Great Wall is a famous Chinese **monument**. （长城是著名的中国历史遗迹。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：memorial（纪念馆）/ landmark（地标）· **易混**：monument（纪念碑/遗迹）vs moment（时刻）vs monetary（货币的）|
| | resultant | /rɪˈzʌltənt/ | adj. 因而发生的，<br>随之产生的 | resultant effect 由此产生的效果<br>resultant force 合力（物理）<br>with resultant benefits 带来随之而来的好处<br>result from 由…导致（动词短语） | The storm caused flooding, with **resultant** damage to homes. （暴风雨引发洪水，进而导致房屋受损。）<br>The **resultant** confusion delayed the project. （随之而来的混乱延误了项目。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：consequent（随之发生的）/ ensuing（继而发生的）· **易混**：resultant（因而发生的，形容词）vs resulting（由…导致的）vs result（结果，名词/动词）|
| | seminar | /ˈsemɪnɑːr/ | n. 研讨会，讨论班；<br>（大学）专题研讨课 | attend a seminar 参加研讨会<br>give a seminar 举办研讨课<br>graduate seminar 研究生研讨课<br>seminar room 研讨室 | The professor will give a **seminar** on climate change. （教授将举办一场关于气候变化的研讨课。）<br>Students are required to attend weekly **seminars**. （学生需要参加每周的研讨课。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：workshop（研讨会/工作坊）/ lecture（讲座）· **易混**：seminar（研讨班）vs seminary（神学院）vs similar（相似的）|
| 2026-06-10 | toss | /tɔːs/ | v. 扔，抛，掷；<br>甩（头/头发）；<br>翻动（食物）；<br>（使）摇摆，颠簸<br>n. 扔，抛；掷硬币决定 | toss a coin 掷硬币<br>toss and turn 辗转反侧<br>toss sth into/onto 把某物扔进/到…<br>win/lose the toss 猜中/猜错硬币 | He **tossed** the ball to his son. （他把球扔给儿子。）<br>I was **tossing** and turning all night. （我整晚辗转反侧。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：throw（扔，更通用）, flip（轻抛，如硬币）, hurl（猛掷）· **易混**：toss vs throw（toss 更随意/轻抛，throw 更用力/正式）|
| | municipal (第2次) | /mjuːˈnɪsɪpl/ | adj. 市的，市政的 | municipal government 市政府<br>municipal council 市议会<br>municipal services 市政服务<br>municipal bond 市政债券 | The **municipal** government plans to build a new library. （市政府计划建造一座新图书馆。）<br>The **municipal** election will be held next month. （市政选举将于下月举行。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：civic（市民的/市政的）· **反义**：federal（联邦的）/ rural（乡村的）· **易混**：municipal（市政的）vs metropolitan（大都市的）|
| | persevere | /ˌpɜːrsəˈvɪr/ | v. 坚持不懈，锲而不舍 | persevere with/in 坚持做…<br>persevere through 熬过，挺过<br>persevere despite 尽管…仍然坚持 | She **persevered** with her studies despite the difficulties. （尽管困难重重，她仍坚持学业。）<br>If you **persevere**, you will eventually succeed. （只要坚持不懈，你终会成功。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：persist（坚持，偏固执）/ insist（坚持主张）· **反义**：give up（放弃）· **易混**：persevere（褒义坚持）vs persist（中性/偏贬固执）|
| | mandate | /ˈmændeɪt/ | n. 授权，委任；<br>（政府的）任期；命令<br>v. 授权；强制执行 | mandate to do sth 做某事的授权<br>under a mandate 根据授权<br>mandate reform 强制改革<br>presidential mandate 总统任期 | The government has a clear **mandate** to reform education. （政府有明确的授权来改革教育。）<br>The new law **mandates** stricter safety standards. （新法律强制要求更严格的安全标准。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：authorize（授权）/ decree（法令）· **易混**：mandate（授权/命令）vs mandatory（强制性的，形容词）vs maneuver（操纵）|
| | paralyze | /ˈpærəlaɪz/ | v. 使瘫痪，使麻痹；<br>使陷入瘫痪，使不能正常运作 | be paralyzed with fear 吓呆了<br>paralyze the economy 使经济瘫痪<br>partially paralyzed 部分瘫痪<br>paralyzed from the waist down 腰部以下瘫痪 | The accident **paralyzed** him from the waist down. （那场事故导致他腰部以下瘫痪。）<br>The strike **paralyzed** the city's transportation system. （罢工使城市交通系统陷入瘫痪。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cripple（使瘫痪/严重削弱）/ disable（使丧失能力）· **易混**：paralyze（美式）vs paralyse（英式拼写）vs paradise（天堂）|
| | discern | /dɪˈsɜːrn/ | v. 辨别，识别；<br>觉察出，看出 | discern A from B 区分A与B<br>discern the difference 辨别差异<br>barely discernible 几乎无法辨认<br>discern a pattern 看出规律 | It is difficult to **discern** the truth from the rumors. （很难从谣言中辨别真相。）<br>She could **discern** a faint outline in the darkness. （她在黑暗中能看出一个模糊的轮廓。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：distinguish（区分）/ perceive（察觉）· **易混**：discern（辨别/觉察）vs concern（关心）vs discrete（离散的）|
| | strenuous | /ˈstrenjuəs/ | adj. 费力的，艰苦的；<br>奋力的，努力的 | strenuous exercise 剧烈运动<br>strenuous efforts 艰苦的努力<br>strenuous objection 强烈反对<br>strenuously adv. 奋力地 | Avoid **strenuous** exercise after a heavy meal. （饱餐后避免剧烈运动。）<br>They made **strenuous** efforts to finish the project on time. （他们为按时完成项目付出了艰苦的努力。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：arduous（艰巨的）/ vigorous（充满活力的）· **反义**：effortless（毫不费力的）· **易混**：strenuous（费力的）vs strenuous（发音像"死拽牛"——累得要死拽着一头牛）|
| | suffice (第2次) | /səˈfaɪs/ | v. 足够，足以；<br>（使）满足 | suffice (it) to say (that) … 只需说…就够了<br>suffice for 满足…的需要<br>Suffice it to say 简而言之 | **Suffice** it to say that the meeting was not productive. （只能说这次会议毫无成效。）<br>A simple "no" will **suffice**. （简单说个"不"就够了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：be enough（更口语化）/ be adequate（足够）· **易混**：suffice（足够）vs satisfy（使满足）vs sacrifice（牺牲）|
| | stamp | /stæmp/ | n. 邮票；印章，图章；<br>印记，特征<br>v. 跺（脚）；盖印于；<br>贴上邮票 | postage stamp 邮票<br>rubber stamp 橡皮图章；走过场式批准<br>stamp one's foot 跺脚<br>bear the stamp of 带有…的特征 | She collects **stamps** from around the world. （她收集来自世界各地的邮票。）<br>He **stamped** his foot in anger. （他愤怒地跺脚。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：seal（印章/封印）/ imprint（印记）· **易混**：stamp（邮票/跺脚）vs stump（树桩；难倒）vs swamp（沼泽）|
| | ruthless (第2次) | /ˈruːθləs/ | adj. 无情的，残忍的；<br>不留情面的，坚决的 | ruthless competition 残酷竞争<br>ruthless efficiency 毫不留情的高效<br>ruthless criticism 毫不留情的批评 | The market is **ruthless** — only the best survive. （市场是残酷的——只有最强者才能生存。）<br>She was **ruthless** in her pursuit of success. （她在追求成功方面毫不留情。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：merciless（冷酷无情）/ relentless（无情的，持续不断的）· **反义**：merciful（仁慈的）· **易混**：ruthless（无情）vs reckless（鲁莽）|
| | nonsense | /ˈnɑːnsens/ | n. 胡说，废话；<br>愚蠢的行为 | talk nonsense 胡说八道<br>make nonsense of 使…变得荒谬<br>absolute/utter nonsense 纯粹胡说<br>no-nonsense adj. 严肃的，务实的 | What he said was complete **nonsense**. （他说的话完全是胡说八道。）<br>Stop this **nonsense** and get back to work! （别胡闹了，回去干活！） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：rubbish（废话，英式）/ absurdity（荒谬）· **反义**：sense（道理）/ reason（理性）· **易混**：nonsense（胡说）vs nuisance（讨厌的人/事）|
| | contagious (第2次) | /kənˈteɪdʒəs/ | adj. 传染的；<br>有感染力的 | contagious disease 传染病<br>contagious laughter 有感染力的笑声<br>highly contagious 高度传染性的 | Yawning is **contagious** — once one person starts, everyone follows. （打哈欠是会传染的——一个人开始，大家都跟着打。）<br>Her optimism is **contagious** and lifts everyone's spirits. （她的乐观很有感染力，提升了每个人的精神。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：infectious（传染的/有感染力的）· **核心区分**：contagious 强调**接触**传染（如接触传播的疾病），infectious 强调**通过空气等介质**传染（如流感 airborne）|
| | fluctuate | /ˈflʌktʃueɪt/ | v. 波动，起伏；<br>变动，不稳定 | fluctuate between A and B 在A与B之间波动<br>fluctuate wildly 剧烈波动<br>fluctuate with 随…波动<br>fluctuation n. 波动 | The stock market has been **fluctuating** wildly this week. （本周股市一直在剧烈波动。）<br>His mood **fluctuates** between excitement and anxiety. （他的情绪在兴奋和焦虑之间波动。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：swing（摇摆，偏剧烈）/ vary（变化，更通用）· **反义**：stabilize（稳定）· **易混**：fluctuate（波动）vs fluent（流利的）vs fluctuate vs flux（变迁）|
| | kidnap | /ˈkɪdnæp/ | v. 绑架，劫持<br>n. 绑架（罪） | kidnap sb for ransom 绑票勒索<br>attempted kidnap 绑架未遂<br>kidnapper n. 绑匪 | The businessman was **kidnapped** on his way home. （那位商人在回家路上被绑架了。）<br>The child was **kidnapped** from the playground. （那孩子从操场上被拐走了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：abduct（诱拐，更正式）/ hijack（劫持交通工具）· **易混**：kidnap（kid + nap = 小孩+抓走 → 绑架）vs kidnap vs hijack（kidnap 绑人，hijack 劫车/机）|
| | arena | /əˈriːnə/ | n. 竞技场，运动场；<br>领域，舞台 | sports arena 体育竞技场<br>political arena 政治舞台<br>enter the arena 进入竞技场/领域<br>gladiatorial arena 角斗场 | The new sports **arena** can seat 20,000 people. （新的体育竞技场可容纳2万人。）<br>The company is preparing to enter the global **arena**. （公司正准备进入全球舞台。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stadium（体育场，更具体）/ field（领域）· **易混**：arena（竞技场/领域）vs area（区域）vs antenna（天线）|
| | sore | /sɔːr/ | adj. 疼痛的，酸痛的；<br>恼火的<br>n. 痛处，疮 | sore throat 喉咙痛<br>sore muscles 肌肉酸痛<br>sore point/spot 痛点，伤心事<br>stick out like a sore thumb 格外扎眼 | I have a **sore** throat and a runny nose. （我喉咙痛还流鼻涕。）<br>He is still **sore** about losing the game. （他对输了比赛仍然耿耿于怀。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：painful（疼痛的）/ aching（酸痛的）· **易混**：sore（酸痛/恼火）vs soar（飙升/翱翔）vs sour（酸的）|
| | decimal (第2次) | /ˈdesɪml/ | adj. 十进制的，小数的<br>n. 小数 | decimal system 十进制<br>decimal point 小数点<br>decimal place 小数位 | 0.5 is a **decimal** number. （0.5是一个小数。）<br>The **decimal** system is based on powers of ten. （十进制以10的幂为基础。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：fractional（小数的）· **易混**：decimal（小数/十进制）vs decade（十年）vs decent（得体的）|
| | formation (第2次) | /fɔːrˈmeɪʃn/ | n. 形成，构成；队形 | rock formation 岩层<br>in formation 排成队形<br>word formation 构词法 | The **formation** of the committee took months. （委员会的组建花了几个月。）<br>Birds flew in perfect **formation**. （鸟儿排成完美队形飞翔。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：structure（结构）/ configuration（构造）· **易混**：formation（形成）vs information（信息）|
| | dual (第2次) | /ˈduːəl/ | adj. 双重的，两部分的 | dual role 双重角色<br>dual citizenship 双重国籍<br>dual purpose 双重目的 | She has **dual** nationality — British and French. （她有双重国籍——英国和法国。）<br>The course serves a **dual** purpose of training and networking. （这门课有培训和社交双重目的。） <br><br>🔍 **反义**：single（单一的）· **易混**：dual（双重的）vs duel（决斗，同音！）vs duct（管道）|
| | plagiarism (第2次) | /ˈpleɪdʒərɪzəm/ | n. 抄袭，剽窃 | commit plagiarism 剽窃<br>academic plagiarism 学术剽窃<br>plagiarism detector 查重软件 | The author was fired for **plagiarism**. （那位作者因剽窃被解雇。）<br>Universities use **plagiarism** checkers to detect copied work. （大学用查重软件检测抄袭内容。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：copyright infringement（版权侵权）· **易混**：plagiarism（剽窃）vs plagiarism ≠ quotation（引用）|
| | moderate | /ˈmɑːdərət/ | adj. 适度的，中等的；温和的<br>v. 缓和，调节 | moderate exercise 适度运动<br>moderate price 中等价位<br>moderate views 温和观点<br>moderate a debate 主持辩论 | A **moderate** amount of exercise is good for health. （适度运动有益健康。）<br>He was asked to **moderate** the panel discussion. （他被邀请主持小组讨论。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mild（温和的）/ temper（调和）· **反义**：extreme（极端的）· **易混**：moderate（适度/温和）vs modern（现代）vs modest（谦虚）|
| | elicit | /ɪˈlɪsɪt/ | v. 引出，探出；<br>诱出（信息/反应） | elicit a response 引出回应<br>elicit information 探出信息<br>elicit sympathy 博取同情<br>elicit laughter 引人发笑 | His joke **elicited** laughter from the audience. （他的笑话引起了观众的笑声。）<br>The police tried to **elicit** information from the suspect. （警方试图从嫌疑人口中探出信息。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：draw out（引出）/ evoke（唤起）· **易混**：elicit（引出）vs illicit（非法的，同音！）vs explicit（明确的）|
| | bounce | /baʊns/ | v. 弹起，反弹；<br>蹦跳<br>n. 弹跳；活力 | bounce back 反弹，恢复<br>bounce a check 空头支票<br>bounce off 从…弹开<br>bounce ideas off sb 和某人交流想法 | The ball **bounced** over the fence. （球弹跳过了围栏。）<br>The company **bounced** back after the crisis. （公司在危机后恢复了过来。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：rebound（反弹）/ spring（弹跳）· **易混**：bounce（弹跳）vs bound（必定/跳跃）vs announce（宣布）|
| | primitive | /ˈprɪmətɪv/ | adj. 原始的，早期的；<br>简陋的，粗糙的 | primitive society 原始社会<br>primitive technology 原始技术<br>primitive instincts 原始本能 | The cave contains **primitive** drawings from thousands of years ago. （洞穴里有数千年前的原始壁画。）<br>The tools were rather **primitive** by modern standards. （以现代标准来看，那些工具相当简陋。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ancient（古代的）/ crude（粗糙的）· **反义**：advanced（先进的）/ sophisticated（精密的）· **易混**：primitive（原始）vs primary（首要）vs prime（主要的）|
| | cylinder | /ˈsɪlɪndər/ | n. 圆柱体；<br>气缸 | cylinder head 气缸盖<br>four-cylinder engine 四缸发动机<br>gas cylinder 煤气罐，气瓶 | The engine has six **cylinders**. （这台发动机有六个气缸。）<br>A **cylinder** is a three-dimensional shape with two circular ends. （圆柱体是有两个圆形端面的三维形状。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：tube（管子）/ barrel（圆筒）· **易混**：cylinder（圆柱/气缸）vs calendar（日历）|
| | subjective | /səbˈdʒektɪv/ | adj. 主观的；<br>个人的 | subjective judgment 主观判断<br>subjective experience 主观体验<br>subjective vs objective 主观vs客观 | Her opinion is highly **subjective** and not based on facts. （她的观点非常主观，不以事实为依据。）<br>Taste is a very **subjective** matter. （口味是非常主观的事情。） <br><br>🔍 **反义**：objective（客观的）· **易混**：subjective（主观的）vs subject（主题/科目）vs subjective ≠ subject to（受…支配）|
| | shuttle | /ˈʃʌtl/ | n. 穿梭班车/航班；<br>梭子<br>v. 穿梭往返 | space shuttle 航天飞机<br>shuttle bus 穿梭巴士<br>shuttle between A and B 在A与B间穿梭 | A free **shuttle** bus runs between the airport and the hotel. （机场和酒店之间有免费穿梭巴士。）<br>He **shuttles** between Beijing and Shanghai every week. （他每周在北京和上海之间穿梭。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：shuttle（穿梭）vs shutter（快门/百叶窗）vs shudder（颤抖）|
| | clause | /klɔːz/ | n. 从句，分句；<br>（法律文件的）条款 | main clause 主句<br>subordinate clause 从句<br>contract clause 合同条款<br>confidentiality clause 保密条款 | A complex sentence usually contains a main **clause** and a subordinate **clause**. （复合句通常包含一个主句和一个从句。）<br>There is a penalty **clause** in the contract. （合同中有罚则条款。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：clause（从句/条款）vs cause（原因）vs claws（爪子）|
| | lamp | /læmp/ | n. 灯 | desk lamp 台灯<br>street lamp 路灯<br>table lamp 台灯<br>lamp post 路灯柱 | She turned on the desk **lamp** to read. （她打开台灯看书。）<br>The street **lamps** came on at dusk. （路灯在黄昏时亮了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：light（灯，更通用）/ lantern（灯笼）· **易混**：lamp（灯）vs lamb（羔羊）vs limp（跛行）|
| | sophomore | /ˈsɑːfəmɔːr/ | n.（大学）二年级学生 | sophomore year 大二<br>sophomore slump 大二低谷 | He is a **sophomore** at Harvard. （他是哈佛大学二年级学生。）<br>The **sophomore** course covers advanced topics. （大二课程涵盖进阶内容。） <br><br>🔍 **关联**：freshman（大一）→ sophomore（大二）→ junior（大三）→ senior（大四）· **易混**：sophomore vs sophisticated（复杂的/世故的）|
| | beware | /bɪˈwer/ | v. 当心，小心，提防 | beware of 当心…<br>beware the danger 小心危险<br>buyer beware 买者自负 | **Beware** of pickpockets in crowded areas. （在拥挤区域小心扒手。）<br>**Beware** of promises that sound too good to be true. （小心听起来好得离谱的承诺。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：watch out（小心）/ be cautious（谨慎）· **易混**：beware（小心）vs aware（意识到的）· beware 只用原形，不用于进行时|
| | famine | /ˈfæmɪn/ | n. 饥荒；<br>严重短缺 | severe famine 严重饥荒<br>famine relief 饥荒救济<br>water famine 水荒<br>die of famine 死于饥荒 | Thousands of people died in the **famine**. （成千上万的人死于那场饥荒。）<br>The UN sent food aid to the **famine**-stricken region. （联合国向饥荒地区送去了食物援助。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：starvation（饥饿）· **易混**：famine（饥荒）vs feminine（女性的）vs famous（著名的）|
| | vacant | /ˈveɪkənt/ | adj. 空的，空闲的；<br>（职位）空缺的；<br>茫然的 | vacant seat 空座位<br>vacant position 空缺职位<br>vacant expression 茫然的表情<br>vacancy n. 空缺 | The hotel has no **vacant** rooms this weekend. （本周末酒店没有空房了。）<br>He stared into space with a **vacant** expression. （他神情茫然地凝视着远方。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：empty（空的）/ unoccupied（无人占用的）· **易混**：vacant（空置/空缺）vs vacation（假期）vs vacuum（真空）|
| | nucleus | /ˈnuːkliəs/ | n. 核心，中心；<br>原子核；细胞核 | nucleus of …的核心<br>cell nucleus 细胞核<br>atomic nucleus 原子核<br>the nucleus of the team 团队核心 | These four members form the **nucleus** of the team. （这四位成员是团队的核心。）<br>The **nucleus** of an atom contains protons and neutrons. （原子核包含质子和中子。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：core（核心）/ center（中心）· **易混**：nucleus（核心/原子核）vs nuclear（核能的）vs nucleic（核酸的）|
| | fraud | /frɔːd/ | n. 欺诈，诈骗；<br>骗子 | commit fraud 实施诈骗<br>credit card fraud 信用卡诈骗<br>be charged with fraud 被指控欺诈<br>fraudulent adj. 欺诈的 | He was sentenced to prison for **fraud**. （他因诈骗被判入狱。）<br>The investment turned out to be a total **fraud**. （那项投资原来是一场彻头彻尾的骗局。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：deception（欺骗）/ scam（骗局）· **易混**：fraud（欺诈/骗子）vs fraud ≠ fault（错误）vs fraud ≠ flawed（有缺陷的）|
| | patent | /ˈpætnt/ | n. 专利，专利权<br>adj. 显而易见的<br>v. 获得专利 | apply for a patent 申请专利<br>patent protection 专利保护<br>patent pending 专利申请中<br>patently adv. 明显地 | He applied for a **patent** for his invention. （他为他的发明申请了专利。）<br>It is a **patent** lie — no one believes it. （这是明显的谎言——没人相信。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：copyright（版权）/ trademark（商标）· **易混**：patent（专利/明显的）vs patient（病人/耐心的）vs pattern（模式）|
| | intersection (第2次) | /ˌɪntərˈsekʃn/ | n. 十字路口，交叉路口；<br>交点，交叉点 | at the intersection of 在…的交叉处<br>intersection point 交点<br>busy intersection 繁忙的路口<br>intersection of A and B A与B的交集 | Turn left at the next **intersection**. （在下一个十字路口左转。）<br>Math is at the **intersection** of logic and creativity. （数学处于逻辑与创造力的交汇点。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：crossroad（十字路口）/ junction（交叉口）· **易混**：intersection（交叉点/十字路口）vs interaction（互动）vs section（部分）|
| | radius (第2次) | /ˈreɪdiəs/ | n. 半径；<br>范围，半径范围 | within a radius of 在…半径范围内<br>blast radius 爆炸半径<br>radius of influence 影响范围<br>radius of the circle 圆的半径 | The explosion destroyed everything within a 5-mile **radius**. （爆炸摧毁了半径5英里内的一切。）<br>The store delivers within a 10-mile **radius**. （该商店在10英里半径范围内送货。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：diameter（直径）/ range（范围）· **易混**：radius（半径）vs radium（镭，化学元素）vs ratio（比率）|
| | adjacent | /əˈdʒeɪsnt/ | adj. 邻近的，毗连的，<br>相邻的 | adjacent to 与…相邻<br>adjacent rooms 相邻的房间<br>adjacent angles 邻角（数学） | They stayed in **adjacent** rooms at the hotel. （他们住在酒店相邻的房间里。）<br>The park is **adjacent** to the library. （公园紧邻图书馆。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：neighboring（邻近的）/ adjoining（毗连的）· **反义**：distant（遥远的）/ remote（偏远的）· **易混**：adjacent（空间上相邻）vs adjacent ≠ adjoining（adjoining 强调"连在一起"，adjacent 强调"靠近但未必连接"）|
| | equator (第2次) | /ɪˈkweɪtər/ | n. 赤道 | on the equator 在赤道上<br>near the equator 赤道附近<br>cross the equator 穿越赤道<br>celestial equator 天赤道 | Countries near the **equator** have warm climates year-round. （赤道附近的国家全年气候温暖。）<br>The ship crossed the **equator** at noon. （船在正午穿过了赤道。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：equator（赤道）· **易混**：equator（赤道）vs equal（相等的）vs equation（方程）|
| | whereas | /werˈæz/ | conj. 然而，但是；<br>鉴于（法律用语） | whereas clause 鉴于条款<br>…, whereas …  …，然而… | She is very outgoing, **whereas** her husband is quite shy. （她非常外向，而她的丈夫却相当害羞。）<br>**Whereas** the contract states otherwise, this clause shall prevail. （鉴于合同另有规定，以此条款为准。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：while（而，对比）/ but（但是）· **易混**：whereas（然而，对比）vs where（哪里）vs whereby（凭借）|
| | haul (第2次) | /hɔːl/ | v. 拖，拉，拽；<br>费力搬运<br>n. 大批赃物；一大批 | haul sth away 把…拖走<br>a long haul 长途运输；漫长的过程<br>over the long haul 从长远来看<br>haul in 拉进；逮捕 | They **hauled** the heavy furniture up three flights of stairs. （他们把沉重的家具拖上了三层楼。）<br>It's been a long **haul**, but we finally graduated. （这是一段漫长的旅程，但我们终于毕业了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：drag（拖拽）/ tug（用力拉）· **易混**：haul（费力拖运）vs hall（大厅）vs heal（治愈）|
| | consult (第2次) | /kənˈsʌlt/ | v. 咨询，请教；<br>查阅；商量 | consult a doctor 咨询医生<br>consult with sb 与某人商量<br>consult a dictionary 查词典<br>consult an expert 请教专家 | You should **consult** a financial advisor before investing. （投资前你应该咨询理财顾问。）<br>I need to **consult** with my team before making a decision. （做决定前我需要和团队商量。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：confer（商议）/ seek advice（寻求建议）· **易混**：consult（咨询/查阅）vs insult（侮辱）vs result（结果）vs council（委员会）|
| | stair | /ster/ | n. 楼梯，阶梯；<br>（pl. stairs）楼梯 | flight of stairs 一段楼梯<br>climb the stairs 爬楼梯<br>at the top/bottom of the stairs 在楼梯顶部/底部<br>downstairs/upstairs 楼下/楼上 | She ran up the **stairs** to answer the phone. （她跑上楼梯去接电话。）<br>He slipped and fell down the **stairs**. （他滑倒从楼梯上摔了下来。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：staircase（楼梯间）/ step（台阶）· **易混**：stair（楼梯）vs stare（盯着看，同音）vs star（星星）|
| | salient (第3次) | /ˈseɪliənt/ | adj. 显著的，突出的；<br>最重要的 | salient feature 显著特征<br>salient point 要点<br>salient characteristic 突出特点 | The **salient** feature of the policy is its emphasis on equality. （该政策的显著特点是强调平等。）<br>She highlighted the most **salient** arguments in her presentation. （她在演讲中突出了最重要的论点。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：prominent（突出的）/ striking（引人注目的）/ conspicuous（显眼的）· **易混**：salient（突出且重要的）vs salient ≠ salient（≠ silent 沉默的，形近易混）|
| | thermometer | /θərˈmɑːmɪtər/ | n. 温度计，体温计 | clinical thermometer 体温计<br>digital thermometer 电子温度计<br>read a thermometer 看温度计读数<br>Celsius/Fahrenheit thermometer 摄氏/华氏温度计 | The nurse put a **thermometer** under his arm to check his temperature. （护士把温度计放在他腋下量体温。）<br>The **thermometer** showed that it was 38°C outside. （温度计显示外面是38°C。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：thermal（热的，形容词）· **易混**：thermometer（温度计）vs thermal（热量的）vs therapy（治疗）· **词根**：thermo-=热 + meter=测量器|
| | knit (第2次) | /nɪt/ | v. 编织，针织；<br>紧密结合 | knit a sweater 织毛衣<br>knit together 紧密结合<br>close-knit 紧密团结的<br>knit one's brows 皱眉 | She **knitted** a scarf for her grandson. （她为孙子织了一条围巾。）<br>The crisis **knit** the community closer together. （危机让这个社区更加紧密团结。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：weave（编织）/ unite（团结）· **易混**：knit（编织/结合）vs knot（打结）vs kit（工具包）|
| | testify | /ˈtestɪfaɪ/ | v. 作证，证明；<br>表明，说明 | testify in court 出庭作证<br>testify to 证明，说明<br>testify against/for sb 作出对某人不利/有利的证词<br>testimony n. 证词 | The witness refused to **testify** in court. （证人拒绝出庭作证。）<br>His success **testifies** to his hard work and determination. （他的成功证明了他的努力和决心。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：attest（证实）/ certify（证明）· **易混**：testify（作证）vs test（测试）vs testify ≠ terrify（使恐惧）|
| | ascertain | /ˌæsərˈteɪn/ | v. 查明，确定，<br>弄清 | ascertain the facts 查明事实<br>ascertain the cause 确定原因<br>ascertain whether/how 确定是否/如何<br>it is difficult to ascertain 难以确定 | The police are trying to **ascertain** the cause of the fire. （警方正在努力查明火灾原因。）<br>We need to **ascertain** whether the data is accurate. （我们需要确定数据是否准确。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：determine（确定）/ verify（核实）· **易混**：ascertain（查明）vs certain（确定的）vs assert（断言）· **词根**：as-=to + certain=确定 → 使确定|
| | loaf | /loʊf/ | n. 一条（面包）；<br>大块<br>v. 游荡，闲逛 | a loaf of bread 一条面包<br>meat loaf 肉糕<br>loaf around 闲逛，无所事事<br>pl. loaves | She bought a **loaf** of fresh bread from the bakery. （她从面包店买了一条新鲜面包。）<br>Stop **loafing** around and do something useful! （别再闲逛了，做点有用的事！） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：loaf（一条面包/闲逛）vs load（装载/负担）vs leaf（叶子）· **易混**：loaf vs loafer（ loaf+er =  loaf→  loaf+er→ loafer 游手好闲者 / 乐福鞋）|
| | confidential | /ˌkɑːnfɪˈdenʃl/ | adj. 机密的，保密的；<br>秘密的 | confidential document 机密文件<br>confidential information 机密信息<br>strictly confidential 绝密<br>keep sth confidential 对…保密 | All **confidential** documents must be locked in the safe. （所有机密文件必须锁在保险柜里。）<br>The meeting was **confidential** — only senior staff attended. （会议是保密的——只有高级职员参加。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：classified（机密的）/ secret（秘密的）· **反义**：public（公开的）· **易混**：confidential（机密的）vs confident（自信的，形近！）vs confidant（知己）|
| | chalk | /tʃɔːk/ | n. 粉笔；白垩<br>v. 用粉笔写 | a piece of chalk 一支粉笔<br>chalkboard 黑板<br>chalk up 记下；取得（得分/胜利）<br>chalk and cheese 天壤之别 | The teacher wrote on the blackboard with **chalk**. （老师用粉笔在黑板上写字。）<br>The team **chalked** up their third win in a row. （这支队伍取得了三连胜。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：chalk（粉笔）· **易混**：chalk（粉笔）vs talk（说话）vs stalk（茎；跟踪）· 习语：as different as chalk and cheese（截然不同）|
| | grip (第2次) | /ɡrɪp/ | v. 紧握，抓牢；<br>强烈吸引<br>n. 紧握；控制，支配 | get a grip on 掌控<br>lose one's grip 失去控制<br>come to grips with 认真对待<br>in the grip of 受…支配 | She **gripped** my hand tightly in fear. （她害怕地紧紧握住我的手。）<br>The nation is in the **grip** of a severe recession. （国家正陷入严重衰退之中。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：grasp（抓牢/理解）/ clasp（紧握）· **易混**：grip（紧握/控制）vs grab（抢夺）vs gripe（抱怨）|
| | elapse | /ɪˈlæps/ | v.（时间）流逝，<br>消逝 | time elapses 时间流逝<br>three years elapsed 三年过去了<br>before the deadline elapses 在截止日期之前<br>elapsed time 已过时间 | Several months **elapsed** before he replied to my letter. （几个月过去了，他才回我的信。）<br>Only a few seconds had **elapsed** when the alarm went off. （才过了几秒钟警报就响了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：pass（过去，更通用）/ go by（流逝）· **易混**：elapse（时间流逝）vs lapse（失误；失效）vs eclipse（日食/月食）|
| | gown (第2次) | /ɡaʊn/ | n. 长袍，礼服；<br>（手术）罩衣 | evening gown 晚礼服<br>graduation gown 毕业礼服<br>wedding gown 婚纱<br>surgical gown 手术衣 | She looked elegant in her silk evening **gown**. （她穿着丝绸晚礼服，看起来优雅大方。）<br>Graduates wore black **gowns** at the ceremony. （毕业生在典礼上穿着黑色学士袍。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：robe（长袍）/ dress（连衣裙）· **易混**：gown（礼服/长袍）vs crown（皇冠）vs grown（长大的）|
| | welfare | /ˈwelfer/ | n. 福利，幸福；<br>社会保障 | social welfare 社会福利<br>welfare state 福利国家<br>welfare benefits 福利津贴<br>public welfare 公共福利 | The government is committed to improving social **welfare**. （政府致力于改善社会福利。）<br>They are concerned about the **welfare** of the children. （他们关心孩子们的福祉。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：well-being（幸福/安康）/ benefit（福利）· **易混**：welfare（福利）vs warfare（战争）vs farewell（告别）|
| | coherent | /koʊˈhɪrənt/ | adj. 连贯的，一致的；<br>条理清晰的 | coherent argument 连贯的论证<br>coherent policy 一贯的政策<br>coherent explanation 条理清晰的解释<br>coherent whole 统一的整体 | His essay was clear and **coherent**. （他的文章清晰且连贯。）<br>The government lacks a **coherent** strategy on climate change. （政府在气候变化问题上缺乏一贯的策略。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：logical（有逻辑的）/ consistent（一致的）· **反义**：incoherent（不连贯的）· **易混**：coherent（连贯的）vs cohesive（有凝聚力的）vs inherent（固有的）|
| | cereal | /ˈsɪriəl/ | n. 谷物，麦片；<br>谷类植物 | breakfast cereal 早餐麦片<br>cereal crops 谷类作物<br>bowl of cereal 一碗麦片<br>whole-grain cereal 全谷物麦片 | I usually have a bowl of **cereal** for breakfast. （我早餐通常吃一碗麦片。）<br>Wheat and rice are important **cereal** crops. （小麦和大米是重要的谷类作物。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：cereal（谷物/麦片）vs serial（连续的/连载的，同音异义！）vs serious（严肃的）|
| | proposal | /prəˈpoʊzl/ | n. 提议，建议；<br>求婚 | make a proposal 提出建议<br>marriage proposal 求婚<br>reject a proposal 拒绝提议<br>proposal for (doing) sth 关于…的提议 | The committee approved the **proposal** to build a new school. （委员会批准了建一所新学校的提议。）<br>He got down on one knee and made a **proposal**. （他单膝跪地求婚。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：suggestion（建议）/ proposition（提议）· **易混**：proposal（提议/求婚）vs propose（提议，动词）vs purpose（目的）|
| | denounce (第3次) | /dɪˈnaʊns/ | v. 谴责，指责；<br>告发 | denounce sb/sth as 指责…为…<br>denounce a policy 谴责政策<br>be denounced by 被…谴责<br>publicly denounce 公开谴责 | The activist **denounced** the company for exploiting workers. （该活动人士谴责公司剥削工人。）<br>He was **denounced** as a traitor by his former allies. （他被昔日盟友谴责为叛徒。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：condemn（谴责）/ criticize（批评）· **易混**：denounce（公开谴责+告发）vs renounce（宣布放弃）vs announce（宣布）|
| | wicked (第2次) | /ˈwɪkɪd/ | adj. 邪恶的，坏的；<br>淘气的 | wicked witch 邪恶的女巫<br>wicked smile 坏笑<br>wicked sense of humor 恶作剧般的幽默<br>wickedly adv. 邪恶地 | The villain in the story was truly **wicked**. （故事里的反派真是邪恶透顶。）<br>She gave him a **wicked** grin before playing the prank. （在恶作剧前她冲他坏笑了一下。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：evil（邪恶的）/ mischievous（淘气的）· **易混**：wicked（邪恶/淘气）vs witch（女巫）vs wicker（柳条）|
| | territory (第2次) | /ˈterətɔːri/ | n. 领土，领地；<br>领域，范围 | territory dispute 领土争端<br>uncharted territory 未知领域<br>familiar territory 熟悉的领域<br>territorial adj. 领土的 | The two countries are in dispute over the **territory**. （两国就该领土存在争端。）<br>This research takes us into completely new **territory**. （这项研究将我们带入全新的领域。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：domain（领域）/ realm（领域）· **易混**：territory（领土/领域）vs terror（恐怖）vs terrace（阳台）|
| | gloomy | /ˈɡluːmi/ | adj. 阴暗的，阴沉的；<br>沮丧的，悲观的 | gloomy weather 阴沉的天气<br>gloomy outlook 悲观的前景<br>gloomy atmosphere 阴郁的氛围<br>feel gloomy 感到沮丧 | The sky was dark and **gloomy** before the storm. （暴风雨前天空昏暗阴沉。）<br>He felt **gloomy** about his job prospects. （他对就业前景感到悲观。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：dim（昏暗的）/ pessimistic（悲观的）· **反义**：bright（明亮的）/ cheerful（欢快的）· **易混**：gloomy（阴郁的）vs glory（荣耀）vs groom（新郎）|
| | inaugurate | /ɪˈnɔːɡjəreɪt/ | v. 为…举行就职典礼；<br>开创，开启；<br>为…举行落成仪式 | inaugurate a president 总统就职<br>inaugurate a new era 开启新时代<br>inauguration n. 就职典礼；开幕式<br>inaugural address 就职演说 | The new president was **inaugurated** in January. （新总统于一月宣誓就职。）<br>The discovery **inaugurated** a new era in medicine. （这项发现开创了医学的新纪元。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：launch（启动）/ initiate（开始）· **易混**：inaugurate（就职/开创）vs inaugurate ≠ inaugurate（≠ immigrate 移民）vs augment（增加）|
| | enclosure | /ɪnˈkloʊʒər/ | n. 围场，圈地；<br>附件，随信附上之物 | enclosure movement 圈地运动<br>see enclosure 见附件<br>official enclosure 官方附件<br>enclose v. 围住；附入 | The sheep grazed peacefully in the **enclosure**. （羊群在围栏里安静地吃草。）<br>Please find the contract in the **enclosure**. （请在附件中查看合同。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：attachment（附件，邮件）/ compound（围起来的场地）· **易混**：enclosure（围场/附件）vs closure（关闭）vs enclosure ≠ disclosure（披露）|
| | courtesy | /ˈkɜːrtəsi/ | n. 礼貌，谦恭；<br>好意<br>adj. 免费的，赠送的 | as a courtesy 出于礼貌<br>common courtesy 基本礼貌<br>courtesy visit 礼节性拜访<br>courtesy bus 免费班车 | She replied out of **courtesy** rather than interest. （她回信是出于礼貌而非兴趣。）<br>The hotel provides a **courtesy** shuttle to the airport. （酒店提供前往机场的免费班车。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：politeness（礼貌）· **反义**：rudeness（粗鲁）· **易混**：courtesy（礼貌/免费）vs courtesy ≠ curtsy（屈膝礼）vs court（法庭/宫廷）|
| | revenge | /rɪˈvendʒ/ | n. 复仇，报复<br>v. 为…报仇 | take revenge on sb 向某人复仇<br>in revenge for 为报复…<br>seek revenge 寻求报复<br>revenge oneself on 报复某人 | He wanted to take **revenge** on those who had betrayed him. （他想报复那些背叛他的人。）<br>Hamlet sought to **revenge** his father's murder. （哈姆雷特想要为父亲的谋杀报仇。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：vengeance（复仇，名词）/ avenge（为…报仇，动词）· **易混**：revenge（复仇）vs avenge（为别人报仇）· revenge 多为自己，avenge 多为正义/他人|
| | ascribe (第2次) | /əˈskraɪb/ | v. 把…归因于；<br>把…归属于 | ascribe sth to 归因于/归属于<br>be ascribed to 被归因于<br>ascribe blame to 归咎于<br>ascribable adj. 可归因于 | Experts **ascribe** the decline to poor management. （专家将下滑归因于管理不善。）<br>This work is **ascribed** to a student of Leonardo da Vinci. （这件作品被认为是达·芬奇一位学生的作品。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：attribute（归因于）/ owe...to...（归功于）· **易混**：ascribe（归因/归属）vs describe（描述）vs subscribe（订阅/同意）|
| | seminar (第2次) | /ˈsemɪnɑːr/ | n. 研讨会，讨论班；<br>（大学）专题研讨课 | attend a seminar 参加研讨会<br>give a seminar 举办研讨课<br>graduate seminar 研究生研讨课<br>online seminar 线上研讨会 | The professor will give a **seminar** on climate change. （教授将举办一场关于气候变化的研讨课。）<br>I signed up for a **seminar** on creative writing. （我报名了一个创意写作研讨班。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：workshop（工作坊）/ lecture（讲座）· **易混**：seminar（研讨班）vs seminary（神学院）vs similar（相似的）|
| | disposition | /ˌdɪspəˈzɪʃn/ | n. 性情，性格；<br>倾向；安排，处置 | cheerful disposition 开朗的性格<br>disposition to/toward 对…的倾向<br>at one's disposition 由某人支配<br>dispose v. 安排；使倾向于 | She has a cheerful **disposition** and gets along with everyone. （她生性开朗，和谁都合得来。）<br>He showed a **disposition** to take risks. （他表现出冒险的倾向。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：temperament（气质）/ tendency（倾向）· **易混**：disposition（性情/倾向）vs disposal（处理/支配）vs position（位置）|
| | furious | /ˈfjʊriəs/ | adj. 狂怒的，暴怒的；<br>激烈的，猛烈的 | be furious with sb 对某人狂怒<br>be furious at/about sth 对某事愤怒<br>furious pace 极快的速度<br>fury n. 狂怒 | She was **furious** with him for lying. （她因为他说谎而暴怒。）<br>The team worked at a **furious** pace to meet the deadline. （团队以疯狂的速度工作以赶上截止日期。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：enraged（暴怒的）/ intense（激烈的）· **易混**：furious（狂怒的）vs curious（好奇的）vs furious ≠ furious（≠ fury 名词）|
| | tablet | /ˈtæblət/ | n. 药片；平板电脑；<br>碑，匾；便签本 | take a tablet 吃药片<br>stone tablet 石碑<br>drawing tablet 数位板<br>tablet computer 平板电脑 | Take two **tablets** before meals. （饭前服用两片。）<br>He uses a **tablet** to take notes in class. （他用平板电脑在课堂上记笔记。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：pill（药丸）/ slate（石板）· **易混**：tablet（药片/平板）vs table（桌子）vs tablet ≠ tablet（≠ booklet 小册子）|
| | vulgar | /ˈvʌlɡər/ | adj. 粗俗的，庸俗的；<br>粗鲁的 | vulgar language 粗俗的语言<br>vulgar taste 俗气的品味<br>vulgar display of wealth 炫富<br>vulgarity n. 粗俗 | His jokes were considered **vulgar** and offensive. （他的笑话被认为粗俗且冒犯。）<br>She found the gold-plated furniture rather **vulgar**. （她觉得那些镀金家具相当俗气。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：crude（粗鲁的）/ coarse（粗俗的）· **反义**：refined（优雅的）· **易混**：vulgar（粗俗的）vs vulgar ≠ vulgar（≠ popular 流行的）vs vulture（秃鹫）|
| | compress | /kəmˈpres/ | v. 压缩，压紧；<br>精简 | compress data 压缩数据<br>compress sth into 把…压缩成<br>cold/hot compress 冷/热敷<br>compression n. 压缩 | The software can **compress** large files to save space. （该软件可以压缩大文件以节省空间。）<br>He **compressed** the report into two pages. （他把报告精简到两页。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：condense（浓缩/精简）/ squeeze（挤压）· **反义**：expand（扩展）· **易混**：compress（压缩）vs comprise（包含）vs impress（印象深刻）|
| | combat | /ˈkɑːmbæt/ | n. 战斗，格斗<br>v. 与…战斗；防止 | armed combat 武装战斗<br>combat troops 作战部队<br>combat crime/poverty 打击犯罪/贫困<br>combative adj. 好斗的 | The soldiers were trained for close **combat**. （士兵接受了近距离格斗训练。）<br>New measures were introduced to **combat** inflation. （出台了新措施来遏制通货膨胀。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：battle（战斗）/ fight（战斗/对抗）· **易混**：combat（战斗/对抗）vs combine（结合）vs comb（梳子）|
| | versatile | /ˈvɜːrsətl/ | adj. 多才多艺的；<br>多功能的，万用的 | versatile actor 多才多艺的演员<br>versatile tool 多功能工具<br>highly versatile 用途广泛的<br>versatility n. 多功能性 | She is a **versatile** musician who plays five instruments. （她是一位多才多艺的音乐家，会演奏五种乐器。）<br>This **versatile** fabric can be used for clothing and decoration. （这种多功能面料可用于服装和装饰。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：adaptable（适应力强的）/ multifunctional（多功能的）· **易混**：versatile（多才多艺的）vs vertical（垂直的）vs version（版本）|
| | tram | /træm/ | n. 有轨电车 | tram line 有轨电车线路<br>take the tram 乘电车<br>tram stop 电车站<br>tram network 电车网络 | We took the **tram** to the city center. （我们乘有轨电车去市中心。）<br>The old **tram** system is still running in this city. （这座城市的旧有轨电车系统仍在运行。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：streetcar（美式有轨电车）/ cable car（缆车）· **易混**：tram（电车）vs tramp（流浪汉）vs trap（陷阱）|
| | blaze | /bleɪz/ | n. 大火，烈火；<br>闪耀，光彩<br>v. 熊熊燃烧；闪耀 | in a blaze 在一片火光中<br>blaze a trail 开辟道路，做开路先锋<br>blaze with anger 怒火中烧<br>blazing adj. 炽热的 | Firefighters battled the **blaze** for hours. （消防员与大火搏斗了数小时。）<br>The scientists **blazed** a trail in genetic research. （科学家在基因研究领域开辟了新路。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：flame（火焰）/ flare（闪耀）· **易混**：blaze（烈火/闪耀）vs blade（刀片）vs blame（责备）|
| | radiant | /ˈreɪdiənt/ | adj. 光芒四射的，<br>容光焕发的；辐射的 | radiant smile 灿烂的笑容<br>radiant beauty 光彩照人的美<br>radiant energy 辐射能<br>radiate v. 辐射，散发 | She looked **radiant** on her wedding day. （她在婚礼那天容光焕发。）<br>The sun gives off **radiant** heat. （太阳散发辐射热。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：glowing（发光的）/ beaming（喜气洋洋的）· **易混**：radiant（光芒四射的）vs radical（激进的）vs radius（半径）|
| | convene | /kənˈviːn/ | v. 召集，召开（会议）；<br>聚集，集合 | convene a meeting 召开会议<br>convene a committee 召集委员会<br>convene for 为…而集合<br>convention n. 大会；惯例 | The board will **convene** next Monday. （董事会将于下周一召开。）<br>The president **convened** an emergency session of Congress. （总统召集了国会紧急会议。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：assemble（集合）/ summon（召集）· **易混**：convene（召集）vs convene ≠ convene（≠ convince 说服）vs convention（大会/习俗）|
| | penalty | /ˈpenəlti/ | n. 惩罚，处罚；<br>罚金；刑罚 | death penalty 死刑<br>pay a penalty 支付罚金<br>penalty clause 罚则条款<br>penalty kick 点球 | The **penalty** for speeding is a heavy fine. （超速的处罚是重额罚款。）<br>He missed the **penalty** in the final minute. （他在最后一分钟罚失了那颗点球。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：punishment（惩罚）/ fine（罚款）· **反义**：reward（奖励）· **易混**：penalty（惩罚/罚金）vs penal（刑罚的，形容词）vs penalty ≠ penalty（≠ penury 贫穷）|
| | copper | /ˈkɑːpər/ | n. 铜；铜币<br>adj. 铜的，铜色的 | copper wire 铜丝<br>copper coin 铜币<br>copper mine 铜矿<br>coppery adj. 铜色的 | The statue is made of **copper**. （这座雕像由铜制成的。）<br>The roof turned a greenish **copper** color over time. （屋顶随着时间变成了铜绿色。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bronze（青铜）/ brass（黄铜）· **易混**：copper（铜）vs cop（警察，俚语）vs cope（应对）|
| | odds | /ɑːdz/ | n. 可能性，几率；<br>不利条件，逆境 | the odds are that 很可能…<br>against all odds 克服重重困难<br>odds and ends 零碎物品<br>at odds with 与…不一致/争执 | **Odds** are that it will rain tomorrow. （明天很可能会下雨。）<br>Against all **odds**, she won the competition. （她克服万难赢得了比赛。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：probability（概率）/ chances（机会）· **易混**：odds（几率/逆境）vs odd（奇怪的/奇数的，形容词）vs add（增加，同音）|
| | predecessor | /ˈpredəsesər/ | n. 前任，前辈；<br>原有事物，前身 | predecessor in office 前任<br>one's immediate predecessor 直接前任<br>unlike one's predecessor 与前任不同<br>precede v. 先于，在…之前 | The new manager is very different from his **predecessor**. （新经理与他的前任截然不同。）<br>This smartphone is far more advanced than its **predecessor**. （这款智能手机比其前身先进得多。） <br><br>🔍 **反义**：successor（继任者）· **词根**：pre-=前 + de-=离开 + cess-=走 + -or=者 → 先走的人 → 前任 |
| | slaughter | /ˈslɔːtər/ | n./v. 屠杀，屠宰 | slaughterhouse 屠宰场<br>mass slaughter 大屠杀<br>slaughter innocent people 屠杀无辜<br>slaughter animals 屠宰动物 | Thousands of innocent people were **slaughtered** in the war. （战争中成千上万的无辜者被屠杀。）<br>The **slaughter** of whales has been banned internationally. （国际已禁止捕杀鲸鱼。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：massacre（大屠杀）/ butcher（屠宰）· **易混**：slaughter（屠杀）vs laughter（笑声，差一个字母！）vs slaughter ≠ slaughter（≠ slight 轻微的）|
| | penalty (第2次) | /ˈpenəlti/ | n. 惩罚，处罚；<br>罚金；点球 | death penalty 死刑<br>pay a penalty 支付罚金<br>penalty kick 点球<br>heavy penalty 重罚 | The **penalty** for drunk driving has been increased. （酒驾的处罚加重了。）<br>He scored from the **penalty** spot. （他从点球点得分了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：punishment（惩罚）/ fine（罚款）· **易混**：penalty（惩罚）vs penal（刑罚的）vs penury（贫穷）|
| | crane | /kreɪn/ | n. 起重机；鹤<br>v. 伸长（脖子） | crane one's neck 伸长脖子<br>tower crane 塔式起重机<br>crane operator 起重机操作员<br>crane fly 大蚊 | The construction site had several tall **cranes**. （工地上有几台高耸的起重机。）<br>She **craned** her neck to see the stage. （她伸长脖子看舞台。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：crane（起重机/鹤/伸长脖子）vs cane（手杖）vs crane ≠ crane（≠ crash 坠毁）|
| | limp | /lɪmp/ | v. 跛行，一瘸一拐<br>adj. 柔软的，无力的 | limp along 跛行前进<br>limp handshake 无力的握手<br>walk with a limp 跛行<br>limply adv. 无力地 | He **limped** off the field after the injury. （受伤后他一瘸一拐地走下了场。）<br>The flowers looked **limp** after days without water. （几天没浇水花蔫了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stagger（蹒跚）/ floppy（松软的）· **易混**：limp（跛行/无力）vs limb（肢体）vs lump（肿块）|
| | segment | /ˈseɡmənt/ | n. 部分，片段；<br>瓣，段 | market segment 细分市场<br>in segments 分段地<br>segment of society 社会阶层<br>orange segment 橘瓣 | The company targets the luxury **segment** of the market. （公司瞄准奢侈品细分市场。）<br>Each orange **segment** was full of juice. （每瓣橘子都饱满多汁。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：section（部分）/ portion（份额）· **易混**：segment（部分）vs cement（水泥）vs sediment（沉淀物）|
| | epoch | /ˈepək/ | n. 时代，纪元，<br>新纪元 | a new epoch 新纪元<br>epoch-making 划时代的<br>historical epoch 历史时期<br>dawn of an epoch 时代的开端 | The discovery marked a new **epoch** in scientific research. （这一发现标志着科研的新纪元。）<br>We are living in an **epoch** of rapid technological change. （我们生活在一个技术飞速变革的时代。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：era（时代）/ age（时期）· **易混**：epoch（时代/纪元）vs epic（史诗的，拼写像）vs echo（回声）|
| | weave | /wiːv/ | v. 编织，织布；<br>迂回穿行<br>n. 织法 | weave a basket 编篮子<br>weave through traffic 在车流中穿行<br>woven fabric 机织物<br>weaver n. 织工 | She learned to **weave** cloth on a traditional loom. （她学会了在传统织布机上织布。）<br>The motorcycle **wove** through the heavy traffic. （摩托车在拥堵的车流中穿行。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：knit（针织）/ braid（编）· **易混**：weave（编织）vs wave（波浪）vs weaver（织工）|
| | mortgage | /ˈmɔːrɡɪdʒ/ | n. 抵押贷款，按揭<br>v. 抵押 | mortgage loan 按揭贷款<br>take out a mortgage 办理房贷<br>monthly mortgage payment 月供<br>subprime mortgage 次级房贷 | They took out a **mortgage** to buy the house. （他们办了按揭贷款买房子。）<br>The bank refused to **mortgage** the property. （银行拒绝抵押该房产。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：mortgage（抵押贷款，t不发音！）vs mortal（凡人/致命的）vs engage（参与）|
| | masculine | /ˈmæskjəlɪn/ | adj. 男性的，男子气概的；<br>阳性的 | masculine voice 男性嗓音<br>masculine role 男性角色<br>masculine features 男性特征<br>feminine（反）女性的 | He has a deep, **masculine** voice. （他有着低沉富有男子气概的嗓音。）<br>Some jobs are stereotyped as **masculine**. （有些职业被刻板地认为是男性的。） <br><br>🔍 **反义**：feminine（女性的）· **易混**：masculine（男性的）vs muscle（肌肉）vs mask（面具）|
| | league | /liːɡ/ | n. 联盟，社团；<br>级别，档次 | football league 足球联赛<br>in league with 与…勾结<br>not in the same league 不在同一档次<br>league table 排名表 | Their team is top of the **league**. （他们队在联赛中排名第一。）<br>He is not in the same **league** as the world's top players. （他和世界顶级选手不在同一档次。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：alliance（联盟）/ association（协会）· **易混**：league（联盟）vs colleague（同事）vs plague（瘟疫）|
| | mingle | /ˈmɪŋɡl/ | v. 混合，交融；<br>（在社交场合）交际 | mingle with 与…混在一起<br>mingle in society 社交<br>mingle tears with laughter 悲喜交加<br>mingle with the crowd 融入人群 | She was eager to **mingle** with the other guests. （她渴望和其他客人交际。）<br>The smell of coffee **mingled** with the aroma of fresh bread. （咖啡的香味与新鲜面包的香气交融在一起。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mix（混合）/ blend（融合）· **易混**：mingle（混合/交际）vs single（单一的）vs tingle（刺痛感）|
| | abate | /əˈbeɪt/ | v. 减弱，减退，<br>减轻 | show no sign of abating 毫无减弱迹象<br>the storm abated 暴风雨减弱了<br>abate pollution 减少污染<br>abatement n. 减轻 | The storm showed no sign of **abating**. （暴风雨毫无减弱的迹象。）<br>Measures were taken to **abate** the noise pollution. （已采取措施减少噪音污染。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：subside（减弱）/ diminish（减少）· **易混**：abate（减弱）vs debate（辩论）vs rebate（退款）|
| | shuttle (第2次) | /ˈʃʌtl/ | n. 穿梭班车；梭子<br>v. 穿梭往返 | space shuttle 航天飞机<br>shuttle bus 穿梭巴士<br>shuttle between 在…间穿梭 | The company runs a free **shuttle** to the station. （公司有到车站的免费班车。）<br>He **shuttles** between London and Paris for work. （他因工作在伦敦和巴黎之间穿梭。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：shuttle（穿梭）vs shutter（快门）vs shudder（颤抖）|
| | mortgage (第2次) | /ˈmɔːrɡɪdʒ/ | n. 抵押贷款，按揭<br>v. 抵押 | mortgage loan 按揭贷款<br>take out a mortgage 办理房贷<br>monthly mortgage payment 月供 | They took out a 30-year **mortgage** to buy the house. （他们办了30年按揭买房。）<br>The company **mortgaged** its assets to raise funds. （公司抵押资产来筹集资金。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：mortgage（t不发音！）vs mortal（凡人）vs engage（参与）|
| | vulgar (第2次) | /ˈvʌlɡər/ | adj. 粗俗的，庸俗的 | vulgar language 粗俗语言<br>vulgar display of wealth 炫富<br>vulgar taste 俗气品味 | His jokes were **vulgar** and offensive. （他的笑话粗俗且冒犯。）<br>The mansion was a **vulgar** display of wealth. （那豪宅是炫富的庸俗表现。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：crude（粗鲁）/ coarse（粗俗）· **反义**：refined（优雅）· **易混**：vulgar vs vulture（秃鹫）|
| | elicit (第2次) | /ɪˈlɪsɪt/ | v. 引出，探出；<br>诱出 | elicit a response 引出回应<br>elicit information 探出信息<br>elicit laughter 引人发笑 | His speech **elicited** thunderous applause. （他的演讲引来了雷鸣般的掌声。）<br>The detective tried to **elicit** the truth from the witness. （侦探试图从证人那里探出真相。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：evoke（唤起）/ draw out（引出）· **易混**：elicit（引出）vs illicit（非法的，同音！）vs explicit（明确的）|
| | penalty (第3次) | /ˈpenəlti/ | n. 惩罚，罚金；点球 | death penalty 死刑<br>penalty clause 罚则条款<br>pay a penalty 缴罚金 | The **penalty** for late payment is 5% per month. （逾期付款的罚金是每月5%。）<br>He converted the **penalty** to win the match. （他罚进点球赢得了比赛。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：punishment（惩罚）/ fine（罚款）· **易混**：penalty vs penal（刑罚的，形近）|
| | clause (第2次) | /klɔːz/ | n. 从句；条款 | main clause 主句<br>contract clause 合同条款<br>penalty clause 罚则条款 | A complex sentence has a main **clause** and a subordinate **clause**. （复合句有主句和从句。）<br>The contract includes a confidentiality **clause**. （合同包含保密条款。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：clause（从句/条款）vs cause（原因）vs claws（爪子）|
| | convene (第2次) | /kənˈviːn/ | v. 召集，召开 | convene a meeting 召开会议<br>convene a committee 召集委员会<br>convene for 集合 | The UN will **convene** an emergency session. （联合国将召开紧急会议。）<br>The committee **convenes** every Monday. （委员会每周一开会。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：assemble（集合）/ summon（召集）· **易混**：convene vs convince（说服）vs convention（大会）|
| | mingle (第2次) | /ˈmɪŋɡl/ | v. 混合，交融；交际 | mingle with 与…混在一起<br>mingle tears with laughter 悲喜交加<br>mingle in society 社交 | The scents of flowers **mingled** in the garden. （花园里各种花香交融。）<br>She **mingled** with the guests at the party. （她在派对上与宾客交际。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mix（混合）/ blend（融合）· **易混**：mingle vs single（单一）vs tingle（刺痛）|
| | fraud (第2次) | /frɔːd/ | n. 欺诈，诈骗；骗子 | commit fraud 实施诈骗<br>credit card fraud 信用卡诈骗<br>fraudulent adj. 欺诈的 | He was jailed for tax **fraud**. （他因税务欺诈入狱。）<br>The charity turned out to be a complete **fraud**. （那个慈善机构原来是个彻头彻尾的骗局。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：deception（欺骗）/ scam（骗局）· **易混**：fraud vs fault（错误）vs flawed（有缺陷的）|
| | seminar (第3次) | /ˈsemɪnɑːr/ | n. 研讨会，讨论班 | attend a seminar 参加研讨会<br>give a seminar 举办研讨课<br>online seminar 线上研讨会 | I attended a **seminar** on climate policy. （我参加了一个气候政策研讨会。）<br>The professor is giving a **seminar** this afternoon. （教授今天下午要讲一堂研讨课。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：workshop（工作坊）/ lecture（讲座）· **易混**：seminar vs seminary（神学院）vs similar（相似）|
| | superficial | /ˌsuːpərˈfɪʃl/ | adj. 表面的，肤浅的；<br>浅薄的 | superficial wound 表皮伤<br>superficial understanding 肤浅的理解<br>superficial resemblance 表面相似<br>superficially adv. 表面上地 | His knowledge of the subject is only **superficial**. （他对这个课题的了解只是表面的。）<br>The damage to the car was **superficial**. （汽车的损伤只是表层的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：shallow（肤浅的）/ surface-level（表面的）· **反义**：profound（深刻的）/ thorough（透彻的）· **易混**：superficial（表面的）vs superficial ≠ superficial（≠ artificial 人工的）|
| | epoch (第2次) | /ˈepək/ | n. 时代，纪元 | a new epoch 新纪元<br>epoch-making 划时代的<br>historical epoch 历史时期 | The internet marked an **epoch**-making change. （互联网标志了划时代的变革。）<br>We are entering a new **epoch** of AI development. （我们正进入AI发展的新纪元。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：era（时代）/ age（时期）· **易混**：epoch vs epic（史诗的）vs echo（回声）|

| | pedal | /ˈpedl/ | n. 踏板，脚蹬<br>v. 骑，踩踏板 | gas pedal 油门<br>bicycle pedal 自行车脚踏<br>pedal a bike 骑自行车<br>soft pedal 弱音踏板；低调处理 | She pressed the brake **pedal** hard. （她猛踩刹车踏板。）<br>He **pedaled** his bike up the steep hill. （他蹬着自行车上了陡坡。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：treadle（踏板）· **易混**：pedal（踏板/踩踏板）vs petal（花瓣，差一个字母！）vs peddle（叫卖，同音！）|

| | furious (第2次) | /ˈfjʊriəs/ | adj. 狂怒的；激烈的 | be furious with sb 对某人暴怒<br>furious pace 极快速度<br>furious debate 激烈辩论 | She was **furious** when she saw the mess. （看到一片狼藉她暴怒不已。）<br>There was a **furious** debate over the policy. （对该政策展开了激烈辩论。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：enraged（暴怒）/ intense（激烈）· **易混**：furious vs curious（好奇）|

| | bore | /bɔːr/ | v. 使厌烦；钻孔<br>n. 令人厌烦的人/事<br>n. 孔，井（borehole） | be bored with 对…厌烦<br>bore a hole 钻孔<br>bore sb to death/tears 烦死人 | His long speech **bored** the audience. （他冗长的演讲让听众厌烦。）<br>The machine **bored** a hole through the wall. （机器在墙上钻了一个洞。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：drill（钻孔）/ weary（使厌倦）· **易混**：bore（钻孔/厌烦）vs boar（野猪，同音异义！）vs bore ≠ bore（≠ bare 裸露的，同音）|
