| | rage | /reɪdʒ/ | n. 狂怒，盛怒；<br>风靡一时的事物<br>v. 发怒；肆虐 | fly into a rage 勃然大怒<br>be all the rage 风靡一时<br>road rage 路怒症<br>a raging storm 肆虐的暴风雨 | He flew into a **rage** when he heard the news. （听到消息后他勃然大怒。）<br>This new game is all the **rage** among teenagers. （这款新游戏在青少年中风靡一时。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：fury（狂怒）/ anger（愤怒）· **易混**：rage（狂怒）vs rag（破布）vs race（种族/比赛）|
| | ditch | /dɪtʃ/ | n. 沟，渠<br>v. 抛弃，丢弃；<br>（非正式）逃学，翘课 | drainage ditch 排水沟<br>ditch a plan 放弃计划<br>ditch school 逃学<br>last-ditch effort 最后的努力 | The car skidded into a **ditch**. （汽车滑进了沟里。）<br>He **ditched** his old friends when he became famous. （他出名后抛弃了老朋友。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：abandon（抛弃）/ discard（丢弃）· **易混**：ditch（沟/抛弃）vs pitch（投掷/音高）vs hitch（搭便车）|
| | miserable | /ˈmɪzrəbl/ | adj. 痛苦的，悲惨的；<br>令人难受的；少得可怜的 | feel miserable 感到痛苦<br>miserable weather 糟糕的天气<br>lead a miserable life 过着悲惨的生活<br>a miserable failure 惨败 | He felt **miserable** after losing his job. （失业后他感到非常痛苦。）<br>The **miserable** weather ruined our holiday. （糟糕的天气毁了我们的假期。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：wretched（悲惨的）/ unhappy（不幸的）· **反义**：cheerful（快乐的）· **易混**：miserable（悲惨的）vs misery（痛苦，名词）vs miserly（吝啬的）|
| | resume | /rɪˈzuːm/ | v. 恢复，重新开始；<br>继续 | resume work 恢复工作<br>resume talks 重启谈判<br>resume normal operations 恢复正常运行<br>resumption n. 恢复 | After the break, they **resumed** the meeting. （休息后，他们继续开会。）<br>Flights will **resume** next week. （航班将于下周恢复。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：resume /rɪˈzuːm/（恢复）vs résumé /ˈrezəmeɪ/（简历，名词，拼写同但读法不同！）|
| | evade | /ɪˈveɪd/ | v. 逃避，回避；<br>规避 | evade taxes 逃税<br>evade the question 回避问题<br>evade responsibility 逃避责任<br>evasion n. 逃避 | He tried to **evade** paying taxes. （他试图逃税。）<br>She **evaded** my question skillfully. （她巧妙地回避了我的问题。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：avoid（避免）/ dodge（躲避）· **易混**：evade（逃避）vs invade（入侵）vs evade ≠ evade（≠ evade + invade 反义对）|
| | mediate | /ˈmiːdieɪt/ | v. 调解，斡旋；<br>促成 | mediate a dispute 调解争端<br>mediate between A and B 在A与B之间调解<br>mediate a settlement 促成和解<br>mediator n. 调解人 | The UN is trying to **mediate** the conflict. （联合国试图调解这场冲突。）<br>A counselor was brought in to **mediate** between the two sides. （一位顾问被请来在双方之间调解。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：arbitrate（仲裁）/ negotiate（谈判）· **易混**：mediate（调解）vs meditate（冥想，拼写很像！）vs immediate（立即的）|
| | gang | /ɡæŋ/ | n. 一帮，一伙；<br>团伙，帮派 | street gang 街头帮派<br>gang violence 帮派暴力<br>gang up on sb 联合起来对付某人<br>work gang 劳动队 | A **gang** of youths were causing trouble in the park. （一伙年轻人在公园里惹麻烦。）<br>The three kids **ganged** up on the smaller boy. （三个孩子合伙欺负那个小男孩。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mob（暴徒团伙）/ crew（一群）· **易混**：gang（帮派）vs gong（锣）vs gang ≠ gang（≠ gangway 通道）|
| | massacre | /ˈmæsəkər/ | n./v. 大屠杀，残杀 | mass massacre 大屠杀<br>massacre innocent civilians 屠杀无辜平民<br>be massacred in 在…中被屠杀 | Thousands were **massacred** during the war. （战争中成千上万的人遭到屠杀。）<br>The **massacre** shocked the entire world. （那场大屠杀震惊了全世界。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：slaughter（屠杀）/ butcher（屠宰）· **易混**：massacre（大屠杀）vs massacre ≠ massacre（≠ massacre 注意拼写 mass + acre）|
| | bronze | /brɑːnz/ | n. 青铜；青铜色<br>adj. 青铜的 | bronze medal 铜牌<br>bronze statue 铜像<br>Bronze Age 青铜时代<br>bronze skin 古铜色皮肤 | She won a **bronze** medal in the Olympics. （她在奥运会上获得铜牌。）<br>His skin turned **bronze** after a summer at the beach. （在海滩度过一个夏天后他的皮肤变成了古铜色。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：copper（铜，红铜）/ brass（黄铜）· **易混**：bronze（青铜）vs bond（纽带）vs breeze（微风）|
| | charter | /ˈtʃɑːrtər/ | n. 宪章；特许状<br>v. 包租（飞机/船）<br>adj. 包租的 | UN Charter 联合国宪章<br>charter flight 包机航班<br>charter school 特许学校<br>chartered accountant 注册会计师 | The **Charter** guarantees basic human rights. （该宪章保障基本人权。）<br>They **chartered** a plane for the trip. （他们为这次旅行包了一架飞机。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：constitution（宪法）/ lease（租用）· **易混**：charter（宪章/包租）vs chart（图表）vs chapter（章节）|
| | wreck | /rek/ | n. 沉船；残骸；<br>（身体或精神）垮掉的人<br>v. 破坏，毁掉 | ship wreck 沉船<br>car wreck 汽车残骸<br>nervous wreck 精神崩溃的人<br>wreck a plan 破坏计划 | Divers found the **wreck** of an ancient ship. （潜水员发现了一艘古沉船。）<br>The scandal nearly **wrecked** his career. （那场丑闻几乎毁了他的事业。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ruin（毁坏）/ destroy（摧毁）· **易混**：wreck（残骸/毁掉）vs wreck ≠ wreck（≠ wreak 造成伤害，同音）vs wretch（可怜的人）|
| | harness | /ˈhɑːrnɪs/ | n. 马具，挽具<br>v. 控制并利用，治理；<br>给…上马具 | harness solar energy 利用太阳能<br>harness one's skills 发挥技能<br>in harness 在工作中<br>safety harness 安全带 | We need to **harness** the power of technology. （我们需要利用科技的力量。）<br>The engineer found a way to **harness** wind energy. （工程师找到了利用风能的方法。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：utilize（利用）/ exploit（开发）· **易混**：harness（利用）vs harness ≠ harness（≠ harshness 严厉）|
| | exquisite | /ɪkˈskwɪzɪt/ | adj. 精美的，精致的；<br>敏锐的 | exquisite craftsmanship 精湛的工艺<br>exquisite taste 高雅的品味<br>exquisite pain 剧烈的疼痛<br>exquisite detail 精致的细节 | The vase is decorated with **exquisite** carvings. （花瓶上装饰着精美的雕刻。）<br>She has an **exquisite** sense of style. （她有着高雅的时尚品味。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：delicate（精致的）/ refined（优雅的）· **反义**：crude（粗糙的）· **易混**：exquisite（精美的）vs exquisite ≠ exquisite（≠ requisite 必需的）|
| | aerial | /ˈeriəl/ | adj. 空中的，航空的<br>n. 天线 | aerial view 鸟瞰图<br>aerial photography 航拍<br>aerial combat 空战<br>TV aerial 电视天线 | The book includes an **aerial** photo of the city. （书中有城市的航拍图。）<br>They installed a new **aerial** on the roof. （他们在屋顶装了新天线。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：airborne（空中的）· **易混**：aerial（空中的/天线）vs areal（区域的）vs aerial ≠ aerial（≠ serial 连续的）|
| | laser | /ˈleɪzər/ | n. 激光 | laser beam 激光束<br>laser surgery 激光手术<br>laser printer 激光打印机<br>laser focus 极度专注 | The doctor used a **laser** to remove the tumor. （医生用激光切除了肿瘤。）<br>She tackled the problem with **laser** focus. （她以极度专注的态度解决问题。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：laser（激光）vs lager（贮藏啤酒）vs laser ≠ laser（≠ lesser 较小的）|
| | reverse | /rɪˈvɜːrs/ | v./n./adj. 反转，倒转；<br>相反的 | reverse a decision 撤销决定<br>in reverse 倒序地<br>reverse gear 倒车档<br>the reverse side 反面 | The court **reversed** the earlier verdict. （法院推翻了之前的裁决。）<br>Put the car in **reverse** and back out slowly. （挂倒挡慢慢倒出去。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：overturn（推翻）/ invert（倒转）· **反义**：forward（向前）· **易混**：reverse（反转）vs reserve（保留）vs revise（修订）|
| | formidable | /fɔːrˈmɪdəbl/ | adj. 可怕的，令人敬畏的；<br>难对付的 | formidable opponent 可怕的对手<br>formidable challenge 巨大的挑战<br>formidable task 艰巨的任务<br>formidable reputation 令人敬畏的声誉 | She is a **formidable** competitor in the industry. （她是业内一个强大的竞争对手。）<br>They face a **formidable** challenge in funding the project. （他们在项目融资方面面临巨大挑战。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：daunting（令人生畏的）/ intimidating（吓人的）· **易混**：formidable（可怕的）vs formative（形成的）vs formidable ≠ formidable（≠ formal 正式的）|
| | oppress | /əˈpres/ | v. 压迫，压制；<br>使压抑 | oppress the people 压迫人民<br>be oppressed by 受…压迫<br>oppressive regime 暴虐的政权<br>oppression n. 压迫 | The dictator **oppressed** his citizens for decades. （独裁者压迫他的人民长达数十年。）<br>The heat was so **oppressive** that nobody could work. （酷热令人窒息，没人能工作。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：suppress（镇压）/ persecute（迫害）· **反义**：liberate（解放）· **易混**：oppress（压迫）vs suppress（镇压/抑制）vs impress（留下印象）|
| | counsel | /ˈkaʊnsl/ | n. 忠告，建议；<br>律师；辩护人<br>v. 劝告，建议 | legal counsel 法律顾问<br>counsel sb to do 建议某人做<br>keep one's own counsel 不透露自己的计划<br>counseling n. 咨询 | He sought legal **counsel** before signing. （签约前他咨询了法律顾问。）<br>She **counseled** him to be patient. （她劝他要耐心。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：advice（建议）/ attorney（律师）· **易混**：counsel（劝告/律师）vs council（委员会，同音！）vs consul（领事）|
| | concise | /kənˈsaɪs/ | adj. 简洁的，简明的 | concise summary 简明的总结<br>concise explanation 简明的解释<br>clear and concise 清晰简洁<br>conciseness n. 简洁 | Please give a **concise** summary of the report. （请给出报告的简明总结。）<br>His writing style is always **concise** and to the point. （他的写作风格总是简洁切题。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：brief（简短的）/ succinct（简洁的）· **反义**：verbose（冗长的）· **易混**：concise（简洁的）vs precise（精确的）vs concise ≠ concise（≠ excise 切除）|
| | roundabout | /ˈraʊndəbaʊt/ | n. 环岛，转盘路<br>adj. 迂回的，绕圈子的 | traffic roundabout 交通环岛<br>take a roundabout route 绕道<br>roundabout way 迂回的方式<br>in a roundabout way 拐弯抹角地 | Take the second exit at the **roundabout**. （在环岛第二个出口驶出。）<br>He told us the news in a **roundabout** way. （他拐弯抹角地告诉了我们这个消息。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：indirect（间接的）/ circuitous（迂回的）· **反义**：direct（直接的）· **易混**：roundabout（环岛/迂回的）vs roundup（综述）vs runabout（轻便小车）|
| | kit | /kɪt/ | n. 成套工具，工具箱；<br>装备 | first-aid kit 急救箱<br>tool kit 工具箱<br>survival kit 救生装备<br>kit sb out with 给某人装备… | Make sure you have a first-aid **kit** in your car. （确保车里备有急救箱。）<br>The soldiers were **kitted** out with new uniforms. （士兵们配备了新制服。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：gear（装备）/ pack（包）· **易混**：kit（工具箱）vs knit（编织）vs kit ≠ kit（≠ kite 风筝）|
| | integral | /ˈɪntɪɡrəl/ | adj. 不可或缺的，<br>构成整体所必需的 | integral part of 不可或缺的部分<br>play an integral role 发挥不可或缺的作用<br>integral to 对…必不可少 | Communication is an **integral** part of teamwork. （沟通是团队协作不可或缺的一部分。）<br>She is **integral** to the success of the project. （她对这个项目的成功至关重要。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：essential（本质的）/ indispensable（不可或缺的）· **易混**：integral（不可或缺的）vs integrate（融合，动词）vs integrity（正直；完整）|
| | sensational | /senˈseɪʃənl/ | adj. 耸人听闻的，轰动的；<br>极好的，绝妙的 | sensational news 轰动性新闻<br>sensational headline 耸人听闻的标题<br>sensational performance 精彩的表演<br>sense n. 感觉；意义 | The tabloid published a **sensational** story about the scandal. （小报刊登了一篇关于丑闻的耸人听闻的报道。）<br>Her **sensational** debut album sold millions. （她那轰动一时的首张专辑卖出了数百万张。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：dramatic（戏剧性的）/ shocking（令人震惊的）· **易混**：sensational（耸人听闻的）vs sensible（明智的）vs sensitive（敏感的）|
| | factual | /ˈfæktʃuəl/ | adj. 事实的，基于事实的 | factual reporting 事实报道<br>factual error 事实错误<br>factual evidence 事实证据<br>factual information 事实信息 | The article was criticized for lacking **factual** accuracy. （该文章因缺乏事实准确性而受到批评。）<br>We need **factual** evidence, not rumors. （我们需要事实证据，不是谣言。） <br><br>🔍 **反义**：fictional（虚构的）· **易混**：factual（事实的）vs actual（实际的）vs factual ≠ factual（≠ factitious 人为的）|
| | elevate | /ˈelɪveɪt/ | v. 提升，提高；<br>使高尚 | elevate the status 提升地位<br>elevate public discourse 提升公众话语水平<br>elevate sb to 提升某人为<br>elevation n. 提升；海拔 | The policy aims to **elevate** living standards. （该政策旨在提高生活水平。）<br>Good leaders **elevate** those around them. （好的领导者能提升身边的人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：raise（提高）/ promote（提升）· **反义**：lower（降低）· **易混**：elevate（提升）vs elevator（电梯）vs relevant（相关的）|
| | discourse | /ˈdɪskɔːrs/ | n. 话语，论述；演讲<br>v. 讲述，论述 | public discourse 公众话语<br>academic discourse 学术论述<br>political discourse 政治话语<br>oral discourse 口头讲述 | The internet has transformed public **discourse**. （互联网改变了公众话语。）<br>He delivered a lengthy **discourse** on the subject. （他就该主题发表了一篇冗长的论述。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：discussion（讨论）/ conversation（对话）· **易混**：discourse（话语/论述）vs course（课程）vs discourage（使气馁）|
| | peer | /pɪr/ | n. 同龄人；同行<br>v. 仔细看，凝视 | peer pressure 同辈压力<br>peer review 同行评审<br>peer at 凝视<br>peerless adj. 无与伦比的 | Teenagers often give in to **peer** pressure. （青少年常常屈服于同辈压力。）<br>The paper was submitted for **peer** review. （这篇论文提交了同行评审。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：colleague（同事）/ contemporary（同代人）· **易混**：peer（同行/凝视）vs pier（码头，同音！）vs pear（梨）|
| | dismiss | /dɪsˈmɪs/ | v. 不予理会；解雇；<br>驳回 | dismiss as irrelevant 认为无关而不予理会<br>dismiss an idea 否定一个想法<br>dismiss sb from 解雇某人<br>dismissal n. 解雇；不予考虑 | His theory was **dismissed** as unscientific. （他的理论被认为是非科学的而不予理会。）<br>She was **dismissed** from her job for misconduct. （她因行为不当被解雇。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：reject（拒绝）/ fire（解雇）· **易混**：dismiss（解雇/不予理会）vs dismiss ≠ dismiss（≠ demise 死亡）|
| | irrelevant | /ɪˈreləvənt/ | adj. 无关的，不相关的 | be irrelevant to 与…无关<br>consider sth irrelevant 认为无关<br>totally irrelevant 完全无关<br>irrelevance n. 无关 | His comments were completely **irrelevant** to the discussion. （他的评论与讨论完全无关。）<br>Most of the data was dismissed as **irrelevant**. （大部分数据被认为无关紧要而不予理会。） <br><br>🔍 **反义**：relevant（相关的）· **易混**：irrelevant（无关的）vs irreverent（不敬的）vs illiterate（文盲的）|
| | fierce | /fɪrs/ | adj. 激烈的，凶猛的；<br>强烈的 | fierce competition 激烈竞争<br>fierce debate 激烈的辩论<br>fierce loyalty 强烈的忠诚<br>fiercely adv. 激烈地 | There is **fierce** competition for the position. （对这个职位的竞争非常激烈。）<br>The **fiercest** critics admitted the plan was a success. （最激烈的批评者也承认计划是成功的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：intense（强烈的）/ ferocious（凶猛的）· **易混**：fierce（激烈的）vs fierce ≠ fierce（≠ pierce 刺穿）|
| | fundamentally | /ˌfʌndəˈmentəli/ | adv. 根本上地，<br>基础地 | fundamentally change 从根本上改变<br>fundamentally different 本质上不同<br>fundamentally flawed 有根本缺陷<br>fundamental adj. 基本的 | The internet has **fundamentally** changed how we communicate. （互联网从根本上改变了我们的交流方式。）<br>The two approaches are **fundamentally** different. （这两种方法有本质区别。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：essentially（本质上）/ basically（基本上）· **易混**：fundamentally（根本上）vs fundamental（基本的，形容词）vs fund（基金）|
| | rush | /rʌʃ/ | v./n. 冲，匆忙；<br>催促，仓促行事 | rush to do sth 争相做某事<br>rush hour 高峰时间<br>in a rush 匆忙地<br>gold rush 淘金热 | Several journals **rushed** to publish the findings. （几家期刊争相发表这些发现。）<br>I was in such a **rush** that I forgot my keys. （我太匆忙忘了带钥匙。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hurry（着急）/ dash（猛冲）· **易混**：rush（匆忙/冲）vs rash（皮疹/鲁莽的）vs lush（茂盛的）|
| | release | /rɪˈliːs/ | v./n. 释放；发布；<br>发行 | release a statement 发布声明<br>release date 发行日期<br>press release 新闻稿<br>release sb from 使某人摆脱… | The band will **release** their new album next month. （乐队下月将发行新专辑。）<br>He was **released** from prison after ten years. （十年后他被释放出狱。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：publish（发布）/ free（释放）· **易混**：release（释放/发布）vs relieve（缓解）vs realize（意识到）|
| | exquisite (第2次) | /ɪkˈskwɪzɪt/ | adj. 精美的，精致的 | exquisite craftsmanship 精湛工艺<br>exquisite taste 高雅品味 | The museum displays **exquisite** works of art. （博物馆展出精美的艺术品。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：delicate（精致）· **易混**：exquisite vs requisite（必需）|
| | resume (第2次) | /rɪˈzuːm/ | v. 恢复，重新开始 | resume work 恢复工作<br>resume talks 重启谈判 | Classes will **resume** on Monday. （周一复课。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：resume /rɪˈzuːm/ vs résumé /ˈrezəmeɪ/|
| | reverse (第2次) | /rɪˈvɜːrs/ | v./adj./n. 反转，相反 | reverse a decision 撤销决定<br>in reverse 倒序 | The court **reversed** the earlier ruling. （法院推翻了先前裁决。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：reverse vs reserve（保留）vs revise（修订）|
| | aerial (第2次) | /ˈeriəl/ | adj. 空中的；n. 天线 | aerial view 鸟瞰<br>aerial photography 航拍 | The drone captured stunning **aerial** footage. （无人机拍摄了惊艳的航拍画面。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：aerial vs areal（区域的）|
| | roundabout (第2次) | /ˈraʊndəbaʊt/ | n. 环岛；adj. 迂回的 | traffic roundabout 交通环岛<br>roundabout way 迂回方式 | Go straight through the **roundabout**. （直行穿过环岛。） <br><br>🔍 **反义**：direct（直接）· **易混**：roundabout vs roundup（综述）|
| | wreck (第2次) | /rek/ | n. 残骸；v. 毁掉 | ship wreck 沉船<br>nervous wreck 精神崩溃 | The storm **wrecked** several boats. （暴风雨毁了几艘船。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ruin（毁坏）· **易混**：wreck vs wreak（造成）|
| | gang (第2次) | /ɡæŋ/ | n. 帮派，一伙 | street gang 街头帮派<br>gang up on 合伙欺负 | A **gang** of youths were arrested. （一伙年轻人被捕。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：gang vs gong（锣）|
| | counsel (第2次) | /ˈkaʊnsl/ | n./v. 忠告；律师 | legal counsel 法律顾问<br>counsel sb to do 劝告 | She **counseled** patience. （她劝告要耐心。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：counsel vs council（委员会，同音）|
| | considerable (第2次) | /kənˈsɪdərəbl/ | adj. 相当大的，大量的 | considerable amount 大量<br>considerable progress 显著进步 | The project made **considerable** progress. （项目取得显著进展。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：considerable vs considerate（体贴的）|
| | likewise (第2次) | /ˈlaɪkwaɪz/ | adv. 同样地 | do likewise 照着做<br>Likewise! 我也是！ | She left early and I did **likewise**. （她早走了，我也一样。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：similarly（同样）· **易混**：likewise vs likely（可能的）|
| | grief (第2次) | /ɡriːf/ | n. 悲伤，悲痛 | come to grief 失败<br>good grief 天哪！ | She was overcome with **grief**. （她悲痛不已。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：sorrow（悲伤）· **易混**：grief vs brief（简短）|
| | mutter | /ˈmʌtər/ | v./n. 嘀咕，喃喃自语 | mutter to oneself 自言自语<br>mutter under one's breath 低声嘀咕 | He **muttered** something under his breath. （他低声嘀咕了几句。）<br>She walked away **muttering** complaints. （她边走边嘀咕着抱怨。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：murmur（低语）/ whisper（耳语）· **易混**：mutter（嘀咕）vs matter（事情）vs butter（黄油）|
| | undo | /ʌnˈduː/ | v. 解开；撤销；<br>毁掉 | undo a button 解扣子<br>undo the damage 挽回损失<br>undo a mistake 纠正错误 | He **undid** his tie. （他解开了领带。）<br>The scandal **undid** years of good work. （丑闻毁掉了多年的努力。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：reverse（撤销）/ ruin（毁掉）· **反义**：do（做）· **易混**：undo（撤销/解开）vs undue（过度的）|
| | artery | /ˈɑːrtəri/ | n. 动脉；干线，要道 | main artery 主动脉<br>traffic artery 交通要道<br>clogged artery 堵塞的动脉 | The highway is a major **artery** connecting the two cities. （这条高速是连接两城的主干道。）<br>Blocked **arteries** can cause heart attacks. （堵塞的动脉可能导致心脏病。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：vein（静脉）/ route（路线）· **易混**：artery（动脉）vs artillery（火炮）|
| | horn | /hɔːrn/ | n. 角；号角；喇叭 | car horn 汽车喇叭<br>French horn 法国号<br>blow a horn 吹号角<br>on the horn 在打电话 | The driver honked the **horn** impatiently. （司机不耐烦地按喇叭。）<br>Deer shed their **horns** every year. （鹿每年都会脱角。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：horn（角/喇叭）vs hone（磨刀石）vs horn ≠ horn（≠ thorn 刺）|
| | stitch | /stɪtʃ/ | n. 一针，针脚；缝线<br>v. 缝 | stitch a wound 缝合伤口<br>not a stitch of clothing 一丝不挂<br>stitch up 缝合；搞定 | The doctor put three **stitches** in the cut. （医生在伤口上缝了三针。）<br>She **stitched** the tear in her dress. （她缝好了裙子上的裂口。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：stitch（缝线）vs switch（开关）vs hitch（搭便车）|
| | bribe | /braɪb/ | v. 贿赂，收买<br>n. 贿赂 | bribe sb to do 贿赂某人做某事<br>accept a bribe 收受贿赂<br>bribe money 贿赂款<br>bribery n. 贿赂（行为） | He tried to **bribe** the official. （他试图贿赂那位官员。）<br>The politician was accused of taking **bribes**. （那位政客被指控收受贿赂。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：corruption（腐败）· **易混**：bribe（贿赂）vs bride（新娘，差一个字母！）|
| | superb | /suːˈpɜːrb/ | adj. 极好的，卓越的 | superb performance 精彩的表演<br>superb quality 卓越的品质<br>superb view 绝佳的景色 | The hotel offers **superb** views of the mountains. （酒店享有绝佳的山景。）<br>She gave a **superb** performance on stage. （她在舞台上进行了精彩的表演。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：excellent（优秀）/ outstanding（杰出）· **易混**：superb（极好的）vs super（超级的）vs suburb（郊区）|
| | reference | /ˈrefrəns/ | n. 参考，提及；<br>推荐信 | in reference to 关于<br>reference book 参考书<br>for future reference 供日后参考<br>character reference 品德推荐信 | Please keep this document for future **reference**. （请保留此文件供日后参考。）<br>She made no **reference** to the incident. （她对这个事件只字未提。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mention（提及）/ citation（引用）· **易混**：reference（参考）vs reverence（尊敬）vs inference（推论）|
| | discretion | /dɪˈskreʃn/ | n. 谨慎，审慎；<br>自行决定权 | at one's discretion 由某人自行决定<br>use discretion 审慎行事<br>absolute discretion 绝对酌情权 | You can use your own **discretion** in this matter. （这件事你可以自行决定。）<br>The information was shared with the utmost **discretion**. （信息在极为谨慎的情况下被分享。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：prudence（谨慎）/ judgment（判断）· **易混**：discretion（谨慎/酌情权）vs discrete（离散的，拼写极像！）|
| | poll | /poʊl/ | n. 民意调查；投票<br>v. 对…进行民意调查 | opinion poll 民意调查<br>exit poll 出口民调<br>go to the polls 去投票<br>poll sb on sth 就某事调查某人 | The latest **poll** shows the candidate leading. （最新民调显示该候选人领先。）<br>Voters will go to the **polls** next Tuesday. （选民将于下周二投票。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：poll（民调/投票）vs pole（杆/极点，同音）vs pool（游泳池）|
| | pretext | /ˈpriːtekst/ | n. 借口，托词 | on the pretext of 以…为借口<br>under the pretext that 以…为借口<br>a flimsy pretext 站不住脚的借口 | He left early on the **pretext** of feeling ill. （他以身体不适为借口提前离开了。）<br>They invaded under the **pretext** of protecting civilians. （他们以保护平民为借口入侵了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：excuse（借口）/ guise（幌子）· **易混**：pretext（借口）vs pretext ≠ pretext（≠ pretext vs context 背景）|
| | proximate | /ˈprɑːksɪmət/ | adj. 最近的，最接近的；<br>直接的 | proximate cause 直接原因<br>proximate to 接近…的<br>proximate analysis 近似分析<br>proximity n. 接近，邻近 | The **proximate** cause of the accident was speeding. （事故的直接原因是超速。）<br>The building is **proximate** to the train station. （这座建筑靠近火车站。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：nearby（附近的）/ immediate（直接的）· **反义**：remote（遥远的）· **易混**：proximate（最近的）vs approximate（大约的）vs proximity（接近）|
| | glare | /ɡler/ | v./n. 怒视；强光，<br>耀眼的光 | glare at sb 怒视某人<br>in the glare of publicity 在众目睽睽之下<br>anti-glare 防眩光<br>glaring adj. 显眼的；耀眼的 | She **glared** at him across the room. （她从房间那头怒视着他。）<br>The **glare** of the headlights blinded me. （车头灯的强光晃瞎了我。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stare（盯）/ dazzle（炫目）· **易混**：glare（怒视/强光）vs glance（一瞥）vs flatter（奉承）|
| | bronze (第2次) | /brɑːnz/ | n. 青铜；青铜色<br>adj. 青铜的 | bronze medal 铜牌<br>bronze statue 铜像<br>Bronze Age 青铜时代<br>bronze skin 古铜色皮肤 | She won a **bronze** medal in the Olympics. （她在奥运会上获得铜牌。）<br>His skin turned **bronze** after a summer at the beach. （在海滩度过一个夏天后他的皮肤变成了古铜色。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：copper（铜，红铜）/ brass（黄铜）· **易混**：bronze（青铜）vs bond（纽带）vs breeze（微风）|
| | spoil | /spɔɪl/ | v. 破坏，糟蹋；<br>溺爱，宠坏 | spoil the fun 扫兴<br>spoil a child 宠坏孩子<br>spoil for 渴望（打架等）<br>spoils n. 战利品 | The rain **spoiled** our picnic. （下雨毁了我们的野餐。）<br>Grandparents tend to **spoil** their grandchildren. （祖父母往往会溺爱孙辈。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ruin（毁掉）/ indulge（溺爱）· **易混**：spoil（破坏/溺爱）vs soil（土壤）vs boil（煮沸）|
| | communism | /ˈkɑːmjunɪzəm/ | n. 共产主义 | communist party 共产党<br>communist n./adj. 共产主义者/共产主义的 | **Communism** aims to create a classless society. （共产主义旨在创造一个无阶级的社会。）<br>He wrote several books about the history of **communism**. （他写了几本关于共产主义历史的书。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：socialism（社会主义）· **易混**：communism（共产主义）vs community（社区）vs commune（公社）|
| | vanity | /ˈvænəti/ | n. 虚荣，自负；<br>梳妆台 | vanity fair 名利场<br>out of vanity 出于虚荣心<br>vanity mirror 化妆镜 | She refused the award out of false modesty, not **vanity**. （她出于假谦虚拒绝了奖项，而不是虚荣心。）<br>He bought the sports car purely out of **vanity**. （他买那辆跑车纯粹是出于虚荣心。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：conceit（自负）/ pride（骄傲）· **易混**：vanity（虚荣）vs vanish（消失）vs variety（多样性）|
| | jewel | /ˈdʒuːəl/ | n. 宝石，珠宝；<br>瑰宝，精华 | crown jewel 王冠上的宝石；皇冠明珠<br>jewel box 首饰盒<br>jewel in the crown 最珍贵的东西 | The palace contains many priceless **jewels**. （宫殿里存放着许多无价珠宝。）<br>The park is the **jewel** of the city. （这座公园是这座城市的瑰宝。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：gem（宝石）/ treasure（珍宝）· **易混**：jewel（宝石）vs jeweler（珠宝商）vs jewelry（珠宝总称）|
| | wagon | /ˈwæɡən/ | n. 马车，货车；<br>（铁路）货车车厢 | station wagon 旅行车<br>on the wagon 戒酒<br>fall off the wagon 重新开始喝酒 | They loaded the **wagon** with hay. （他们把干草装上了马车。）<br>He's been **on the wagon** for three months now. （他已经戒酒三个月了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cart（手推车）/ carriage（马车）· **易混**：wagon（马车/货车）vs dragon（龙）vs wagon ≠ wagen|
| | superstition | /ˌsuːpərˈstɪʃn/ | n. 迷信；<br>迷信行为 | old superstition 古老的迷信<br>out of superstition 出于迷信<br>superstitious adj. 迷信的 | Many people avoid the number 13 out of **superstition**. （很多人出于迷信避开数字13。）<br>The **superstition** about black cats is common in many cultures. （关于黑猫的迷信在许多文化中都很普遍。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：myth（神话/迷思）/ folklore（民间传说）· **易混**：superstition（迷信）vs superstition ≠ superstition（vs institution 制度）|
| | jurisdiction | /ˌdʒʊrɪsˈdɪkʃn/ | n. 司法权，管辖权；<br>管辖范围 | have jurisdiction over 对…有管辖权<br>within one's jurisdiction 在某人管辖范围内<br>federal jurisdiction 联邦管辖 | The case falls outside this court's **jurisdiction**. （此案不在本法院的管辖范围内。）<br>Immigration is under federal **jurisdiction**. （移民事务属于联邦管辖。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：authority（权力）/ domain（领域）· **易混**：jurisdiction（管辖权）vs jury（陪审团）vs justification（辩护）|
| | menace | /ˈmenəs/ | n. 威胁，危险；<br>令人烦恼的人/事<br>v. 威胁 | a menace to society 社会祸害<br>pose a menace 构成威胁<br>menace with 以…威胁 | The escaped prisoner was described as a **menace** to society. （该逃犯被描述为社会祸害。）<br>Those dogs are **menacing** the neighborhood. （那些狗正威胁着社区的安全。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：threat（威胁）/ danger（危险）· **易混**：menace（威胁/祸害）vs menace ≠ menace（vs mental 精神的）|
| | indignant | /ɪnˈdɪɡnənt/ | adj. 愤怒的，<br>愤愤不平的 | be indignant at/about 对…感到愤慨<br>indignant letter 愤慨的信<br>indignation n. 愤怒，愤慨 | She was **indignant** at being treated unfairly. （她因受到不公平对待而感到愤慨。）<br>His remarks provoked an **indignant** response from the audience. （他的言论引起了观众的愤慨回应。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：angry（生气）/ outraged（震怒）· **易混**：indignant（愤慨的）vs indignant ≠ indignant（vs indigenous 本土的）vs indignant ≠ indignant（vs indignity 侮辱）|
| | commonwealth | /ˈkɑːmənwelθ/ | n. 联邦；<br>共和国；国家 | the Commonwealth 英联邦<br>Commonwealth countries 英联邦国家<br>commonwealth of nations 国家共同体 | Australia is a member of the **Commonwealth**. （澳大利亚是英联邦成员国。）<br>The **commonwealth** was founded on principles of equality. （这个共和国建立在平等原则之上。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：federation（联邦）/ republic（共和国）· **易混**：commonwealth（联邦）vs commonwealth ≠ commonwealth（common + wealth = 公共财富，字面义好记！）|
| | recession | /rɪˈseʃn/ | n. 经济衰退，<br>萧条；退后 | economic recession 经济衰退<br>in recession 处于衰退中<br>deep recession 严重衰退<br>recede v. 后退，减弱 | The country is facing its worst **recession** in decades. （该国正面临数十年来最严重的经济衰退。）<br>Many businesses closed during the **recession**. （经济衰退期间许多企业倒闭了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：depression（萧条，更严重）/ downturn（下滑）· **反义**：boom（繁荣）· **易混**：recession（衰退）vs recede（后退）vs reception（接待）|
| | projector | /prəˈdʒektər/ | n. 投影仪；<br>放映机 | multimedia projector 多媒体投影仪<br>film projector 电影放映机<br>slide projector 幻灯机 | The teacher used a **projector** to show the slides. （老师用投影仪展示幻灯片。）<br>The old **projector** broke down during the movie. （旧放映机在放映电影时坏掉了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：beamer（投影仪，口语）· **易混**：projector（投影仪）vs project（项目/投射）vs projection（投影/预测）|
| | juvenile | /ˈdʒuːvənl/ | adj. 青少年的；<br>幼稚的<br>n. 青少年 | juvenile crime 青少年犯罪<br>juvenile literature 青少年文学<br>juvenile behavior 幼稚行为<br>juvenile delinquent 少年犯 | The program aims to reduce **juvenile** crime. （该项目旨在减少青少年犯罪。）<br>His **juvenile** jokes annoyed everyone. （他幼稚的玩笑惹恼了大家。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：adolescent（青少年）/ childish（幼稚的）· **反义**：mature（成熟的）· **易混**：juvenile（青少年/幼稚）vs junior（年少的/下级）vs juvenile ≠ juvenile（vs rejuvenate 使年轻）|
| | dealer | /ˈdiːlər/ | n. 经销商，商人；<br>发牌者 | car dealer 汽车经销商<br>drug dealer 毒贩<br>art dealer 艺术品交易商<br>deal n./v. 交易，处理 | He bought the car from a used-car **dealer**. （他从一个二手车经销商那里买了这辆车。）<br>The **dealer** shuffled and dealt the cards. （发牌者洗牌并发牌。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：trader（交易者）/ merchant（商人）· **易混**：dealer（经销商）vs dealer ≠ dealer（deal + er = 做交易的人，好记！）vs healer（治疗者）|
| | vocation | /voʊˈkeɪʃn/ | n. 职业；使命感；<br>天职 | sense of vocation 使命感<br>teaching vocation 教学职业<br>find one's vocation 找到自己的天职<br>vocational adj. 职业的 | She felt a strong **vocation** to become a nurse. （她强烈感到当护士是自己的天职。）<br>Teaching is more than a job — it's a **vocation**. （教书不只是一份工作，更是一种使命。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：calling（使命感）/ profession（职业）· **易混**：vocation（职业/使命）vs vacation（假期，只差一个字母！）vs avocation（业余爱好）|
| | intermediate | /ˌɪntərˈmiːdiət/ | adj. 中间的，中级的<br>n. 中级水平者 | intermediate level 中级水平<br>intermediate stage 中间阶段<br>intermediate course 中级课程<br>intermediary n. 中间人 | The course is designed for **intermediate** learners. （该课程专为中级学习者设计。）<br>He acts as an **intermediate** between the two departments. （他充当两个部门之间的中间人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：medium（中等的）/ middle（中间的）· **反义**：beginner（初级）/ advanced（高级）· **易混**：intermediate（中级）vs intermediary（中间人）vs immediate（立即的）|
| | liability | /ˌlaɪəˈbɪləti/ | n. 责任，义务；<br>负担，累赘；<br>债务（复数） | legal liability 法律责任<br>liability for 对…负责<br>become a liability 成为累赘<br>assets and liabilities 资产与负债 | The company accepts full **liability** for the accident. （公司对这起事故承担全部责任。）<br>His lack of experience is a **liability** in this job. （他的经验不足是这份工作的一个短板。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：responsibility（责任）/ obligation（义务）· **反义**：asset（资产/优势）· **易混**：liability（责任/累赘）vs liable（有责任的）vs reliability（可靠性）|
| | scold | /skoʊld/ | v. 责骂，训斥 | scold sb for (doing) sth 因某事责骂某人<br>get a scolding 被训斥<br>scold harshly 严厉责骂 | She **scolded** her son for breaking the window. （她因儿子打破了窗户而责骂他。）<br>He was **scolded** by the boss for being late again. （他因再次迟到被老板训斥了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：rebuke（斥责）/ reprimand（训斥，正式）· **反义**：praise（表扬）· **易混**：scold（责骂）vs cold（冷）vs scald（烫伤）|
| | crude | /kruːd/ | adj. 粗糙的，粗制的；<br>粗鲁的；原油的 | crude oil 原油<br>crude method 粗糙的方法<br>crude language 粗鲁的语言<br>crude estimate 粗略估计 | The pipeline transports **crude** oil from the oil field. （管道从油田输送原油。）<br>His **crude** remarks offended many people. （他粗鲁的言论冒犯了很多人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：raw（未加工的）/ vulgar（粗俗的）/ rough（粗糙的）· **反义**：refined（精炼的）· **易混**：crude（粗糙/原油）vs rude（粗鲁）vs crude ≠ crude（vs cruel 残忍的）|
| | vigorous | /ˈvɪɡərəs/ | adj. 精力充沛的；<br>强有力的；强劲的 | vigorous exercise 剧烈运动<br>vigorous debate 激烈的辩论<br>vigorous campaign 强有力的运动<br>vigor n. 精力，活力 | She is a **vigorous** supporter of education reform. （她是教育改革的积极支持者。）<br>Regular **vigorous** exercise helps keep the heart healthy. （经常进行剧烈运动有助于保持心脏健康。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：energetic（精力充沛的）/ robust（强健的）· **反义**：lethargic（昏昏欲睡的）· **易混**：vigorous（精力充沛）vs rigorous（严格的，差一个字母！）|
| | dispute | /dɪˈspjuːt/ | n. 争端，争论<br>v. 争论；质疑 | in dispute 有争议的<br>territory dispute 领土争端<br>dispute over/about 就…争论<br>beyond dispute 无可争议 | The two countries are in **dispute** over fishing rights. （两国就渔业权存在争端。）<br>No one will **dispute** his contribution to the project. （没人会质疑他对项目的贡献。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：controversy（争议）/ debate（辩论）/ conflict（冲突）· **易混**：dispute（争端）vs dispute ≠ dispute（≠ repute 名声）vs depute（委派）|
| | qualification | /ˌkwɑːlɪfɪˈkeɪʃn/ | n. 资格，学历；<br>限定条件 | professional qualification 职业资格<br>academic qualification 学历<br>without qualification 毫无保留地<br>qualify v. 使有资格 | She has the right **qualifications** for the job. （她具备这份工作的合适资历。）<br>He accepted the offer without **qualification**. （他毫无保留地接受了提议。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：credential（资质证明）/ requirement（要求）· **易混**：qualification（资格）vs quality（质量）vs quantify（量化）|
| | knob | /nɑːb/ | n. 旋钮；球形把手；<br>一团（黄油等） | door knob 门把手<br>volume knob 音量旋钮<br>turn the knob 转动旋钮<br>a knob of butter 一小块黄油 | Turn the **knob** to adjust the volume. （转动旋钮来调节音量。）<br>He grabbed the door **knob** and opened the door. （他抓住门把手打开门。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：handle（把手）/ dial（刻度盘）· **易混**：knob（旋钮）vs knock（敲）vs knot（结）|
| | siege | /siːdʒ/ | n. 围攻，围困；<br>包围 | under siege 被围困<br>lay siege to 围攻，包围<br>lift the siege 解除包围<br>state of siege 紧急状态 | The city has been **under siege** for three months. （这座城市已经被围困三个月了。）<br>Protesters **laid siege to** the parliament building. （抗议者包围了议会大楼。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：blockade（封锁）/ encirclement（包围）· **易混**：siege（围攻）vs seize（抓住）vs sieze（拼写错误，正确为 seize）|
| | warfare | /ˈwɔːrfer/ | n. 战争，作战；<br>冲突，斗争 | modern warfare 现代战争<br>guerrilla warfare 游击战<br>psychological warfare 心理战<br>economic warfare 经济战 | The country has been torn apart by years of **warfare**. （该国被多年的战争摧毁。）<br>Companies are engaged in fierce **economic warfare**. （公司们正在进行激烈的经济战。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：war（战争）/ conflict（冲突）· **易混**：warfare（战争）vs welfare（福利，差一个字母！）vs farewell（告别）|
| | ascertain (第2次) | /ˌæsərˈteɪn/ | v. 查明，确定，<br>弄清 | ascertain the facts 查明事实<br>ascertain the cause 确定原因<br>ascertain whether 确定是否<br>it is difficult to ascertain 难以确定 | The police are trying to **ascertain** the cause of the fire. （警方正在努力查明火灾原因。）<br>We need to **ascertain** whether the data is accurate. （我们需要确定数据是否准确。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：determine（确定）/ verify（核实）· **易混**：ascertain（查明）vs certain（确定的）vs assert（断言）|
| | radius (第3次) | /ˈreɪdiəs/ | n. 半径；范围 | within a radius of 在…半径范围内<br>blast radius 爆炸半径 | The explosion destroyed everything within a 5-mile **radius**. （爆炸摧毁了半径5英里内的一切。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：diameter（直径）/ range（范围）· **易混**：radius（半径）vs radium（镭）vs ratio（比率）|
| | adjacent (第2次) | /əˈdʒeɪsnt/ | adj. 邻近的，毗连的 | adjacent to 与…相邻<br>adjacent rooms 相邻房间<br>adjacent angles 邻角 | The park is **adjacent** to the library. （公园紧邻图书馆。）<br>They stayed in **adjacent** rooms. （他们住在相邻房间里。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：neighboring（邻近）/ adjoining（毗连）· **反义**：distant/remote · **易混**：adjacent（靠近但未必连接）vs adjoining（连在一起）|
| | statesman | /ˈsteɪtsmən/ | n. 政治家，<br>国务活动家 | elder statesman 政界元老<br>distinguished statesman 杰出的政治家<br>statesmanlike adj. 有政治家风范的 | He was regarded as a great **statesman** of his time. （他被认为是他那个时代的伟大政治家。）<br>A true **statesman** puts the nation's interests first. （真正的政治家把国家利益放在首位。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：politician（政客，常贬义）/ diplomat（外交官）· **易混**：statesman（政治家，褒义）vs politician（政客，可贬义）· statesman = states + man = 治理国家的人|
| | rake | /reɪk/ | n. 耙子<br>v. 耙平；搜索；<br>扫射 | rake up 翻出（旧事）；耙拢<br>rake in 大量敛集（钱财）<br>garden rake 园艺耙<br>rake through 翻找 | He used a **rake** to gather the fallen leaves. （他用耙子把落叶拢在一起。）<br>The company is **raking in** huge profits. （公司正大赚特赚。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：scrape（刮）/ comb（梳理，搜索）· **易混**：rake（耙子）vs brake（刹车）vs rake ≠ rake（≠ take）|
| | debut (第2次) | /deɪˈbjuː/ | n./v. 首次亮相，<br>初次登台 | make one's debut 首次亮相<br>debut album 首张专辑<br>debut performance 首演 | The band made their **debut** last year. （这支乐队去年首次亮相。）<br>The new product will **debut** next month. （新产品将于下月首次推出。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：premiere（首映/首演）· **易混**：debut（首次亮相）vs debt（债务，发音不同）|
| | deduct | /dɪˈdʌkt/ | v. 扣除，减去 | deduct from 从…中扣除<br>deduct tax 扣除税款<br>deduct expenses 扣除费用<br>deduction n. 扣除；推论 | The company will **deduct** the cost from your salary. （公司将从你的工资中扣除这笔费用。）<br>Tax is **deducted** directly from your account. （税款直接从你的账户扣除。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：subtract（减去）/ withhold（扣留）· **反义**：add（加）· **易混**：deduct（扣除）vs deduce（推论）vs deduct ≠ deduct（≠ seduce 引诱）|
| | fraud (复现) | /frɔːd/ | n. 欺诈，诈骗；<br>骗子 | commit fraud 进行诈骗<br>credit card fraud 信用卡诈骗<br>electoral fraud 选举舞弊 | He was convicted of **fraud**. （他被判诈骗罪成立。）<br>The investment scheme turned out to be a **fraud**. （这个投资计划原来是个骗局。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：deception（欺骗）/ scam（骗局）· **易混**：fraud（欺诈）vs fraud ≠ fraud（≠ Freud 弗洛伊德）vs frayed（磨损的）|
| | beware (第2次) | /bɪˈwer/ | v. 当心，小心，提防 | beware of 当心…<br>beware of pickpockets 小心扒手<br>buyer beware 买者自负 | **Beware** of pickpockets in crowded areas. （在拥挤区域小心扒手。）<br>**Beware** of promises that sound too good to be true. （小心好得离谱的承诺。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：watch out（小心）· **易混**：beware（小心）vs aware（意识到的）· ⚠️ beware 只用原形，不用进行时！|
| | procession | /prəˈseʃn/ | n. 队伍，行列；<br>游行 | funeral procession 送葬队伍<br>march in procession 列队行进<br>religious procession 宗教游行 | A long **procession** of cars followed the hearse. （一长列车队跟在灵车后面。）<br>The graduates marched in **procession** to receive their diplomas. （毕业生列队行进领取毕业证书。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：parade（游行）/ march（行军）· **易混**：procession（行列）vs process（过程）vs possession（拥有）|
| | affection | /əˈfekʃn/ | n. 喜爱，钟爱；<br>感情 | show affection 表示喜爱<br>deep affection 深深的爱<br>affection for 对…的喜爱<br>affectionate adj. 深情的 | She has a deep **affection** for her grandchildren. （她对孙辈们有着深深的喜爱。）<br>He finds it hard to show **affection** in public. （他很难在公开场合表达爱意。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：love（爱）/ fondness（喜爱）· **易混**：affection（喜爱/感情）vs affectation（做作，假装）vs affect（影响/假装）|
| | dialect | /ˈdaɪəlekt/ | n. 方言，地方话 | local dialect 本地方言<br>regional dialect 地区方言<br>speak in dialect 讲方言<br>dialectal adj. 方言的 | She speaks a strong local **dialect**. （她讲一口浓重的本地方言。）<br>The novel is written partly in **dialect**. （这部小说部分用方言写成。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：vernacular（本地话）/ slang（俚语）· **易混**：dialect（方言）vs dialogue（对话）vs dial（拨号）|
| | courtesy (第2次) | /ˈkɜːrtəsi/ | n. 礼貌，谦恭<br>adj. 免费的 | common courtesy 基本礼貌<br>courtesy visit 礼节性拜访<br>courtesy bus 免费班车 | She replied out of **courtesy**. （她出于礼貌回信了。）<br>The hotel offers a **courtesy** shuttle. （酒店提供免费班车。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：politeness（礼貌）· **反义**：rudeness（粗鲁）· **易混**：courtesy（礼貌/免费）vs court（法庭）vs curtsy（屈膝礼）|
| | stake | /steɪk/ | n. 桩；赌注；利害关系<br>v. 以…打赌；用桩支撑 | at stake 处于危险中<br>have a stake in 与…有利害关系<br>high stakes 高风险<br>stake a claim 提出所有权要求 | Thousands of lives are **at stake**. （成千上万的生命岌岌可危。）<br>He has a personal **stake** in the company's success. （他与公司的成功有个人利害关系。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bet（赌注）/ risk（风险）/ post（桩）· **易混**：stake（利害关系）vs steak（牛排，同音！）vs stale（不新鲜的）|
| | radiant (第2次) | /ˈreɪdiənt/ | adj. 光芒四射的，<br>容光焕发的 | radiant smile 灿烂笑容<br>radiant beauty 光彩照人的美<br>radiate v. 辐射 | She looked **radiant** on her wedding day. （她在婚礼那天容光焕发。）<br>The sun gives off **radiant** heat. （太阳散发辐射热。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：glowing（发光的）/ beaming（喜气洋洋的）· **易混**：radiant（光芒四射）vs radical（激进的）vs radius（半径）|
| | famine (复现) | /ˈfæmɪn/ | n. 饥荒 | severe famine 严重饥荒<br>famine relief 饥荒救济<br>faces famine 面临饥荒 | Millions of people are suffering from **famine**. （数百万人正遭受饥荒。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：starvation（饥饿）· **易混**：famine（饥荒）vs feminine（女性的）vs famous（著名的）|
| | thermometer (第2次) | /θərˈmɑːmɪtər/ | n. 温度计，体温计 | clinical thermometer 体温计<br>digital thermometer 电子温度计<br>read a thermometer 读温度计 | The nurse put a **thermometer** under his arm. （护士把温度计放在他腋下。）<br>The **thermometer** showed 38°C outside. （温度计显示外面38°C。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：thermometer（温度计）vs thermal（热量的）vs therapy（治疗）· 词根：thermo-热 + meter测量器|
| | fare | /fer/ | n. 车费，票价；<br>饮食<br>v. 进展，表现 | bus/taxi fare 公交车/出租车费<br>air fare 机票费<br>simple fare 简单的饭菜<br>fare well/badly 表现好/差 | The bus **fare** has gone up again. （公交车费又涨了。）<br>How did you **fare** in the exam? （你考试考得怎么样？） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：fee（费用）/ ticket price（票价）· **易混**：fare（车费/饮食）vs fair（公平，同音！）vs fare ≠ fare（≠ fire 火）|
| | temporal | /ˈtempərəl/ | adj. 时间的；<br>世俗的，现世的；<br>暂时的 | temporal order 时间顺序<br>temporal power 世俗权力<br>temporal lobe 颞叶<br>temporary adj. 暂时的 | The church had both spiritual and **temporal** authority. （教会同时拥有精神权威和世俗权力。）<br>We live in a **temporal** world. （我们生活在一个现世的世界里。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：secular（世俗的）/ temporary（暂时的）· **反义**：eternal（永恒的）/ spiritual（精神的）· **易混**：temporal（时间的/世俗的）vs temporary（暂时的）vs tempo（节奏）|
| | irrespective | /ˌɪrɪˈspektɪv/ | adj. 不顾的，<br>不考虑的 | irrespective of 不论，不顾<br>irrespective of age 不论年龄<br>irrespective of the consequences 不顾后果 | The law applies to everyone **irrespective** of their background. （法律适用于所有人，不论其背景如何。）<br>All students are welcome, **irrespective** of their test scores. （欢迎所有学生，不论考试分数高低。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：regardless of（不论）/ without regard to（不考虑）· **易混**：irrespective（不论）vs regardless（不论，同义但regardless更常用）vs respective（各自的）|
| | limp (第2次) | /lɪmp/ | v. 跛行<br>adj. 无力的，软的 | walk with a limp 跛行<br>limp handshake 无力握手<br>go limp 变软 | He **limped** off the field after the injury. （受伤后他跛着走下场。）<br>The flowers looked **limp** without water. （没水花蔫了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stagger（蹒跚）/ floppy（松软的）· **易混**：limp（跛行）vs limb（肢体）vs lump（肿块）|
| | swell | /swel/ | v. 肿胀，膨胀；<br>增大，增强<br>n. 波浪起伏；增加 | swell up 肿起来<br>swell with pride 充满自豪<br>a swell of 一阵（情绪/声音）<br>swollen adj. 肿胀的 | His ankle **swelled** after the fall. （摔了之后他的脚踝肿了起来。）<br>The crowd **swelled** to over 10,000. （人群增加到一万多人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：expand（膨胀）/ inflate（充气）· **反义**：shrink（收缩）· **易混**：swell（肿胀/增大）vs well（好/井）vs dwell（居住/纠结）|
| | feeble (第2次) | /ˈfiːbl/ | adj. 虚弱的，无力的；<br>站不住脚的 | feeble attempt 无力尝试<br>feeble excuse 站不住脚的借口<br>feeble voice 微弱的声音 | She made a **feeble** attempt to defend herself. （她做了无力的自我辩护。）<br>His argument was **feeble** and unconvincing. （他的论点站不住脚。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：weak（弱的）/ frail（脆弱的）· **反义**：strong（强壮的）· **易混**：feeble（虚弱）vs fiber（纤维）|
| | profile | /ˈproʊfaɪl/ | n. 侧面；轮廓；<br>简介；形象<br>v. 概述，写简介 | high profile 高调，引人注目<br>low profile 低调<br>company profile 公司简介<br>keep a low profile 保持低调 | The magazine published a **profile** of the new CEO. （杂志刊登了新CEO的人物简介。）<br>He keeps a low **profile** on social media. （他在社交媒体上保持低调。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：portrait（肖像）/ outline（轮廓）/ summary（概要）· **易混**：profile（侧面/简介）vs profit（利润）vs prophet（先知）|
| | undermine | /ˌʌndərˈmaɪn/ | v. 削弱，逐渐损害；<br>从根基处破坏 | undermine authority 削弱权威<br>undermine confidence 损害信心<br>undermine the foundation 破坏地基 | Constant criticism can **undermine** a child's confidence. （不断的批评会损害孩子的信心。）<br>Rumors are **undermining** the government's authority. （谣言正在削弱政府的权威。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：weaken（削弱）/ erode（侵蚀）/ sabotage（破坏）· **反义**：strengthen（加强）/ bolster（支持）· **易混**：undermine（削弱/破坏）vs underline（强调/下划线）vs underneath（在下面）|
| | snap | /snæp/ | v. 突然折断；<br>厉声说；拍照<br>n. 咔嚓声；快照 | snap at sb 厉声对某人说<br>snap a photo 拍张照片<br>snap shut 啪地关上<br>in a snap 立刻，马上 | The branch **snapped** in the wind. （树枝在风中折断了。）<br>She **snapped** at the waiter for being slow. （她对慢吞吞的服务员厉声斥责。）<br>He **snapped** a picture of the sunset. （他拍了张日落的快照。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：break（折断）/ bark（厉声说）/ photograph（拍照）· **易混**：snap（折断/拍照）vs snap ≠ snap（≠ snip 剪）vs snappy（时髦的）|
| | defy | /dɪˈfaɪ/ | v. 违抗，反抗；<br>使不可能 | defy the law 违抗法律<br>defy authority 挑战权威<br>defy explanation 无法解释<br>defy logic 不合逻辑 | She **defied** her parents and went to the party. （她违抗父母去参加了派对。）<br>The beauty of the scene **defies** description. （这美景难以用语言形容。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：disobey（违抗）/ challenge（挑战）· **反义**：obey（服从）· **易混**：defy（违抗）vs deny（否认）vs defy ≠ defy（≠ defer 推迟）|
| | pine | /paɪn/ | n. 松树<br>v. 渴望；憔悴 | pine tree 松树<br>pine for 渴望，思念<br>pine away 日渐憔悴<br>pine cone 松果 | After his dog died, he just **pined** away. （他的狗死后，他就日渐憔悴了。）<br>She was **pining** for her hometown. （她思念着她的家乡。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：long for（渴望）/ yearn for（向往）· **易混**：pine（松树/渴望）vs pin（别针）vs spine（脊柱）|
| | eradicate | /ɪˈrædɪkeɪt/ | v. 根除，消灭 | eradicate poverty 消除贫困<br>eradicate disease 消灭疾病<br>eradicate corruption 根除腐败<br>eradication n. 根除 | The government aims to **eradicate** poverty within a decade. （政府计划在十年内消除贫困。）<br>Smallpox has been completely **eradicated**. （天花已经被彻底消灭。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：eliminate（消除）/ wipe out（消灭）· **反义**：establish（建立）/ introduce（引入）· **易混**：eradicate（根除）vs radicate（生根）vs eradicate ≠ eradicate（≠ eradicate vs eradiate 辐射）|
| | cashier | /kæˈʃɪr/ | n. 收银员 | cashier desk 收银台<br>cashier check 银行本票<br>work as a cashier 做收银员 | The **cashier** scanned all the items and bagged them. （收银员扫码了所有商品并装好袋。）<br>She works part-time as a **cashier** at the supermarket. （她在超市兼职做收银员。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：teller（银行出纳员）/ clerk（店员）· **易混**：cashier（收银员）vs cash（现金）vs casket（棺材）· cash + ier = 管钱的人|
| | landlord | /ˈlændlɔːrd/ | n. 房东；地主；<br>（旅馆）老板 | landlord and tenant 房东与租客<br>landlord's permission 房东许可<br>absentee landlord 不住在当地的房东 | The **landlord** raised the rent again. （房东又涨房租了。）<br>I have to call the **landlord** to fix the leaking pipe. （我得打电话给房东修漏水管。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：property owner（房主）· **反义**：tenant（租客）· **易混**：landlord（房东）vs landlord ≠ landlord（≠ landlord ≠ landowner 地主）· land + lord = 土地之主|
| | timber | /ˈtɪmbər/ | n. 木材，木料；<br>林木 | timber industry 木材工业<br>timber frame 木结构<br>timber merchant 木材商<br>timber! 树要倒了！（呼声） | The house is built with **timber** from the local forest. （这房子用的是当地森林的木材。）<br>Deforestation threatens the **timber** supply. （森林砍伐威胁着木材供应。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：lumber（木材）/ wood（木头）· **易混**：timber（木材）vs timbre（音色，同音！）vs timber ≠ timber（≠ lumber）|
| | bump | /bʌmp/ | v. 碰，撞<br>n. 肿块；碰撞；<br>隆起 | bump into 碰见；撞上<br>bump against 撞到<br>speed bump 减速带<br>goose bumps 鸡皮疙瘩 | I **bumped into** an old friend at the supermarket. （我在超市偶遇了一位老朋友。）<br>She got a **bump** on her head from the fall. （她摔了一跤，头上撞了个包。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：collide（碰撞）/ knock（敲击）/ lump（肿块）· **易混**：bump（碰撞/肿块）vs dump（倾倒）vs pump（泵）|
| | carpet | /ˈkɑːrpɪt/ | n. 地毯<br>v. 铺地毯；训斥 | red carpet 红地毯<br>carpet bombing 地毯式轰炸<br>on the carpet 被训斥<br>wall-to-wall carpet 满铺地毯 | They rolled out the red **carpet** for the visiting president. （他们为来访总统铺了红地毯。）<br>The boss had him **on the carpet** for being late. （老板因迟到训斥了他。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：rug（小地毯）· **易混**：carpet（地毯，整铺）vs rug（小地毯，可移动）vs carpet ≠ carpet（≠ carper 吹毛求疵者）|
| | turnover | /ˈtɜːrnoʊvər/ | n. 营业额；人员流动率；<br>翻面（食物） | high turnover 高流动率/高营业额<br>staff turnover 员工流失率<br>annual turnover 年营业额<br>apple turnover 苹果酥 | The company reported a **turnover** of $10 million. （公司报告了1000万美元的营业额。）<br>High staff **turnover** is a sign of low morale. （员工高流失率是士气低落的信号。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：revenue（收入）/ rotation（轮换）· **易混**：turnover（营业额/流动率）vs turn over（翻转，动词短语）vs turnover ≠ turnover（≠ takeover 接管）|
| | plunge | /plʌndʒ/ | v. 猛跌，骤降；<br>跳水；投入<br>n. 暴跌；跳入 | plunge into 投入；陷入<br>take the plunge 决定冒险一试<br>plunge in prices 价格暴跌<br>plunge sb into debt 使某人陷入债务 | Stock prices **plunged** after the bad news. （坏消息后股价暴跌。）<br>She decided to **take the plunge** and start her own business. （她决定冒险一试，自己创业。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：dive（跳水）/ plummet（暴跌）· **反义**：soar（飙升）· **易混**：plunge（暴跌/投入）vs plunder（掠夺）vs plume（羽毛）|
| | curve | /kɜːrv/ | n. 曲线，弧线；<br>弯道<br>v. 弯曲，呈曲线状 | learning curve 学习曲线<br>ahead of the curve 领先潮流<br>throw a curve 出其不意<br>curvature n. 曲率 | It takes time to climb the **learning curve**. （爬学习曲线需要时间。）<br>The road **curves** to the right up ahead. （前面的路向右弯。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bend（弯曲）/ arc（弧形）· **易混**：curve（曲线）vs curb（抑制/路边）vs carve（雕刻）|
| | grope | /ɡroʊp/ | v. 摸索，探索；<br>（猥亵地）抚摸 | grope for 摸索着找<br>grope in the dark 在黑暗中摸索<br>grope one's way 摸索着前进<br>grope blindly 盲目摸索 | He **groped** for the light switch in the dark. （他在黑暗中摸索着找电灯开关。）<br>The country is **groping** for a way out of the crisis. （该国正在摸索走出危机的方法。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：fumble（笨拙摸索）/ search（搜索）· **易混**：grope（摸索）vs group（组）vs rope（绳子）|
| | sponge | /spʌndʒ/ | n. 海绵<br>v. 用海绵擦拭；<br>白吃白拿 | sponge cake 海绵蛋糕<br>sponge off sb 靠某人养活、蹭吃蹭喝<br>sponge the floor 用海绵擦地板<br>spongy adj. 海绵状的 | He wiped the table with a damp **sponge**. （他用湿海绵擦了桌子。）<br>He's been **sponging** off his parents for years. （他多年来一直蹭父母吃住。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：absorb（吸收）/ freeload（白吃白喝）· **易混**：sponge（海绵/蹭吃）vs sponge ≠ sponge（≠ sponge vs spawn 产卵）vs spondee（诗歌术语）|
| | carriage | /ˈkærɪdʒ/ | n. 马车；车厢；<br>运输；仪态 | horse-drawn carriage 马车<br>railway carriage 火车车厢<br>carriage of goods 货物运输<br>dignified carriage 端庄的仪态 | They took a romantic ride in a horse-drawn **carriage**. （他们坐浪漫的马车兜风。）<br>The first-class **carriage** is at the front of the train. （一等车厢在火车的前部。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：wagon（马车/货车）/ coach（长途客车/车厢）· **易混**：carriage（马车/车厢）vs marriage（婚姻，拼写极像！）vs carriage ≠ carriage（≠ carriage vs cart 手推车）|
| | excerpt | /ˈeksɜːrpt/ | n. 摘录，节选<br>v. 摘录，引用 | excerpt from 从…摘录<br>an excerpt of 一段…的节选<br>read an excerpt 读一段摘录 | The teacher read an **excerpt** from the novel. （老师读了小说的一段节选。）<br>This is an **excerpt** from her new book. （这是她新书的一段摘录。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：extract（摘录）/ passage（段落）· **易混**：excerpt（摘录）vs excerpt ≠ excerpt（≠ excerpt vs expert 专家）vs except（除了）|
| | sarcastic | /sɑːrˈkæstɪk/ | adj. 讽刺的，<br>挖苦的 | sarcastic remark 讽刺的话<br>sarcastic tone 讽刺的语气<br>sarcastic comment 挖苦的评论<br>sarcasm n. 讽刺 | She made a **sarcastic** comment about his new haircut. （她对他的新发型挖苦了一番。）<br>Don't get **sarcastic** with me. （别对我阴阳怪气的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ironic（反讽的）/ cynical（愤世嫉俗的）· **反义**：sincere（真诚的）· **易混**：sarcastic（挖苦的）vs sardonic（嘲笑的）vs sarcastic ≠ sarcastic（≠ sarcastic vs drastic 激烈的）|
| | paste | /peɪst/ | n. 糊状物；浆糊<br>v. 粘贴；裱糊 | copy and paste 复制粘贴<br>toothpaste 牙膏<br>paste up 张贴<br>wallpaper paste 壁纸浆糊 | Just **copy and paste** the text into the document. （把文字复制粘贴到文档里就行。）<br>She **pasted** the poster on the wall. （她把海报贴在墙上。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：glue（胶水）/ stick（粘贴）· **易混**：paste（粘贴/浆糊）vs paste ≠ paste（≠ paste vs past 过去）vs taste（品尝）|

| | conscientious | /ˌkɑːnʃiˈenʃəs/ | adj. 认真的，<br>尽责的，勤勤恳恳的 | conscientious student 认真的学生<br>conscientious worker 尽责的工人<br>conscientious objection 良心反对<br>conscientiously adv. 认真地 | She is a **conscientious** student who always does her homework. （她是个认真的学生，总是按时做作业。）<br>He was **conscientious** about details in his work. （他对工作中的细节一丝不苟。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：diligent（勤奋的）/ meticulous（一丝不苟的）· **反义**：careless（粗心的）· **易混**：conscientious（尽责的）vs conscious（有意识的）vs conscience（良心）|
| | fore | /fɔːr/ | adj./adv. 在前部的；<br>在船头<br>n. 前部 | come to the fore 崭露头角<br>fore and aft 从船头到船尾<br>bring to the fore 使…凸显<br>foreground n. 前景 | The issue has come to the **fore** in recent debates. （这个问题在最近的辩论中凸显了出来。）<br>New leaders emerged to the **fore** during the crisis. （危机期间新领导人崭露头角。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：front（前面）/ forward（向前）· **反义**：aft（船尾）/ back（后面）· **易混**：fore（前部）vs for（为了，同音！）vs four（四，同音！）|

| | lane (第2次) | /leɪn/ | n. 车道；小路，小巷 | bus lane 公交车道<br>country lane 乡间小路<br>fast lane 快车道 | Stay in the right **lane**. （保持在右车道。）<br>They walked down a narrow country **lane**. （他们走在狭窄的乡间小路上。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：path（小路）/ alley（巷子）· **易混**：lane（车道）vs line（线/线路）vs lame（瘸的）|
| | fore (第2次) | /fɔːr/ | adj./adv./n. 前部 | come to the fore 崭露头角<br>fore and aft 船头到船尾 | New leaders came to the **fore** during the crisis. （危机期间新领导人崭露头角。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：front（前面）· **反义**：aft/back · **易混**：fore vs for（为了，同音！）vs four（四，同音！）|

| | indignant (第2次) | /ɪnˈdɪɡnənt/ | adj. 愤愤不平的 | be indignant at 对…愤慨<br>indignation n. 愤慨 | She was **indignant** at being treated unfairly. （她因不公对待而愤慨。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：angry/outraged · **易混**：indignant vs indigenous（本土的）vs indignity（侮辱）|
| | snap (第2次) | /snæp/ | v. 折断；厉声说；拍照<br>n. 咔嚓声 | snap at sb 厉声斥责<br>snap a photo 拍照<br>in a snap 立刻 | She **snapped** at the waiter. （她对服务员厉声斥责。）<br>He **snapped** a picture of the sunset. （他拍了张日落快照。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：break/bark/photograph · **易混**：snap vs snip（剪）vs snappy（时髦）|
| | monarch | /ˈmɑːnərk/ | n. 君主，国王；<br>帝王 | absolute monarch 专制君主<br>British monarch 英国君主<br>monarchy n. 君主制<br>monarch butterfly 帝王蝶 | The **monarch** ruled the country for over 50 years. （这位君主统治国家超过50年。）<br>The **monarch** butterfly is known for its long migration. （帝王蝶以长距离迁徙而闻名。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：king（国王）/ sovereign（元首）· **反义**：subject（臣民）· **易混**：monarch（君主）vs monarchy（君主制）vs monastery（修道院）|
| | vigorous (复现) | /ˈvɪɡərəs/ | adj. 精力充沛的；<br>强有力的 | vigorous exercise 剧烈运动<br>vigor n. 精力 | She is a **vigorous** supporter of reform. （她是改革的积极支持者。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：energetic/robust · **易混**：vigorous vs rigorous（严格的，一字之差！）|
| | utter | /ˈʌtər/ | v. 说，发出（声音）<br>adj. 完全的，彻底的 | utter a word 说一句话<br>utter nonsense 纯粹胡说<br>utter silence 完全的寂静<br>utterly adv. 完全地 | She didn't **utter** a single word during the meeting. （她在会上一个字都没说。）<br>It was an **utter** disaster. （那是一场彻底的灾难。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：say（说）/ complete（完全）· **易混**：utter（说/完全）vs butter（黄油）vs mutter（嘀咕）|
| | saturate | /ˈsætʃəreɪt/ | v. 使饱和，浸透；<br>使充满 | saturate with 用…浸透<br>saturated fat 饱和脂肪<br>saturate the market 使市场饱和<br>saturation n. 饱和 | The rain **saturated** the soil. （雨水浸透了土壤。）<br>The market is already **saturated** with similar products. （市场已经被类似产品饱和了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：soak（浸泡）/ flood（充斥）· **反义**：dry（使干燥）· **易混**：saturate（饱和）vs saturate ≠ saturate（≠ saturate vs Saturday 星期六）|
| | gauge | /ɡeɪdʒ/ | n. 测量仪表；标准<br>v. 测量；估计 | fuel gauge 燃油表<br>pressure gauge 压力表<br>rain gauge 雨量计<br>gauge the situation 估计形势 | The fuel **gauge** showed the tank was almost empty. （油表显示油箱几乎空了。）<br>It's hard to **gauge** his reaction from his expression. （很难从他的表情判断他的反应。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：measure（测量）/ assess（评估）/ meter（仪表）· **易混**：gauge（测量）vs gorge（峡谷）vs gouge（凿）|
