| 2026-06-18 | militant | /ˈmɪlɪtənt/ | adj. 好战的，激进的，<br>战斗性强的<br>n. 好斗者，激进分子 | militant group 激进组织<br>militant protest 激烈抗议<br>militant union 激进的工会<br>militancy n. 好战，激进 | The **militant** group claimed responsibility for the attack. （该激进组织声称对袭击负责。）<br>She became more **militant** in her demands for equal rights. （她在争取平等权利方面变得更加激进。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：aggressive（好斗的）/ combative（好战的）/ radical（激进的）· **反义**：peaceful（和平的）/ moderate（温和的）· **易混**：militant（好战的/激进分子）vs military（军队的/军事的）vs militia（民兵组织）|
| | parasite | /ˈpærəsaɪt/ | n. 寄生虫；<br>靠他人为生者，寄生虫般的人 | intestinal parasite 肠道寄生虫<br>social parasite 社会寄生虫<br>parasitic adj. 寄生的<br>parasitism n. 寄生现象 | Fleas are **parasites** that live on the blood of animals. （跳蚤是靠动物血液为生的寄生虫。）<br>He is a lazy **parasite** who lives off his parents. （他是个靠父母养活的懒寄生虫。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：leech（水蛭；吸血鬼）/ sponger（蹭吃蹭喝的人）· **反义**：host（宿主）/ benefactor（恩人）· **易混**：parasite（寄生虫）vs paradise（天堂）vs paralyze（使瘫痪）|
| | crude (第2次) | /kruːd/ | adj. 粗糙的；原油的 | crude oil 原油<br>crude language 粗鲁语言 | The pipeline transports **crude** oil. （管道输送原油。）<br>His **crude** remarks offended many. （他粗鲁的言论冒犯了很多人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：raw/vulgar/rough · **反义**：refined · **易混**：crude vs rude（粗鲁）vs cruel（残忍）|
| | wagon (第2次) | /ˈwæɡən/ | n. 马车，货车 | on the wagon 戒酒<br>fall off the wagon 重新喝酒<br>station wagon 旅行车 | He's been **on the wagon** for three months. （他已经戒酒三个月了。）<br>They loaded the **wagon** with hay. （他们把干草装上马车。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cart/carriage · **易混**：wagon vs dragon（龙）|
| | auditorium | /ˌɔːdɪˈtɔːriəm/ | n. 礼堂；观众席；<br>音乐厅 | school auditorium 学校礼堂<br>packed auditorium 座无虚席的礼堂<br>auditorium seating 观众席座位<br>audience n. 观众 | The concert was held in the school **auditorium**. （音乐会在学校礼堂举行。）<br>The **auditorium** can seat 2,000 people. （这个礼堂可容纳2000人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hall（大厅）/ theater（剧院）· **易混**：auditorium（礼堂）vs audience（观众）vs audio（音频）· 词根：aud- 听 → 听东西的地方|
| | booth | /buːθ/ | n. 小隔间；摊位；<br>电话亭 | phone booth 电话亭<br>ticket booth 售票亭<br>voting booth 投票间<br>photo booth 自助照相亭 | I called her from a phone **booth**. （我从电话亭给她打了电话。）<br>We bought tickets at the **booth** near the entrance. （我们在入口附近的售票亭买了票。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stall（摊位）/ kiosk（小亭子）· **易混**：booth（小隔间）vs boot（靴子）vs booth ≠ booth（≠ both 两者）|
| | propaganda | /ˌprɑːpəˈɡændə/ | n. 宣传（常含贬义）；<br>鼓吹 | political propaganda 政治宣传<br>government propaganda 政府宣传<br>spread propaganda 散布宣传<br>propagandist n. 宣传者 | The government used **propaganda** to win public support. （政府用宣传来赢得公众支持。）<br>Don't believe everything you read — it's just **propaganda**. （别全信你读到的——那只是宣传而已。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：publicity（宣传，中性）/ brainwashing（洗脑）· **易混**：propaganda（宣传）vs propagate（传播/繁殖）· 含贬义，英文中常指带有偏见的宣传|
| | bulletin | /ˈbʊlətɪn/ | n. 公告，公报；<br>新闻简报 | news bulletin 新闻简报<br>bulletin board 公告板<br>official bulletin 官方公报<br>weather bulletin 天气公告 | The hospital issues a daily **bulletin** on the patient's condition. （医院每天发布患者病情公告。）<br>I read the notice on the **bulletin** board. （我在公告板上看到了通知。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：announcement（公告）/ newsletter（简报）· **易混**：bulletin（公告）vs bullet（子弹，拼写极像！）vs bulletin ≠ bulletin（≠ bull 公牛）|
| | doctrine | /ˈdɑːktrɪn/ | n. 教义，学说；<br>主义，信条 | religious doctrine 宗教教义<br>military doctrine 军事学说<br>legal doctrine 法律原则<br>doctrinal adj. 教义的 | The church teaches its **doctrine** to all followers. （教会向所有信徒传授其教义。）<br>This **doctrine** has influenced policy for decades. （这一学说影响政策数十年。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：principle（原则）/ dogma（教条）· **易混**：doctrine（学说）vs doctor（医生/博士，同词根 doc- 教导）vs document（文件）|
| | barn | /bɑːrn/ | n. 谷仓，粮仓 | old barn 旧谷仓<br>barn door 谷仓门<br>like a barn door 像谷仓门一样大 | The farmer stored hay in the **barn**. （农民把干草储存在谷仓里。）<br>The old red **barn** is a landmark of this farm. （那座红色旧谷仓是这个农场的标志。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：granary（粮仓）/ warehouse（仓库）· **易混**：barn（谷仓）vs burn（燃烧）vs born（出生）|
| | jury | /ˈdʒʊri/ | n. 陪审团；<br>评判委员会 | serve on a jury 担任陪审员<br>jury verdict 陪审团裁决<br>trial by jury 陪审团审判<br>jury duty 陪审义务 | The **jury** found him guilty of the crime. （陪审团判他罪名成立。）<br>She was called for **jury** duty last month. （她上个月被召去履行陪审义务。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：panel（专家组）· **易混**：jury（陪审团）vs judge（法官）vs justice（公正/大法官）· 12个普通人组成的裁决小组|
| | toil | /tɔɪl/ | v. 辛苦工作，苦干<br>n. 苦工，辛苦 | toil away 埋头苦干<br>toil for 为…辛劳<br>years of toil 多年的辛苦<br>toilsome adj. 辛苦的 | They **toiled** in the fields from dawn to dusk. （他们从早到晚在田间辛苦劳作。）<br>After years of **toil**, he finally achieved his dream. （经过多年辛劳，他终于实现了梦想。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：labor（劳动）/ drudgery（苦差事）· **反义**：leisure（休闲）· **易混**：toil（苦干）vs soil（土壤）vs oil（油）|
| | incur | /ɪnˈkɜːr/ | v. 招致，引起（坏的结果）；<br>承担（费用等） | incur costs 产生费用<br>incur debt 负债<br>incur the risk of 招致…风险<br>incur sb's anger 惹怒某人 | The company **incurred** heavy losses last year. （公司去年遭受了重大损失。）<br>His reckless behavior **incurred** the manager's anger. （他鲁莽的行为惹怒了经理。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bring about（引起）/ suffer（遭受）· **易混**：incur（招致，负面）vs occur（发生）vs incur ≠ incur（≠ incur vs recur 复发）|
| | inhibit | /ɪnˈhɪbɪt/ | v. 抑制，阻止；<br>使拘束 | inhibit sb from doing 阻止某人做某事<br>inhibit growth 抑制生长<br>inhibited adj. 拘束的<br>inhibition n. 抑制，禁忌 | The drug **inhibits** the growth of cancer cells. （这种药抑制癌细胞的生长。）<br>Shyness can **inhibit** you from speaking up. （害羞可能会抑制你发言。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：restrain（约束）/ suppress（压制）· **反义**：encourage（鼓励）/ stimulate（刺激）· **易混**：inhibit（抑制）vs inhabit（居住）vs prohibit（禁止）|
| | hypocrisy | /hɪˈpɑːkrəsi/ | n. 虚伪，伪善 | pure hypocrisy 纯粹虚伪<br>expose hypocrisy 揭露虚伪<br>hypocrite n. 伪君子<br>hypocritical adj. 虚伪的 | It's **hypocrisy** to say you care about the poor but never donate. （说你关心穷人却从不捐款，这就是虚伪。）<br>His speech was full of **hypocrisy**. （他的演讲充满了虚伪。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：insincerity（不真诚）/ double standard（双重标准）· **反义**：sincerity（真诚）· **易混**：hypocrisy（虚伪）vs hypocrisy ≠ hypocrisy（≠ hypocrisy vs hypothesis 假设）· hypo- 下 + crisy 判断 → 表里不如一|
| | stereo | /ˈsterioʊ/ | n. 立体声；立体音响<br>adj. 立体的 | stereo system 立体声音响<br>stereo sound 立体声<br>stereo speaker 立体声扬声器<br>stereotype n. 刻板印象 | He bought a new **stereo** for his living room. （他给客厅买了一台新的立体声音响。）<br>The movie is available in **stereo** sound. （这部电影有立体声版本。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hi-fi（高保真）· **反义**：mono（单声道）· **易混**：stereo（立体声）vs stereotype（刻板印象）vs stereo ≠ stereo（≠ stereo vs stern 严厉）|
| | exaggerate | /ɪɡˈzædʒəreɪt/ | v. 夸张，夸大 | exaggerate the importance 夸大重要性<br>exaggerate the truth 夸大事实<br>without exaggerating 毫不夸张地说<br>exaggeration n. 夸张 | He tends to **exaggerate** his achievements. （他倾向于夸大自己的成就。）<br>Don't **exaggerate** — it wasn't that bad. （别夸张了——没那么糟。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：overstate（夸大）/ magnify（放大）· **反义**：understate（轻描淡写）· **易混**：exaggerate（夸张）vs aggregate（聚集）vs exagitate ≠ aggravate（加重）|
| | comrade | /ˈkɑːmræd/ | n. 同志；同伴，<br>战友 | comrade in arms 战友<br>fellow comrade 同志<br>comradeship n. 同志情谊 | The soldiers supported their wounded **comrade**. （士兵们搀扶着受伤的战友。）<br>He addressed the crowd as "fellow **comrades**". （他对人群称"同志们"。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：companion（同伴）/ colleague（同事）· **易混**：comrade（同志）vs comrade ≠ comrade（≠ comrade vs commerce 商业）· 常用于政治/军事语境|
| | cease | /siːs/ | v. 停止，终止，<br>结束 | cease to do sth 停止做某事<br>cease fire 停火<br>without cease 不停地<br>cease and desist 停止并终止 | The factory **ceased** production last year. （工厂去年停止了生产。）<br>The rain finally **ceased** and the sun came out. （雨终于停了，太阳出来了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stop（停止）/ halt（使停止）/ discontinue（中止）· **反义**：continue（继续）/ begin（开始）· **易混**：cease（停止）vs seize（抓住，拼写极像！）vs cease ≠ cease（≠ cease vs teaser 戏弄者）|
| | merit (第2次) | /ˈmerɪt/ | n. 优点，价值；功绩<br>v. 值得，应得 | have merit 有价值<br>merit pay 绩效工资<br>on its own merits 根据本身情况<br>merit attention 值得关注 | The plan has considerable **merit**. （这个计划很有价值。）<br>Your suggestion **merits** further discussion. （你的建议值得进一步讨论。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：value（价值）/ worth（值得）· **反义**：demerit（缺点）/ flaw（缺陷）· **易混**：merit（优点/值得）vs merit ≠ merit（≠ merit vs merely 仅仅）|
| | revolve | /rɪˈvɑːlv/ | v. 旋转，绕转；<br>以…为中心 | revolve around 围绕…旋转；以…为中心<br>revolve on 绕…旋转<br>revolving door 旋转门<br>revolution n. 革命；旋转一周 | The earth **revolves** around the sun. （地球绕太阳旋转。）<br>Her entire life **revolves** around her children. （她的整个生活以孩子为中心。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：rotate（旋转，自转）/ spin（快速旋转）/ orbit（沿轨道运行）· **反义**：stand still（静止）· **易混**：revolve（旋转/围绕）vs evolve（进化）vs involve（涉及）vs revolt（反抗）|
| | commence | /kəˈmens/ | v. 开始，着手 | commence doing/to do sth 开始做某事<br>commence with 以…开始<br>commencement n. 开始；毕业典礼<br>commencement ceremony 毕业典礼 | The meeting **commenced** at 9 a.m. sharp. （会议上午九点准时开始。）<br>She **commenced** her career as a teacher in a small village. （她在一个小村庄开始了教师生涯。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：begin（开始，最常用）/ start（开始）/ initiate（发起）· **反义**：cease（停止）/ conclude（结束）/ terminate（终止）· **易混**：commence（开始）vs commerce（商业）vs commend（赞扬）vs comment（评论）|
| | brace (第2次) | /breɪs/ | v. 使防备；支撑，加固<br>n. 支架；（pl.）牙箍 | brace oneself for 为…做好准备<br>brace up 振作起来<br>neck brace 颈托<br>brace for impact 准备迎接冲击 | We **braced** ourselves for the upcoming storm. （我们为即将来临的暴风雨做好了准备。）<br>The wall needs to be **braced** with steel beams. （这面墙需要用钢梁加固。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：prepare oneself（做好准备）/ support（支撑）/ reinforce（加固）· **易混**：brace（支架/防备）vs embrace（拥抱）vs trace（追踪）|
| | sow | /soʊ/ | v. 播种；散布，<br>煽动 | sow seeds 播种<br>sow doubt 散布怀疑<br>sow discord 煽动不和<br>reap what you sow 种瓜得瓜 | The farmer **sowed** wheat in the field. （农民在田里播种小麦。）<br>They tried to **sow** doubt among the voters. （他们试图在选民中散布怀疑。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：plant（种植）/ scatter（撒播）/ spread（散布）· **反义**：reap（收获）/ harvest（收割）· **易混**：sow（播种）vs sew（缝纫，同音 /soʊ/！）vs saw（看见/锯子）· ⚠️ 过去式 sowed，过去分词 sown/sowed|
| | coordinate | /koʊˈɔːrdɪneɪt/ | v. 协调，配合<br>/koʊˈɔːrdɪnət/<br>n. 坐标；配套服装 | coordinate with 与…协调<br>coordinate efforts 协调努力<br>coordinate system 坐标系<br>coordination n. 协调 | We need to **coordinate** our schedules for the meeting. （我们需要协调日程来安排会议。）<br>The rescue teams **coordinated** their efforts effectively. （救援队有效协调了各方努力。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：organize（组织）/ synchronize（同步）/ harmonize（协调）· **反义**：disorganize（打乱）· **易混**：coordinate（协调）vs cooperate（合作）vs subordinate（下属/从属）· 词根：co-共同 + ordin-秩序 = 使有共同秩序 → 协调|
| | lump | /lʌmp/ | n. 块，肿块；<br>（方）糖块<br>v. 把…归并在一起 | a lump of 一块…<br>lump sum 一次性付款<br>lump together 把…混为一谈<br>have a lump in one's throat 如鲠在喉 | He found a **lump** on his neck. （他发现脖子上有个肿块。）<br>Don't **lump** all these problems together. （别把所有问题混为一谈。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bump（碰撞肿块）/ chunk（大块）/ mass（团块）· **易混**：lump（块/肿块）vs limp（跛行）vs lamp（灯）vs lump ≠ bump（肿块因碰撞）|
| | nominate | /ˈnɑːmɪneɪt/ | v. 提名，推荐；<br>任命，指定 | nominate sb for 提名某人为…<br>nominate sb as/to 任命某人为…<br>be nominated for an award 被提名奖项<br>nomination n. 提名 | He was **nominated** for the Nobel Peace Prize. （他被提名诺贝尔和平奖。）<br>She **nominated** her best friend as her successor. （她提名最好的朋友作为继任者。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：appoint（任命）/ propose（提议）/ designate（指定）· **反义**：reject（拒绝）/ dismiss（解雇）· **易混**：nominate（提名）vs dominate（支配）vs nominal（名义上的）vs nominee（被提名者，名词）· 词根：nomin- 名字 → 提名|
| | beam | /biːm/ | n. 梁，横梁；<br>光束，光线<br>v. 发光；微笑；发射 | beam with joy 喜气洋洋<br>laser beam 激光束<br>steel beam 钢梁<br>on the beam 正确的，对头的 | She **beamed** with pride as her son received the award. （儿子领奖时她骄傲得满面笑容。）<br>The sun's **beams** shone through the window. （阳光透过窗户照射进来。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ray（光线）/ girder（大梁）/ smile（微笑）· **易混**：beam（梁/光束/微笑）vs bean（豆子）vs beam ≠ beam（≠ beam vs seem 似乎）|
| | screw | /skruː/ | n. 螺丝，螺钉<br>v. 拧紧；旋动 | screw up 弄糟；鼓起勇气<br>screw driver 螺丝刀<br>tighten/loosen a screw 拧紧/拧松螺丝<br>have a screw loose 脑子有毛病 | He **screwed** the lid on tightly. （他把盖子拧紧了。）<br>I really **screwed** up the exam. （我考试真的考砸了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bolt（螺栓）/ twist（扭转）/ tighten（拧紧）· **反义**：unscrew（拧开）· **易混**：screw（螺丝/拧）vs crew（船员）vs shrew（泼妇）|
| | alloy | /ˈælɔɪ/ | n. 合金<br>/əˈlɔɪ/<br>v. 将…铸成合金；<br>掺杂 | aluminum alloy 铝合金<br>alloy steel 合金钢<br>alloy of copper and zinc 铜锌合金<br>alloy with 与…混合 | Brass is an **alloy** of copper and zinc. （黄铜是铜和锌的合金。）<br>The happiness was **alloyed** with a touch of sadness. （快乐中掺杂着一丝悲伤。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mixture（混合物）/ blend（混合）/ compound（化合物）· **易混**：alloy（合金）vs ally（盟友，拼写极像！）vs alley（小巷）vs allay（减轻）|
| | inferior | /ɪnˈfɪriər/ | adj. 较差的，次的；<br>下级的<br>n. 下级，下属 | be inferior to 比…差<br>inferior quality 劣质<br>feel inferior 感到自卑<br>inferiority complex 自卑情结 | This brand is **inferior** to that one in quality. （这个品牌在质量上比那个差。）<br>He always feels **inferior** to his colleagues. （他总觉得比同事矮一截。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：substandard（低于标准的）/ subordinate（下级的）/ worse（更差的）· **反义**：superior（优越的，上级的）· **易混**：inferior（较差的）vs interior（内部的）vs infer（推断）|
| | swallow | /ˈswɑːloʊ/ | v. 吞咽；吞没；<br>轻信；忍受<br>n. 燕子；吞咽 | swallow up 吞没，吞噬<br>swallow one's pride 放下自尊<br>hard to swallow 难以接受<br>swallow the bait 上钩 | He **swallowed** the pill with a glass of water. （他就着一杯水吞下了药片。）<br>It's a hard fact to **swallow**, but we have to accept it. （这个事实很难接受，但我们不得不面对。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：gulp（大口吞）/ devour（狼吞虎咽）/ absorb（吞并）· **易混**：swallow（吞咽/燕子）vs shallow（浅的）vs swallow ≠ swallow（≠ swallow vs sparrow 麻雀）|
| | inherent | /ɪnˈhɪrənt/ | adj. 固有的，内在的，<br>与生俱来的 | be inherent in 是…固有的<br>inherent contradiction 内在矛盾<br>inherent risk 固有风险<br>inherently adv. 固有地 | There are risks **inherent** in any investment. （任何投资都有固有的风险。）<br>She has an **inherent** sense of justice. （她有一种与生俱来的正义感。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：intrinsic（本质的）/ innate（天生的）/ built-in（内置的）· **反义**：acquired（后天习得的）/ external（外在的）· **易混**：inherent（固有的）vs coherent（连贯的）vs inherit（继承）|
| | vicious | /ˈvɪʃəs/ | adj. 恶毒的，凶残的；<br>剧烈的，严重的 | vicious cycle/circle 恶性循环<br>vicious attack 残忍的攻击<br>vicious rumor 恶毒的谣言<br>viciously adv. 恶毒地 | The country is trapped in a **vicious** cycle of poverty and violence. （该国陷入了贫穷与暴力的恶性循环。）<br>He made a **vicious** remark about her appearance. （他对她的外表做了恶毒的评论。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：malicious（恶意的）/ brutal（残忍的）/ savage（凶猛的）· **反义**：kind（善良的）/ gentle（温和的）/ virtuous（有道德的）· **易混**：vicious（恶毒的）vs vicious cycle（恶性循环，考研高频！）vs vice（恶习）vs viscous（粘稠的）|
| | thrust (第3次) | /θrʌst/ | v. 猛推，猛塞；刺，戳<br>n. 推力；要点，主旨 | thrust sth into 塞进/刺入<br>thrust sb aside 推开某人<br>the main thrust 核心要点<br>thrust and parry 刺与挡 | He **thrust** his hands into his pockets. （他把双手插进口袋。）<br>The main **thrust** of the argument is clear. （论证的核心要点很明确。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：push（推）/ shove（粗鲁推）/ stab（刺）· **易混**：thrust（猛推/刺）vs trust（信任）vs thrust ≠ thrust（≠ thrust vs crust 外壳）|
| | furnish | /ˈfɜːrnɪʃ/ | v. 布置家具；<br>提供，供应 | furnish with 用…装备/提供<br>furnish a room 布置房间<br>furnish information 提供信息<br>furnished apartment 带家具的公寓 | The apartment is fully **furnished**. （这套公寓家具齐全。）<br>The report **furnished** us with all the necessary information. （报告给我们提供了所有必要信息。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：equip（装备）/ provide（提供）/ supply（供应）· **易混**：furnish（提供/布置）vs furniture（家具，名词）vs finish（完成）vs flourish（繁荣）|
| | convert | /kənˈvɜːrt/ | v. 转变，转换；<br>使皈依，改变信仰<br>n. 皈依者，改变信仰者 | convert A into/to B 把A转化为B<br>convert to 转而信奉…<br>convertible adj. 可转换的<br>conversion n. 转变，皈依 | The old factory was **converted** into apartments. （旧工厂被改造成了公寓。）<br>She **converted** to Buddhism in her twenties. （她二十多岁时皈依了佛教。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：transform（转变）/ change（改变）/ switch（切换）· **易混**：convert（转变/皈依）vs converse（交谈）vs convict（定罪）vs convey（传达）· 词根：con-完全 + vert-转 → 彻底转变|
| | liner | /ˈlaɪnər/ | n. 班轮，邮轮；<br>衬里，内衬 | ocean liner 远洋班轮<br>cruise liner 豪华邮轮<br>eyeliner 眼线笔<br>bin liner 垃圾袋内衬 | The luxury **liner** can accommodate 3,000 passengers. （这艘豪华邮轮可容纳3000名乘客。）<br>She applied **eyeliner** carefully before the party. （派对前她仔细地画了眼线。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cruise ship（邮轮）/ vessel（船只）/ lining（衬里）· **易混**：liner（班轮/衬里）vs line（线/航线）vs linear（线性的）vs liner ≠ liner（≠ liner vs liver 肝脏）|
| | valid | /ˈvælɪd/ | adj. 有效的，合法的；<br>有根据的，合理的 | valid passport 有效护照<br>valid reason 正当理由<br>valid point 有道理的观点<br>validity n. 有效性 | Your passport must be **valid** for at least six months. （你的护照必须有至少六个月有效期。）<br>She raised a **valid** point about the budget. （她提出了关于预算的合理观点。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：effective（有效的）/ legitimate（合法的）/ sound（合理的）· **反义**：invalid（无效的）/ void（作废的）· **易混**：valid（有效的）vs vivid（生动的）vs value（价值）|
| | booth (第2次) | /buːθ/ | n. 小隔间；摊位；<br>电话亭 | phone booth 电话亭<br>ticket booth 售票亭<br>voting booth 投票间<br>photo booth 自助照相亭 | I called her from a phone **booth**. （我从电话亭给她打了电话。）<br>We bought tickets at the **booth** near the entrance. （我们在入口附近的售票亭买了票。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stall（摊位）/ kiosk（小亭子）· **易混**：booth（小隔间）vs boot（靴子）vs both（两者）|
| | component | /kəmˈpoʊnənt/ | n. 组成部分，成分，<br>部件<br>adj. 组成的 | key component 关键组成部分<br>electronic component 电子元件<br>component part 零部件<br>component of …的组成部分 | Trust is a key **component** of any successful relationship. （信任是任何成功关系的关键组成部分。）<br>The factory supplies **components** for the car industry. （这家工厂为汽车工业供应零部件。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：element（要素）/ part（部分）/ ingredient（成分，尤指食物）· **易混**：component（组成部分）vs opponent（对手）vs compost（堆肥）· 词根：com-共同 + pon-放置 = 放在一起 → 组成部分|
| | cottage | /ˈkɑːtɪdʒ/ | n. 小屋，村舍；<br>别墅 | country cottage 乡村小屋<br>summer cottage 避暑别墅<br>cottage cheese 农家干酪<br>cottage industry 家庭手工业 | They spent the weekend in a cozy **cottage** by the lake. （他们在湖边一间舒适的小屋里度了周末。）<br>The village has many charming stone **cottages**. （村里有很多迷人的石头小屋。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cabin（小木屋）/ hut（简陋小屋）/ villa（别墅）· **易混**：cottage（小屋）vs cottage ≠ cottage（≠ cottage vs collage 拼贴画）vs cotton（棉花）|
| | aspire | /əˈspaɪər/ | v. 渴望，有志于，<br>立志 | aspire to do sth 渴望做某事<br>aspire to sth 向往某事<br>aspire to be 立志成为<br>aspiration n. 抱负，志向 | She **aspires** to become a successful writer. （她立志成为一位成功的作家。）<br>He **aspired** to greatness from a young age. （他从小就志存高远。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：desire（渴望）/ strive for（努力追求）/ aim（目标）· **易混**：aspire（渴望）vs inspire（激励）vs expire（到期）· 词根：a-向 + spir-呼吸 = 向着…呼吸 → 渴望|
| | drip | /drɪp/ | v. 滴下，滴落<br>n. 水滴；滴水声；<br>（医）点滴 | drip from 从…滴下<br>drip with sweat 汗流浃背<br>IV drip 静脉滴注<br>drip coffee 滴滤咖啡 | Water was **dripping** from the leaky faucet. （水从漏水的水龙头滴下来。）<br>He was put on a **drip** after the surgery. （手术后他挂上了点滴。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：drop（滴落）/ trickle（细流）/ leak（漏）· **易混**：drip（滴下）vs drop（掉落/放弃）vs dip（浸一下）vs drip ≠ drip（≠ drip vs grip 紧握）|
| | furnace | /ˈfɜːrnɪs/ | n. 熔炉，火炉；<br>暖气炉 | blast furnace 高炉<br>coal furnace 煤炉<br>gas furnace 燃气暖气炉<br>like a furnace 像火炉一样热 | The steel is melted in a huge **furnace**. （钢在巨大的熔炉中熔化。）<br>It feels like a **furnace** in here — can you open the window? （这里面热得像火炉——能开个窗吗？） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：oven（烤炉）/ kiln（窑）/ heater（暖气）· **易混**：furnace（熔炉）vs furniture（家具）vs furnish（提供，刚记的！）vs furnace ≠ furnace（≠ furnace vs funeral 葬礼）|
| | cupboard | /ˈkʌbərd/ | n. 碗柜，橱柜 | kitchen cupboard 厨房橱柜<br>cupboard door 橱柜门<br>airing cupboard 晾物柜<br>skeleton in the cupboard 家丑 | She put the plates back in the **cupboard**. （她把盘子放回了碗柜里。）<br>Every family has a **skeleton in the cupboard**. （家家都有不可告人的秘密。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cabinet（柜子）/ closet（壁橱）/ wardrobe（衣柜）· **易混**：cupboard（橱柜，p不发音！）vs cupboard ≠ cupboard（≠ cupboard vs clipboard 写字板）· cup + board = 放杯子的板 → 碗柜|
| | constitution | /ˌkɑːnstɪˈtuːʃn/ | n. 宪法；章程；<br>体格，体质；构成 | US Constitution 美国宪法<br>draft a constitution 起草宪法<br>have a strong constitution 体格强壮<br>constitutional adj. 宪法的 | The **Constitution** guarantees freedom of speech. （宪法保障言论自由。）<br>He has a strong **constitution** and rarely gets sick. （他体格强壮，很少生病。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：charter（宪章）/ law（法律）/ physique（体格）· **易混**：constitution（宪法/体格）vs institution（机构）vs constitute（构成，动词）· 词根：con- + stitut-立 + -ion = 建立起来的东西 → 宪法/构成|
| | pledge | /pledʒ/ | n. 誓言，保证；<br>抵押品<br>v. 发誓，保证；抵押 | pledge to do sth 承诺做某事<br>pledge allegiance 宣誓效忠<br>fulfill a pledge 履行承诺<br>take the pledge 发誓戒酒 | The government **pledged** to reduce taxes. （政府承诺减税。）<br>He **pledged** his support for the campaign. （他保证支持这场运动。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：promise（承诺）/ vow（誓言）/ oath（宣誓）/ commit（承诺）· **反义**：break a promise（食言）· **易混**：pledge（承诺/誓言）vs edge（边缘）vs ledge（窗台）vs sledge（雪橇）|
| | preceding | /prɪˈsiːdɪŋ/ | adj. 前面的，在先的 | the preceding chapter 前一章<br>in the preceding year 在上一年<br>preceding paragraph 前一段<br>precede v. 先于，在…之前 | The **preceding** chapter discussed the causes of the war. （前一章讨论了战争的起因。）<br>In the **preceding** decade, technology developed rapidly. （在此前十年的时间内，科技发展迅速。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：previous（先前的）/ prior（在先的）/ foregoing（前述的）· **反义**：following（随后的）/ succeeding（继…之后的）/ subsequent（后来的）· **易混**：preceding（前面的）vs proceeding（诉讼/会议记录）vs preceding ≠ preceding（≠ precedent 先例）|
| | contrive | /kənˈtraɪv/ | v. 设法做到；<br>策划，图谋；设计 | contrive to do sth 设法做某事<br>contrive a way 想方设法<br>contrive a plan 策划一个计划<br>contrived adj. 人为的，做作的 | He **contrived** to escape from the prison. （他设法从监狱逃了出来。）<br>The ending of the movie felt too **contrived**. （电影的结局感觉太刻意了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：manage（设法）/ devise（设计，想出）/ scheme（策划）· **易混**：contrive（设法/策划）vs contrive ≠ contrive（≠ contrive vs strive 努力）vs contrived（人为的，常贬义）|
| | hostage | /ˈhɑːstɪdʒ/ | n. 人质 | take/hold sb hostage 劫持某人<br>be held hostage 被扣作人质<br>hostage crisis 人质危机<br>hostage negotiator 人质谈判专家 | The gunmen **took** three staff members **hostage**. （持枪歹徒劫持了三名员工作为人质。）<br>She was **held hostage** for over a week. （她被扣作人质超过一周。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：captive（俘虏）/ prisoner（囚犯）· **易混**：hostage（人质）vs host（主人/主持）vs hostile（敌对的）vs hostage ≠ hostage（≠ hostage vs postage 邮费）|
| | rebel | /ˈrebl/ n.<br>/rɪˈbel/ v. | n. 反叛者，叛逆者<br>v. 反抗，造反；<br>叛逆 | rebel against 反抗，叛逆<br>rebel forces 叛军<br>teenage rebel 叛逆的青少年<br>rebellion n. 叛乱 | The **rebels** took control of the capital. （叛军控制了首都。）<br>Teenagers often **rebel** against their parents' rules. （青少年经常反抗父母的规定。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：revolt（起义）/ resist（反抗）/ insurgent（叛乱者）· **反义**：obey（服从）/ conform（顺从）· **易混**：rebel（反叛）vs repel（击退/使厌恶）vs revel（狂欢）|
| | taboo | /təˈbuː/ | n. 禁忌，忌讳<br>adj. 禁忌的，忌讳的 | social taboo 社会禁忌<br>break a taboo 打破禁忌<br>taboo subject 禁忌话题<br>taboo against 对…的禁忌 | In some cultures, talking about money is a **taboo**. （在某些文化中，谈论金钱是一种禁忌。）<br>There are no **taboo** topics in this therapy group. （这个治疗小组中没有任何禁忌话题。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：forbidden（被禁止的）/ prohibition（禁令）/ stigma（污名）· **反义**：acceptable（可接受的）/ permitted（允许的）· **易混**：taboo（禁忌）vs tattoo（纹身，拼写像！）vs bamboo（竹子）|
| | stationery | /ˈsteɪʃəneri/ | n. 文具；信纸<br>（总称） | stationery store 文具店<br>office stationery 办公文具<br>wedding stationery 婚礼请柬<br>stationer n. 文具商 | She bought some new **stationery** for the new semester. （她为新学期买了一些新文具。）<br>They chose elegant **stationery** for their wedding invitations. （他们为婚礼请柬选了精美的信纸。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：writing materials（书写用品）/ office supplies（办公用品）· **易混**：stationery（文具，e）vs stationary（静止的，a）— **同音异义，考研经典陷阱！** ⚠️|
| | discrepancy | /dɪˈskrepənsi/ | n. 差异，不一致，<br>出入 | discrepancy between A and B A与B的差异<br>apparent discrepancy 明显的不一致<br>discrepancy in …方面的出入<br>discrepant adj. 不一致的 | There is a large **discrepancy** between his words and his actions. （他的言行之间存在很大的不一致。）<br>The auditor found **discrepancies** in the financial records. （审计员在财务记录中发现了出入。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：difference（差异）/ inconsistency（不一致）/ gap（差距）· **反义**：consistency（一致）/ agreement（一致）· **易混**：discrepancy（差异）vs discrepancy ≠ discrepancy（≠ discrepancy vs discre 离散的）|
| | prudent | /ˈpruːdnt/ | adj. 谨慎的，审慎的，<br>精明的 | it is prudent to do sth 做某事是明智的<br>prudent investment 谨慎的投资<br>prudent decision 审慎的决定<br>prudence n. 谨慎 | It would be **prudent** to save some money for emergencies. （存一些钱以备不时之需是明智的。）<br>A **prudent** investor always considers the risks. （审慎的投资者总是考虑风险。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cautious（谨慎的）/ wise（明智的）/ judicious（审慎的）· **反义**：reckless（鲁莽的）/ imprudent（轻率的）· **易混**：prudent（谨慎的）vs proud（骄傲的）vs prudent ≠ prudent（≠ prudent vs student 学生）|
| | arouse | /əˈraʊz/ | v. 唤起，激起；<br>唤醒 | arouse interest 引起兴趣<br>arouse suspicion 引起怀疑<br>arouse anger 激起愤怒<br>arouse sb from sleep 把某人唤醒 | The lecture **aroused** my interest in astronomy. （这场讲座激起了我对天文学的兴趣。）<br>His strange behavior **aroused** the police's suspicion. （他奇怪的行为引起了警方的怀疑。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stir（激起）/ provoke（激起，挑衅）/ awaken（唤醒）· **反义**：suppress（压制）/ calm（平息）· **易混**：arouse（唤起）vs arise（出现，vi.）vs rouse（唤醒，更直接）|
| | hoist | /hɔɪst/ | v. 吊起，升起，<br>提起<br>n. 起重机；升起 | hoist a flag 升旗<br>hoist up 吊起<br>hoist the sails 扬帆<br>give sb a hoist 拉某人一把 | The sailors **hoisted** the flag at sunrise. （水手们在日出时升旗。）<br>He **hoisted** the heavy box onto his shoulder. （他把重箱子扛到肩上。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：lift（举起）/ raise（升起）/ heave（用力举起）· **反义**：lower（降下）· **易混**：hoist（吊起）vs host（主人）vs moist（潮湿的）vs hoist ≠ hoist（≠ hoist vs joist 托梁）|
| | calcium | /ˈkælsiəm/ | n. 钙 | calcium deficiency 缺钙<br>calcium supplement 钙补充剂<br>calcium carbonate 碳酸钙<br>rich in calcium 富含钙 | Milk is rich in **calcium**, which is good for bones. （牛奶富含钙，对骨骼有益。）<br>She takes **calcium** supplements every day. （她每天吃钙片。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mineral（矿物质）/ element（元素）· **易混**：calcium（钙）vs calculate（计算）vs calculus（微积分，同词根！）· 词根 calc- 石头 → 钙来自石灰石|
| | oral | /ˈɔːrəl/ | adj. 口头的，口腔的<br>n. 口试 | oral exam 口试<br>oral hygiene 口腔卫生<br>oral tradition 口头传统<br>orally adv. 口头地 | We have an **oral** exam next week. （下周我们有口试。）<br>Good **oral** hygiene is important for healthy teeth. （良好的口腔卫生对牙齿健康很重要。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：verbal（口头的）/ spoken（口语的）· **反义**：written（书面的）· **易混**：oral（口头的）vs aural（听觉的）— **同音 /ˈɔːrəl/！考研经典陷阱！**|
| | petrol | /ˈpetrəl/ | n. 汽油<br>（美式：gasoline） | petrol station 加油站<br>petrol tank 汽油箱<br>petrol engine 汽油发动机<br>unleaded petrol 无铅汽油 | We need to stop at a **petrol** station to fill up. （我们得在加油站停一下加油。）<br>The price of **petrol** has risen again. （汽油价格又涨了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：gasoline（汽油，美式）· **易混**：petrol（汽油）vs patrol（巡逻）vs petroleum（石油）· 词根：petr- 石 + ol- 油 → 石油 → 汽油|
| | spacious | /ˈspeɪʃəs/ | adj. 宽敞的，广阔的 | spacious room 宽敞的房间<br>spacious interior 宽敞的内部<br>spacious garden 宽阔的花园<br>spaciousness n. 宽敞 | The apartment is bright and **spacious**. （这套公寓明亮宽敞。）<br>They moved to a more **spacious** house in the suburbs. （他们搬到郊区更宽敞的房子了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：roomy（宽敞的）/ capacious（容量大的）/ vast（广阔的）· **反义**：cramped（狭窄的）/ crowded（拥挤的）· **易混**：spacious（宽敞的）vs specious（貌似有理的，同音！）vs space（空间）|
| | sly | /slaɪ/ | adj. 狡猾的，狡诈的；<br>会意的，心照不宣的 | on the sly 偷偷地，秘密地<br>sly smile 会意的微笑<br>sly trick 狡猾的诡计<br>as sly as a fox 像狐狸一样狡猾 | He's as **sly** as a fox — you can never trust him. （他像狐狸一样狡猾——你永远不能信任他。）<br>She gave me a **sly** glance and grinned. （她偷偷瞥了我一眼，咧嘴笑了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cunning（狡猾的）/ crafty（诡计多端的）/ sneaky（鬼鬼祟祟的）· **反义**：honest（诚实的）/ frank（坦率的）· **易混**：sly（狡猾的）vs shy（害羞的）vs sky（天空）|
| | barren | /ˈbærən/ | adj. 贫瘠的，不毛的；<br>不育的；无成果的 | barren land 贫瘠的土地<br>barren woman 不育的妇女<br>barren discussion 毫无成果的讨论<br>barrenness n. 贫瘠 | The once fertile land has turned into a **barren** desert. （曾经肥沃的土地变成了贫瘠的沙漠。）<br>Their discussion was **barren** of any useful ideas. （他们的讨论没有任何有用的想法。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：infertile（不肥沃的）/ sterile（不育的）/ unproductive（无产出的）· **反义**：fertile（肥沃的）/ fruitful（有成果的）· **易混**：barren（贫瘠的）vs baron（男爵，同音！）vs barn（谷仓）|
| | stocking | /ˈstɑːkɪŋ/ | n. 长袜<br>（常用 pl. stockings） | Christmas stocking 圣诞袜<br>a pair of stockings 一双长袜<br>silk stockings 丝袜<br>stocking filler 圣诞袜小礼物 | She hung a **stocking** by the fireplace for Christmas. （她为圣诞节在壁炉旁挂了一只长袜。）<br>She put on a pair of silk **stockings**. （她穿上一双丝袜。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：socks（短袜）/ tights（紧身裤袜）/ pantyhose（连裤袜）· **易混**：stocking（长袜）vs stock（股票/库存）vs stocking ≠ stocking（≠ stocking vs shocking 令人震惊的）|
| | intact | /ɪnˈtækt/ | adj. 完好无损的，<br>原封不动的 | remain intact 保持完好<br>keep sth intact 保持…完好<br>largely intact 基本完好<br>intactness n. 完好 | The ancient temple remained remarkably **intact** after the earthquake. （地震后古庙竟基本完好无损。）<br>Most of the original features have been kept **intact**. （大部分原貌被原封不动地保留了下来。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：undamaged（未损坏的）/ complete（完整的）/ untouched（未触动的）· **反义**：damaged（损坏的）/ broken（破碎的）· **易混**：intact（完好无损的）vs intact ≠ intact（≠ intact vs contact 接触）vs in tact（✗ 别拆开写！）|
| | doze | /doʊz/ | v. 打瞌睡，打盹<br>n. 瞌睡 | doze off 打瞌睡，睡着了<br>have a doze 打个盹<br>doze through 在…过程中打瞌睡<br>doze fitfully 断断续续地打盹 | I **dozed** off during the long lecture. （在漫长的讲座中我打起了瞌睡。）<br>He had a quick **doze** after lunch. （午饭后他快速打了个盹。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：nap（小睡）/ snooze（打盹）/ drowse（昏昏欲睡）· **反义**：wake（醒来）/ stay alert（保持清醒）· **易混**：doze（打盹）vs dose（剂量）vs doze ≠ doze（≠ doze vs dozen 一打）|
| | curb | /kɜːrb/ | n. 控制，抑制；<br>路缘<br>v. 控制，抑制 | curb inflation 抑制通货膨胀<br>curb one's temper 控制脾气<br>curb on …的控制<br>put a curb on 限制 | The government introduced measures to **curb** inflation. （政府出台措施抑制通货膨胀。）<br>He needs to learn to **curb** his temper. （他需要学会控制脾气。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：restrain（约束）/ control（控制）/ limit（限制）/ suppress（压制）· **反义**：encourage（鼓励）/ unleash（释放）· **易混**：curb（抑制/路缘）vs curve（曲线）vs curl（卷曲）vs curb ≠ curb（≠ curb vs cub 幼兽）|
| | superfluous | /suːˈpɜːrfluəs/ | adj. 多余的，<br>过剩的 | superfluous words 多余的话<br>superfluous details 多余的细节<br>render sth superfluous 使…变得多余 | Most of the report was **superfluous**. （报告的大部分内容都是多余的。）<br>His comments were entirely **superfluous**. （他的评论完全是多余的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：unnecessary（不必要）/ redundant（冗余）· **反义**：essential（必要的）· **易混**：superfluous（多余）vs superficial（表面的，同 super- 前缀）· super + flu(流) + ous → 流出来的 → 多余的|
| | drawback | /ˈdrɔːbæk/ | n. 缺点，弊端；<br>不利条件 | major drawback 主要缺点<br>the only drawback 唯一的缺点<br>despite this drawback 尽管有这个缺点 | The only **drawback** is the cost. （唯一的缺点是成本。）<br>One **drawback** of living downtown is the noise. （住市中心的一个缺点是噪音。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：disadvantage（劣势）/ shortcoming（短处）· **反义**：advantage（优势）/ benefit（好处）· **易混**：drawback（缺点）vs setback（挫折）vs drawback ≠ drawback（≠ drawback vs drawback 两词义不同）|
| | franchise | /ˈfræntʃaɪz/ | n. 特许经营权；<br>选举权<br>v. 授予特许经营权 | fast-food franchise 快餐连锁加盟店<br>franchise agreement 特许经营协议<br>universal franchise 普选权<br>franchisee n. 特许经营者 | McDonald's operates as a **franchise** worldwide. （麦当劳以特许经营方式在全球运营。）<br>Women were granted the **franchise** in 1920. （女性于1920年获得了选举权。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：license（许可）/ chain store（连锁店）· **易混**：franchise（特许经营/选举权）vs franchise ≠ franchise（≠ franchise vs enumerate 枚举）· 一词两义：商业"加盟" + 政治"选举权"|
| | overhear | /ˌoʊvərˈhɪr/ | v. 无意中听到；<br>偷听到 | overhear a conversation 无意中听到对话<br>overhear sb saying sth 无意中听到某人说某事<br>couldn't help overhearing 忍不住听到了 | I **overheard** them talking about the surprise party. （我无意中听到他们在谈论惊喜派对。）<br>She **overheard** her colleagues gossiping in the break room. （她无意中听到同事在休息室八卦。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：eavesdrop（故意偷听）· **易混**：overhear（无意听到）vs eavesdrop（故意偷听，本质区别！）vs overhear ≠ overlook（俯瞰/忽视）|
| | stipulate | /ˈstɪpjuleɪt/ | v. 规定，明确要求 | stipulate that 规定…<br>as stipulated in 按照…的规定<br>stipulate conditions 规定条件<br>stipulation n. 规定，条款 | The contract **stipulates** that payment must be made within 30 days. （合同规定款项须在30天内支付。）<br>The regulations **stipulate** a minimum wage for all workers. （法规规定了所有工人的最低工资。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：specify（明确说明）/ prescribe（规定）· **易混**：stipulate（规定）vs stimulate（刺激）vs simulate（模拟）|
| | plaster | /ˈplæstər/ | n. 灰泥；石膏；<br>创可贴<br>v. 涂灰泥；张贴 | plaster cast 石膏绷带<br>sticking plaster 创可贴<br>plaster the walls 粉刷墙壁<br>plaster with posters 贴满海报 | His arm was in a **plaster** cast for six weeks. （他的手臂打了六周石膏。）<br>The walls were **plastered** with movie posters. （墙上贴满了电影海报。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bandage（绷带）/ cement（水泥）· **易混**：plaster（石膏/灰泥）vs plastic（塑料）vs plaster ≠ plaster（≠ plaster vs pasture 牧场）|
| | tow | /toʊ/ | v./n. 拖，拉，牵引 | tow away 拖走<br>tow truck 拖车<br>in tow 紧随在后<br>take in tow 管教，照料 | The car was **towed** away by the police. （车被警察拖走了。）<br>She arrived with three kids **in tow**. （她带着三个孩子来了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：drag（拖拽）/ haul（拖运）· **易混**：tow（拖）vs toe（脚趾，同音！）vs tow ≠ tow（≠ tow vs bow 鞠躬）|
| | skull | /skʌl/ | n. 头骨，颅骨 | fracture one's skull 头骨骨折<br>skull and crossbones 骷髅标志<br>thick skull 迟钝，笨<br>skull cap 无边帽 | The X-ray showed a crack in his **skull**. （X光显示他的头骨有裂痕。）<br>He can't get it into his thick **skull**. （他就是理解不了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cranium（颅骨）· **易混**：skull（头骨）vs skill（技能）vs skull ≠ skull（≠ skull vs skeleton 骨架）|
| | escort | /ˈeskɔːrt/ | n. 护送者；护卫队<br>v. /ɪˈskɔːrt/ 护送 | police escort 警察护送<br>under escort 被押送<br>escort sb to 护送某人到… | The president arrived with a police **escort**. （总统在警察护送下到达。）<br>He **escorted** her to the door. （他护送她到门口。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：accompany（陪同）/ guard（护卫）· **易混**：escort（护送）vs escort ≠ escort（≠ escort vs escape 逃跑）|
| | porcelain | /ˈpɔːrsəlɪn/ | n. 瓷器<br>adj. 瓷的 | porcelain vase 瓷花瓶<br>fine porcelain 精美瓷器<br>porcelain doll 瓷娃娃<br>china/porcelain 中国/瓷器 | The museum has a collection of ancient Chinese **porcelain**. （博物馆收藏了一批中国古代瓷器。）<br>She treasured the delicate **porcelain** teacup. （她珍藏着那只精致的瓷茶杯。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：china（瓷器，更通用）· **易混**：porcelain（瓷器）vs porcelain ≠ porcelain（注意拼写：porce-lain，不是 procelain！）|
| | apparatus (第2次) | /ˌæpəˈrætəs/ | n. 仪器，设备；机构 | state apparatus 国家机器<br>breathing apparatus 呼吸器 | The lab has state-of-the-art **apparatus**. （实验室有最先进的设备。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：equipment/appliance · **易混**：apparatus vs appetite（食欲）|
| | chancellor | /ˈtʃænsələr/ | n. 大臣；总理；<br>（大学）校长 | Chancellor of the Exchequer（英）财政大臣<br>vice chancellor 副校长<br>shadow chancellor 影子财政大臣 | The **Chancellor** announced a new budget today. （财政大臣今天宣布了新预算。）<br>She is the first female **chancellor** of the university. （她是该校首位女校长。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：prime minister（首相）/ principal（校长）· **易混**：chancellor（大臣/校长）vs chancel（圣坛）vs cancel（取消）|
| | stack (第2次) | /stæk/ | n. 一摞；大量<br>v. 堆放 | a stack of 一堆<br>stack up 堆积<br>odds are stacked against 形势不利 | There was a **stack** of papers on his desk. （他桌上有一摞文件。）<br>The odds are **stacked** against us. （形势对我们不利。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：pile/heap · **易混**：stack vs stock（库存，同音！）|
| | opaque | /oʊˈpeɪk/ | adj. 不透明的；<br>晦涩难懂的 | opaque glass 不透明玻璃<br>opaque language 晦涩的语言<br>opaque to light 不透光的<br>opacity n. 不透明度 | The window was made of **opaque** glass. （窗户用的是不透明玻璃。）<br>His writing style is too **opaque** for most readers. （他的写作风格对大多数读者来说太晦涩了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：unclear（不清楚）/ cloudy（浑浊）· **反义**：transparent（透明）/ clear（清晰）· **易混**：opaque（不透明）vs opaque ≠ opaque（≠ opaque vs plaque 匾牌）|
| | instrumental | /ˌɪnstrəˈmentl/ | adj. 起关键作用的；<br>仪器的；器乐的 | be instrumental in 在…中起关键作用<br>instrumental music 器乐<br>instrumental role 关键作用<br>instrument n. 工具/乐器 | She was **instrumental** in securing the deal. （她在促成这笔交易中起了关键作用。）<br>The album is entirely **instrumental**. （这张专辑完全是器乐曲。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：crucial（关键的）/ pivotal（关键的）· **易混**：instrumental（关键的/器乐的）vs instrument（工具/乐器）vs instrumental ≠ instrumental（≠ instrumental vs install 安装）|
| | litter (第2次) | /ˈlɪtər/ | n. 垃圾；一窝幼崽<br>v. 乱扔垃圾 | no littering 禁止乱扔<br>a litter of puppies 一窝小狗<br>littered with 满是 | The floor was **littered** with papers. （地板上散落着纸片。）<br>A **litter** of puppies was born last night. （昨晚出生了一窝小狗。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：garbage/trash · **易混**：litter vs little（小的） vs liter（升）|
| | plagiarism (复现) | /ˈpleɪdʒərɪzəm/ | n. 抄袭，剽窃 | accuse of plagiarism 指控抄袭<br>plagiarize v. 抄袭 | The student was expelled for **plagiarism**. （该学生因抄袭被开除。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：copying（抄袭）· **易混**：plagiarism vs plague（瘟疫）|
| | transient (第2次) | /ˈtrænziənt/ | adj. 短暂的，转瞬即逝的 | transient pleasure 短暂快乐<br>transient population 流动人口 | The beauty of cherry blossoms is **transient**. （樱花之美是短暂的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：temporary · **反义**：permanent · **易混**：transient vs transparent（透明）|
| | integral (第2次) | /ˈɪntɪɡrəl/ | adj. 不可或缺的 | integral part of 不可或缺的部分<br>integral to 对…必不可少 | Communication is an **integral** part of teamwork. （沟通是团队协作不可或缺的一部分。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：essential/indispensable · **易混**：integral vs integrate（融合）vs integrity（正直）|
| | inertia | /ɪˈnɜːrʃə/ | n. 惯性；惰性；<br>缺乏活力 | overcome inertia 克服惰性<br>mental inertia 思维惰性<br>by inertia 凭惯性<br>inertial adj. 惯性的 | It's hard to overcome the **inertia** of old habits. （很难克服旧习惯的惯性。）<br>Bureaucratic **inertia** slowed down the reform. （官僚惰性拖慢了改革。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：laziness（懒惰，贬义）/ momentum（动量，物理）· **易混**：inertia（惯性/惰性）vs inert（惰性的）vs inertia ≠ inertia（≠ inertia vs insert 插入）|
| | startle | /ˈstɑːrtl/ | v. 使吃惊，使吓一跳 | be startled by 被…吓一跳<br>startle sb into doing 把某人吓得做某事<br>startling news 惊人的消息<br>startling adj. 惊人的 | A sudden noise **startled** her. （一声突然的响声吓了她一跳。）<br>I didn't mean to **startle** you. （我不是故意要吓你的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：surprise（使惊讶）/ frighten（吓唬）· **易混**：startle（使吓一跳）vs start（开始）vs starve（饿死）|
| | naive (第2次) | /naɪˈiːv/ | adj. 天真的，幼稚的 | naive belief 天真信念<br>naive optimism 盲目乐观 | It was **naive** of him to trust a stranger. （他信任陌生人太天真了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：innocent/gullible · **反义**：sophisticated · **易混**：naive vs native（本地人/本土的，拼写极像！）|
| | acquisition | /ˌækwɪˈzɪʃn/ | n. 获得；收购；<br>习得 | language acquisition 语言习得<br>merger and acquisition 并购<br>data acquisition 数据采集<br>acquire v. 获得 | The company announced the **acquisition** of a competitor. （公司宣布收购了一家竞争对手。）<br>Language **acquisition** is easier for young children. （小孩子习得语言更容易。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：purchase（购买）/ obtainment（获取）· **易混**：acquisition（获得/收购）vs acquire（获得，动词）vs acquisition ≠ acquisition（≠ acquisition vs inquisitive 好奇的）|
| | wreck (复现) | /rek/ | n. 残骸；v. 毁掉 | ship wreck 沉船<br>nervous wreck 精神崩溃 | The storm **wrecked** several boats. （暴风雨毁了几艘船。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ruin/destroy · **易混**：wreck vs wreak（造成）|
| | fling (复现) | /flɪŋ/ | v. 猛投，用力扔<br>n. 一时的放纵 | fling oneself into 投身于<br>have a fling 放纵一番 | He **flung** his bag onto the sofa. （他把包扔到沙发上。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：throw/toss · **易混**：fling vs slang（俚语）|
| | cellar | /ˈselər/ | n. 地窖，地下室；<br>酒窖 | wine cellar 酒窖<br>storm cellar 防风地窖<br>coal cellar 煤窖<br>cellar door 地窖门 | The wine is stored in the **cellar**. （葡萄酒存放在地窖里。）<br>They hid in the **cellar** during the storm. （暴风雨期间他们躲在地窖里。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：basement（地下室，更高的）· **易混**：cellar（地窖）vs seller（卖家，同音！）vs cellar ≠ cellar（≠ cellar vs cell 细胞）|
| | dual (复现) | /ˈduːəl/ | adj. 双重的，两部分的 | dual purpose 双重目的<br>dual citizenship 双重国籍 | She holds **dual** citizenship. （她拥有双重国籍。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：double · **易混**：dual vs duel（决斗，同音！）|
| | tropic | /ˈtrɑːpɪk/ | n. 回归线；热带<br>adj. 热带的 | the Tropics 热带地区<br>Tropic of Cancer 北回归线<br>Tropic of Capricorn 南回归线<br>tropical adj. 热带的 | The island is located in the **tropics**. （该岛位于热带地区。）<br>The **tropic** sun can be harsh. （热带的太阳可能很毒辣。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：equatorial（赤道的）· **易混**：tropic（回归线/热带）vs tropical（热带的，形容词）vs tropic ≠ tropic（≠ tropic vs topic 话题）|
| | captive | /ˈkæptɪv/ | n. 俘虏，囚徒<br>adj. 被俘虏的；<br>被迷住的 | hold captive 俘虏，拘禁<br>take captive 俘获<br>captive audience 被动的观众<br>captivate v. 迷住 | The soldiers were held **captive** for three months. （士兵们被俘虏了三个月。）<br>She had a **captive** audience at the conference. （她在会议上有一群被迷住的听众。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：prisoner（囚犯）· **反义**：free（自由的）· **易混**：captive（俘虏）vs capture（捕获，动词）vs caption（字幕）|
| | meantime | /ˈmiːntaɪm/ | n. 其间，同时<br>adv. 在此期间 | in the meantime 与此同时<br>for the meantime 暂时 | The train leaves at eight; in the **meantime**, let's get some coffee. （火车八点出发，趁这时间我们去喝杯咖啡吧。）<br>The project will take months; **meantime**, we'll prepare alternatives. （项目需要几个月，同时我们会准备备选方案。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：meanwhile（同时，同义！）· **易混**：meantime（同时）vs meantime ≠ meantime（≠ meantime vs meaning 意思）· meantime = mean + time → 中间的时间|
| | clergy | /ˈklɜːrdʒi/ | n. 神职人员，牧师 | the clergy 神职人员（总称）<br>member of the clergy 神职人员<br>clergyman n. 牧师（男性） | The **clergy** gathered for the ceremony. （神职人员聚集参加仪式。）<br>He has served as a member of the **clergy** for 30 years. （他担任神职人员已有30年。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：priest（牧师）/ minister（牧师）· **反义**：laity（平信徒）· **易混**：clergy（神职人员总称）vs clerk（店员）vs clergy ≠ clergy（≠ clergy vs energy 能量）|
| | tangle | /ˈtæŋɡl/ | v. 使缠绕，纠结<br>n. 乱团，纠结 | tangle with 与…发生纠纷<br>tangled hair 打结的头发<br>in a tangle 混乱不堪 | The fishing lines got **tangled**. （鱼线缠在一起了。）<br>He doesn't want to **tangle** with the law. （他不想和法律有纠葛。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：entangle（纠缠）/ knot（结）· **反义**：untangle（解开）· **易混**：tangle（缠结）vs tango（探戈）vs mangle（毁坏）|
| | consecutive | /kənˈsekjətɪv/ | adj. 连续的，<br>连贯的 | three consecutive days 连续三天<br>consecutive numbers 连续的数字<br>for the third consecutive year 连续第三年 | It rained for five **consecutive** days. （连续下了五天雨。）<br>This is her third **consecutive** victory. （这是她连续第三场胜利。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：successive（连续的）/ in a row（连续）· **易混**：consecutive（连续的）vs conservative（保守的）vs consecutive ≠ consecutive（≠ consecutive vs executive 行政的）|
| | racket | /ˈrækɪt/ | n. 球拍；喧闹；<br>非法勾当 | tennis racket 网球拍<br>make a racket 大声喧闹<br>illegal racket 非法勾当 | He broke his tennis **racket** during the match. （他在比赛中打断了网球拍。）<br>The kids are making such a **racket**! （孩子们吵死人了！） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bat（球棒）/ noise（噪音）· **易混**：racket（球拍/喧闹）vs rocket（火箭）vs bracket（括号）|
| | spade | /speɪd/ | n. 铁锹，铲子；<br>（扑克牌）黑桃 | call a spade a spade 直言不讳<br>spade work 艰苦的准备工作<br>ace of spades 黑桃A | He dug the garden with a **spade**. （他用铁锹挖花园。）<br>I'll **call a spade a spade** — this plan won't work. （我实话实说——这个计划行不通。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：shovel（铲子）· **易混**：spade（铁锹/黑桃）vs spade ≠ spade（≠ spade vs space 空间）· call a spade a spade 是常用习语！|
| | mat | /mæt/ | n. 垫子；地垫；<br>（运动用）垫 | welcome mat 迎宾垫<br>yoga mat 瑜伽垫<br>bath mat 浴室地垫<br>on the mat 被训斥 | Please wipe your feet on the **mat** before entering. （进门前请在地垫上擦脚。）<br>The boss called him **on the mat** for the mistake. （老板因那个错误训斥了他。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：rug（小地毯）/ cushion（垫子）· **易混**：mat（垫子）vs mate（伙伴）vs math（数学）|
| | lash | /læʃ/ | v. 鞭打；猛击；<br>怒斥<br>n. 睫毛；鞭打 | lash out at 猛烈抨击<br>eyelash n. 睫毛<br>lash against 猛烈拍打（海浪等） | The waves **lashed** against the shore. （海浪猛烈拍打海岸。）<br>He **lashed out** at his critics on social media. （他在社交媒体上猛烈抨击批评者。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：whip（鞭打）/ attack（攻击）· **易混**：lash（鞭打/睫毛）vs clash（冲突）vs flash（闪光）|
| | lump | /lʌmp/ | n. 块，团；肿块<br>v. 把…归并在一起 | lump sum 一次性付款<br>a lump of 一块…<br>lump together 把…混为一谈 | He received a **lump** sum payment after retirement. （他退休后收到一笔一次性付款。）<br>Don't **lump** all teenagers **together** — they're all different. （别把青少年一概而论——他们各不相同。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bump（肿块）/ chunk（大块）· **易混**：lump（肿块/块）vs limp（跛行）vs lump ≠ lump（≠ lump vs lamp 灯）|
| | eminent | /ˈemɪnənt/ | adj. 杰出的，<br>卓越的 | eminent scholar 杰出学者<br>eminent position 显赫地位<br>eminent domain 征用权（法律）<br>eminence n. 杰出 | He is an **eminent** scientist in the field of genetics. （他是遗传学领域的杰出科学家。）<br>She holds an **eminent** position in the government. （她在政府中担任要职。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：distinguished（卓越）/ prominent（杰出的）· **反义**：obscure（默默无闻的）· **易混**：eminent（杰出）vs imminent（即将发生的，同音！）vs eminent ≠ eminent（≠ eminent vs eminent domain 征用权）|
| | skate | /skeɪt/ | v. 滑冰，溜冰<br>n. 冰鞋 | ice skate 溜冰鞋<br>roller skate 旱冰鞋<br>skate over 一笔带过，回避<br>skate park 滑板场 | She goes ice **skating** every winter. （她每年冬天都去溜冰。）<br>He tried to **skate** over the difficult question. （他试图回避那个棘手的问题。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：glide（滑行）· **易混**：skate（滑冰）vs skate ≠ skate（≠ skate vs state 状态）vs stake（利害关系）|
| | dispose | /dɪˈspoʊz/ | v. 处理，处置；<br>使倾向于 | dispose of 处理掉，解决<br>dispose sb to/toward 使某人倾向于<br>disposal n. 处理<br>disposable adj. 一次性的 | We need to **dispose** of the waste properly. （我们需要妥善处理废弃物。）<br>His upbringing **disposed** him to be suspicious of strangers. （他的成长经历使他对陌生人持怀疑态度。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：discard（丢弃）/ arrange（安排）· **易混**：dispose（处理/使倾向）vs dispose ≠ dispose（≠ dispose vs expose 暴露）· dispose of 是高频短语！|
| | unify | /ˈjuːnɪfaɪ/ | v. 统一，使一致；<br>联合 | unify the country 统一国家<br>unify standards 统一标准<br>unifying theme 统一主题<br>unification n. 统一 | The treaty aimed to **unify** the two countries. （该条约旨在统一这两个国家。）<br>We need to **unify** our approach to the problem. （我们需要统一处理这个问题的方法。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：unite（联合）/ consolidate（合并）· **反义**：divide（分割）· **易混**：unify（统一）vs unity（团结，名词）vs unify ≠ unify（≠ unify vs uniform 制服/统一的）|
| | sow | /soʊ/ | v. 播种，种下；<br>散布（谣言/仇恨） | sow seeds 播种<br>sow doubt 散布怀疑<br>sow the seeds of 播下…的种子 | Farmers **sow** wheat in the spring. （农民春天播种小麦。）<br>His words **sowed** doubt in her mind. （他的话在她心中种下了怀疑。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：plant（种植）/ spread（散布）· **反义**：reap（收割）· **易混**：sow（播种，/soʊ/）vs sew（缝纫，同音 /soʊ/！）vs sow（母猪，/saʊ/）——一词两音两义！| | cottage | /ˈkɑːtɪdʒ/ | n. 小屋，村舍；<br>（度假）别墅 | country cottage 乡村小屋<br>cottage industry 家庭手工业<br>cottage cheese 农家干酪<br>thatched cottage 茅草屋 | They spent the summer in a cozy **cottage** by the lake. （他们在湖边一间舒适的小屋里度过了夏天。）<br>She dreams of owning a little **cottage** in the countryside. （她梦想在乡村拥有一间小农舍。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cabin（小木屋）/ hut（简陋小屋）· **易混**：cottage（小屋）vs cottage ≠ cottage（≠ cottage vs college 大学）· 注意两个 t！|
| | screw | /skruː/ | n. 螺丝钉；螺旋<br>v. 拧紧；糟蹋 | screw up 弄糟，搞砸<br>screw driver 螺丝刀<br>tighten the screw 拧紧螺丝<br>have a screw loose 神经不正常 | I really **screwed** up the presentation. （我把演示彻底搞砸了。）<br>He used a **screw**driver to tighten the loose **screw**. （他用螺丝刀拧紧了松动的螺丝。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bolt（螺栓）/ ruin（搞砸）· **易混**：screw（螺丝/搞砸）vs crew（船员）vs sew（缝纫，发音不同！）|
| | proposition | /ˌprɑːpəˈzɪʃn/ | n. 主张，提议；<br>命题；事情 | business proposition 商业提议<br>attractive proposition 有吸引力的建议<br>value proposition 价值主张<br>propose v. 提议 | It's a risky **proposition**, but worth considering. （这是一个有风险的主张，但值得考虑。）<br>His **proposition** was rejected by the board. （他的提议被董事会否决了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：proposal（提议）/ suggestion（建议）· **易混**：proposition（主张/命题）vs proposal（提议/求婚）vs preposition（介词，注意拼写！）|
| | precede | /prɪˈsiːd/ | v. 在…之前发生；<br>走在…前面 | be preceded by 在…之前<br>preceding section 前一章节<br>precedent n. 先例<br>precedence n. 优先 | A brief introduction **preceded** the main speech. （主题演讲前有一段简短的介绍。）<br>The days that **preceded** the war were full of tension. （战争前的日子充满紧张气氛。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：come before（在…之前）/ lead（领先）· **反义**：follow（跟随）/ succeed（继…之后）· **易混**：precede（先于）vs proceed（继续进行，拼写极像！）vs precede ≠ precede（≠ precede vs recede 后退）|
| | sorrow | /ˈsɑːroʊ/ | n. 悲伤，悲痛；<br>不幸 | deep sorrow 深深的悲伤<br>to one's sorrow 令人悲伤的是<br>drown one's sorrows 借酒浇愁<br>sorrowful adj. 悲伤的 | She couldn't hide her **sorrow** at the news. （她无法掩饰听到消息后的悲伤。）<br>The whole nation shared the **sorrow** of the tragedy. （举国上下共同感受到了这场悲剧的悲痛。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：grief（悲伤）/ sadness（难过）· **反义**：joy（快乐）· **易混**：sorrow（悲伤）vs borrow（借）vs sorrow ≠ sorrow（≠ sorrow vs morrow 明天，古语）|
| | shipment | /ˈʃɪpmənt/ | n. 装运；运输的货物 | bulk shipment 大宗货物运输<br>awaiting shipment 等待发货<br>shipment date 发货日期<br>ship v. 运送 | The first **shipment** of aid arrived yesterday. （第一批援助物资昨天到达了。）<br>We are expecting a **shipment** of new products next week. （下周我们期待一批新产品的到货。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：delivery（递送）/ cargo（货物）· **易混**：shipment（装运）vs equipment（设备）vs shipment ≠ shipment（≠ shipment vs ship 船）|
| | mock | /mɑːk/ | v. 嘲笑，嘲弄；<br>模仿<br>adj. 模拟的，假的 | mock at sb 嘲笑某人<br>mock exam 模拟考试<br>mock trial 模拟法庭<br>mock interview 模拟面试 | Don't **mock** her — she's trying her best. （别嘲笑她——她已经尽力了。）<br>We had a **mock** interview before the real one. （我们在正式面试前做了一次模拟面试。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ridicule（嘲笑）/ imitate（模仿）· **反义**：praise（赞扬）· **易混**：mock（嘲笑/模拟）vs mock ≠ mock（≠ mock vs lock 锁）vs mock ≠ mock（≠ mock vs mop 拖把）|
| | envisage | /ɪnˈvɪzɪdʒ/ | v. 设想，想象；<br>展望 | envisage doing sth 设想做某事<br>envisage the future 展望未来<br>envisage the possibility 预见到可能性<br>envisage a plan 设想一个计划 | She **envisaged** a future where everyone has access to education. （她设想了一个人人都能接受教育的未来。）<br>The plan **envisages** the construction of 10,000 new homes. （该计划设想建造一万套新住房。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：imagine（想象）/ foresee（预见）· **易混**：envisage（设想）vs envision（设想，同义，美式更常用）vs envisage ≠ envisage（≠ envisage vs engage 参与）|
| | drawback (第2次) | /ˈdrɔːbæk/ | n. 缺点，弊端 | major drawback 主要缺点<br>the only drawback 唯一的缺点 | The only **drawback** is the cost. （唯一的缺点是成本。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：disadvantage · **反义**：advantage · **易混**：drawback vs setback（挫折）|
| | allegiance (第2次) | /əˈliːdʒəns/ | n. 忠诚，效忠 | pledge allegiance to 宣誓效忠<br>switch allegiance 改变立场 | The soldiers pledged **allegiance** to their country. （士兵们宣誓效忠国家。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：loyalty · **易混**：allegiance vs alliance（联盟）|
| | booth (第2次) | /buːθ/ | n. 小隔间；摊位 | phone booth 电话亭<br>ticket booth 售票亭 | I called her from a phone **booth**. （我从电话亭给她打了电话。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stall/kiosk · **易混**：booth vs boot（靴子）|
| | porcelain (第2次) | /ˈpɔːrsəlɪn/ | n. 瓷器 | fine porcelain 精美瓷器<br>porcelain vase 瓷花瓶 | The museum has a collection of Chinese **porcelain**. （博物馆有中国瓷器收藏。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：china · **易混**：porcelain ≠ procelain（拼写坑！）|
| | nuisance (第2次) | /ˈnuːsns/ | n. 讨厌的人/事 | what a nuisance 真烦人<br>public nuisance 妨害公共秩序 | The noise is a real **nuisance**. （噪音真烦人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：annoyance · **易混**：nuisance vs nonsense（胡说）|
| | thrift (第2次) | /θrɪft/ | n. 节俭，节约 | thrift store 二手店<br>thrifty adj. 节俭的 | **Thrift** is a virtue. （节俭是美德。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：frugality · **反义**：waste · **易混**：thrift vs thrive（繁荣）|
| | dispute (复现) | /dɪˈspjuːt/ | n./v. 争端，争论 | territory dispute 领土争端<br>beyond dispute 无可争议 | The two countries are in **dispute** over fishing rights. （两国就渔业权存在争端。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：controversy/debate · **易混**：dispute vs repute（名声）|
| | repel (第2次) | /rɪˈpel/ | v. 击退；使厌恶 | repel an attack 击退进攻<br>be repelled by 被…恶心到 | The smell **repelled** everyone. （那味道让所有人都恶心。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：drive back/disgust · **反义**：attract · **易混**：repel vs compel（强迫）|
| | plunge (复现) | /plʌndʒ/ | v./n. 暴跌；跳水；投入 | take the plunge 冒险一试<br>plunge into 陷入 | Stock prices **plunged** after the news. （消息后股价暴跌。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：dive/plummet · **反义**：soar · **易混**：plunge vs plunder（掠夺）|
| | indignant (复现) | /ɪnˈdɪɡnənt/ | adj. 愤愤不平的 | be indignant at 对…愤慨 | She was **indignant** at the unfair treatment. （她因不公对待而愤慨。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：angry/outraged · **易混**：indignant vs indigenous（本土的）|
| | toil (第2次) | /tɔɪl/ | v./n. 辛苦工作 | toil away 埋头苦干 | They **toiled** in the fields. （他们在田间辛苦劳作。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：labor · **反义**：leisure · **易混**：toil vs soil（土壤）|
| | tow (第2次) | /toʊ/ | v./n. 拖，牵引 | tow truck 拖车<br>in tow 紧随在后 | The car was **towed** away. （车被拖走了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：drag/haul · **易混**：tow vs toe（脚趾，同音！）|
| | overhear (第2次) | /ˌoʊvərˈhɪr/ | v. 无意中听到 | overhear a conversation 无意听到对话 | I **overheard** them talking about the surprise party. （我无意中听到他们在谈惊喜派对。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：无意听到 · **易混**：overhear vs eavesdrop（故意偷听！）|
| | burglar (第2次) | /ˈbɜːrɡlər/ | n. 入室窃贼 | burglar alarm 防盗报警器<br>burglary n. 入室盗窃 | The **burglar** broke in through the window. （窃贼破窗而入。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：burglar（入室）vs thief（泛指小偷）vs robber（持械抢劫）|
| | escort (第2次) | /ˈeskɔːrt/ | n./v. 护送 | police escort 警察护送<br>escort sb to 护送某人到 | He **escorted** her to the door. （他护送她到门口。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：accompany/guard · **易混**：escort vs escape（逃跑）|
| | skull (第2次) | /skʌl/ | n. 头骨 | skull and crossbones ☠️ | The X-ray showed a crack in his **skull**. （X光显示头骨有裂痕。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：skull vs skill（技能）|
| | recollect | /ˌrekəˈlekt/ | v. 记起，回忆起；<br>重新收集 | recollect doing sth 记得做过某事<br>difficult to recollect 难以回忆起<br>recollection n. 回忆<br>re-collect v. 重新收集 | I can't **recollect** where I put my keys. （我想不起来把钥匙放哪了。）<br>She tried to **recollect** the details of the accident. （她努力回忆事故的细节。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：remember（记得）/ recall（回想起）· **反义**：forget（忘记）· **易混**：recollect（回忆）vs recollect ≠ recollect（≠ recollect vs collect 收集）· re + collect = 重新收集记忆|
| | stipulate (第2次) | /ˈstɪpjuleɪt/ | v. 规定，明确要求 | stipulate that 规定…<br>as stipulated in 按照…规定 | The contract **stipulates** that payment must be made within 30 days. （合同规定30天内付款。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：specify/prescribe · **易混**：stipulate vs stimulate（刺激）vs simulate（模拟）|
| | cohesive (第2次) | /koʊˈhiːsɪv/ | adj. 有凝聚力的；连贯的 | cohesive group 有凝聚力的团体<br>cohesive structure 连贯的结构 | The team needs to be more **cohesive**. （团队需要更有凝聚力。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：united · **易混**：cohesive vs coherent（连贯的）|
| | exaggerate (第2次) | /ɪɡˈzædʒəreɪt/ | v. 夸张，夸大 | exaggerate the truth 夸大事实<br>exaggeration n. 夸张 | He tends to **exaggerate** his achievements. （他倾向于夸大成就。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：overstate · **反义**：understate · **易混**：exaggerate vs aggregate（聚集）|
| | palm | /pɑːm/ | n. 手掌；棕榈树<br>v. 把…藏在手心 | palm tree 棕榈树<br>read one's palm 看手相<br>in the palm of one's hand 在某人掌控中<br>palm off 用假货骗人 | She held the coin in the **palm** of her hand. （她把硬币握在手掌中。）<br>The beach was lined with **palm** trees. （海滩上种满了棕榈树。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hand（手）/ coconut tree（椰子树）· **易混**：palm（手掌/棕榈）vs palm ≠ palm（≠ palm vs calm 平静）· 一词两义差距巨大！|
| | liner | /ˈlaɪnər/ | n. 邮轮；班机；<br>衬里 | ocean liner 远洋邮轮<br>eyeliner 眼线笔<br>bin liner 垃圾袋衬里<br>cruise liner 游轮 | The **liner** set sail from Southampton. （邮轮从南安普顿起航。）<br>She applied **eyeliner** before the party. （派对前她画了眼线。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cruise ship（游轮）/ vessel（船只）· **易混**：liner（邮轮/衬里）vs linear（线性的）vs liner ≠ liner（≠ liner vs line 线）|
| | preclude | /prɪˈkluːd/ | v. 阻止，排除；<br>使不可能 | preclude sb from doing 阻止某人做<br>preclude the possibility 排除可能性<br>preclusive adj. 排除性的 | His injury **precluded** him from playing in the final. （他的伤使他无法参加决赛。）<br>The agreement doesn't **preclude** further negotiations. （该协议并不排除进一步谈判的可能。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：prevent（阻止）/ rule out（排除）· **易混**：preclude（排除）vs precede（先于）vs include（包含）· pre + clude(关闭) = 提前关闭 → 排除|
| | soluble | /ˈsɑːljəbl/ | adj. 可溶解的；<br>可解决的 | water-soluble 水溶性的<br>soluble fiber 可溶性纤维<br>soluble problem 可解决的问题<br>solubility n. 溶解度 | Sugar is **soluble** in water. （糖可溶于水。）<br>The problem is difficult but **soluble**. （这个问题很难但是可以解决的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：dissolvable（可溶解的）/ solvable（可解决的）· **反义**：insoluble（不溶的/无法解决的）· **易混**：soluble（可溶/可解）vs solvable（可解决的，强调数学/逻辑）|
| | furnish | /ˈfɜːrnɪʃ/ | v. 提供，供应；<br>布置家具 | furnish sb with 向某人提供<br>furnish a room 布置房间<br>furnished apartment 带家具的公寓<br>furniture n. 家具 | The company **furnished** us with all the necessary information. （公司向我们提供了所有必要信息。）<br>They **furnished** the living room with a new sofa. （他们给客厅添了一张新沙发。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：provide（提供）/ equip（配备）· **易混**：furnish（提供/布置）vs furniture（家具，名词）vs finish（完成）|
| | coordinate | /koʊˈɔːrdɪneɪt/ | v. 协调，配合<br>n. 坐标<br>adj. 同等的 | coordinate with 与…协调<br>GPS coordinates GPS坐标<br>coordinate efforts 协调努力<br>coordination n. 协调 | We need to **coordinate** our schedules for the meeting. （我们需要协调日程开个会。）<br>The pilot checked the map **coordinates**. （飞行员核对了地图坐标。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：synchronize（同步）/ organize（组织）· **易混**：coordinate（协调/坐标）vs cooperate（合作）vs subordinate（下属）|
| | analogue | /ˈænəlɔːɡ/ | n. 类似物，对应物<br>adj. 模拟的 | digital and analogue 数字与模拟<br>analogue clock 指针式时钟<br>analogue signal 模拟信号<br>analogy n. 类比 | Vinyl records produce an **analogue** sound. （黑胶唱片产生的是模拟声音。）<br>This drug is a synthetic **analogue** of a natural hormone. （这种药是天然激素的合成类似物。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：equivalent（等价物）/ similar（类似物）· **反义**：digital（数字的）· **易混**：analogue（类似/模拟）vs analogy（类比）vs analogue ≠ analogue（美式拼写：analog）|

| | aspire | /əˈspaɪər/ | v. 渴望，追求；<br>有志于 | aspire to 渴望，有志于<br>aspire to do 渴望做某事<br>aspiration n. 抱负<br>aspiring adj. 有抱负的 | She **aspires** to become a doctor. （她渴望成为一名医生。）<br>Young people should **aspire** to greatness. （年轻人应该追求卓越。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：desire（渴望）/ strive for（奋斗）· **易混**：aspire（渴望）vs inspire（激励）vs expire（到期）——spire 家族！|

| | militant | /ˈmɪlɪtənt/ | adj. 激进的，好斗的<br>n. 激进分子 | militant group 激进组织<br>militant attitude 好斗的态度<br>militant movement 激进运动<br>militancy n. 好战，激进 | The **militant** group claimed responsibility for the attack. （激进组织声称对袭击负责。）<br>His **militant** views alienated many of his supporters. （他的激进观点疏远了许多支持者。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：aggressive（好斗的）/ radical（急进的）· **反义**：moderate（温和的）/ pacifist（和平主义）· **易混**：militant（激进的）vs military（军事的）vs militia（民兵）· 词根：milit- 战斗|

| 2026-06-19 | turnover (第2次) | /ˈtɜːrnoʊvər/ | n. 营业额；人员流动率；翻面 | high turnover 高流动率<br>staff turnover 员工流失率<br>annual turnover 年营业额 | The company has a high staff **turnover**. （公司员工流失率很高。）<br>Annual **turnover** exceeded 10 million. （年营业额超过一千万。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：revenue（收入）/ rotation（轮换）· **易混**：turnover vs turn over（翻转，动词短语）vs takeover（接管）|

| | cordial | /ˈkɔːrdʒəl/ | adj. 热情友好的，热诚的 | cordial welcome 热情欢迎<br>cordial relations 友好关系<br>cordial atmosphere 亲切氛围<br>cordially adv. 热情地 | We received a **cordial** welcome from the host. （我们受到了主人的热情欢迎。）<br>The two leaders had a **cordial** meeting. （两位领导人进行了友好的会晤。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：warm（温暖的/热情的）/ friendly（友好的）/ heartfelt（衷心的）· **反义**：cold（冷淡的）/ hostile（敌对的）· **易混**：cordial（热情的）vs cardial（心脏的，形近）vs cord（绳子）|
| | fuss | /fʌs/ | n./v. 大惊小怪，小题大做 | make a fuss 大惊小怪<br>fuss over/about 为…过分操心<br>without fuss 不声张地<br>fussy adj. 挑剔的 | Don't make such a **fuss** about it — it's nothing serious. （别这么大惊小怪——没什么大不了的。）<br>She **fussed** over the details for hours. （她对细节操心了几个小时。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：commotion（骚动）/ bother（烦扰）· **反义**：calm（冷静）· **易混**：fuss（大惊小怪）vs fuse（保险丝/融合）vs fussy（挑剔的）|
| | hypocrisy (第2次) | /hɪˈpɑːkrəsi/ | n. 虚伪，伪善 | pure hypocrisy 纯粹虚伪<br>expose hypocrisy 揭露虚伪<br>hypocrite n. 伪君子 | It's **hypocrisy** to say you care but never help. （说关心却从不帮忙就是虚伪。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：insincerity · **反义**：sincerity · **易混**：hypocrisy vs hypothesis（假设）|

| | conscience | /ˈkɑːnʃəns/ | n. 良心，良知 | guilty conscience 内疚<br>clear conscience 问心无愧 | His **conscience** wouldn't let him lie. （他的良心不允许他撒谎。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：conscience vs conscious（有意识的）vs conscientious（尽责的，已在⚡）|

| | relish | /ˈrelɪʃ/ | v. 享受，喜爱<br>n. 享受；调味酱 | relish the challenge 喜欢挑战<br>relish the thought 喜欢这个想法 | She **relished** the opportunity to travel. （她很喜欢旅行机会。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：relish vs relish ≠ relish（≠ relish vs release 释放）|
| | eminent | /ˈemɪnənt/ | adj. 杰出的，卓越的 | eminent scientist 杰出科学家<br>eminence n. 卓越 | She is an **eminent** scholar in this field. （她是该领域杰出学者。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：eminent vs imminent（迫在眉睫的，已在⚡）vs immanent（内在的）|

| | invasion | /ɪnˈveɪʒn/ | n. 入侵，侵略；涌入 | military invasion 军事入侵<br>privacy invasion 侵犯隐私 | The country faced an **invasion** from its neighbor. （该国面临邻国的入侵。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：attack（攻击）/ intrusion（闯入）· **易混**：invasion vs evasion（逃避）|

| | inn | /ɪn/ | n. 小旅馆，客栈 | country inn 乡村客栈<br>holiday inn 假日酒店 | We stayed at a cozy **inn** in the countryside. （我们住在乡下一家舒适的小客栈。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：inn vs in（里面，同音！）|
| | staircase | /ˈsterkeɪs/ | n. 楼梯（连续的） | spiral staircase 螺旋楼梯 | The old **staircase** creaked with every step. （旧楼梯每走一步都嘎吱作响。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stairs（楼梯）· **易混**：staircase vs stairwell（楼梯井）|
| | fellowship | /ˈfeloʊʃɪp/ | n. 友谊，伙伴关系；奖学金 | research fellowship 研究奖学金<br>sense of fellowship 同伴情谊 | He was awarded a **fellowship** at Harvard. （他获得了哈佛的奖学金。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：companionship（陪伴）/ scholarship（奖学金）|
| | jargon | /ˈdʒɑːrɡən/ | n. 行话，专业术语 | legal jargon 法律术语<br>technical jargon 技术术语 | The report is full of technical **jargon**. （报告全是专业术语。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：terminology（术语）/ slang（俚语）|
| | deduce | /dɪˈduːs/ | v. 推断，演绎 | deduce from 从…推断 | We can **deduce** from the evidence that he was at the scene. （我们从证据推断他在现场。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：infer（推理）/ conclude（得出结论）· **反义**：induce（归纳）· **易混**：deduce vs reduce（减少）|
| | remnant | /ˈremnənt/ | n. 残余，剩余；零头 | remnant of the past 过去遗迹<br>remnant fabric 零头布 | Only a **remnant** of the ancient wall remains. （只剩下一段古城墙的残迹。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：remainder（剩余）/ residue（残留物）|
| | graze | /ɡreɪz/ | v. 放牧；擦伤；擦过 | cattle graze 放牛<br>graze one's knee 擦伤膝盖 | Sheep were **grazing** on the hillside. （羊在山坡上吃草。）<br>The bullet **grazed** his shoulder. （子弹擦伤了他的肩膀。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：pasture（放牧）/ scrape（擦伤）· **易混**：graze vs gaze（凝视）vs grace（优雅）|
| | clip | /klɪp/ | n. 夹子；片段<br>v. 夹住；修剪 | paper clip 回形针<br>video clip 视频片段<br>clip one's nails 剪指甲 | She attached the papers with a **clip**. （她用夹子把文件夹在一起。）<br>I saw a funny **clip** on social media. （我看到一个搞笑片段。） <br><br>🔍 **易混**：clip vs clap（鼓掌）|
| | sanction | /ˈsæŋkʃn/ | n. 制裁；批准<br>v. 制裁；批准 | economic sanctions 经济制裁<br>impose sanctions 实施制裁 | The UN imposed **sanctions** on the regime. （联合国对该政权实施制裁。）<br>The plan needs the board's **sanction**. （该计划需要董事会批准。） <br><br>🔍 **一词两义**：制裁（常用复数）vs 批准|
| | sketch | /sketʃ/ | n. 素描；概述<br>v. 画素描；简述 | sketch map 示意图<br>sketch out 简述 | She drew a quick **sketch** of the landscape. （她快速画了一幅风景素描。）<br>Let me **sketch** out the plan. （我简单说一下计划。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：drawing（绘画）/ outline（概述）· **易混**：sketch vs stretch（伸展）|
| | intermittent | /ˌɪntərˈmɪtənt/ | adj. 间歇的，断断续续的 | intermittent rain 阵雨<br>intermittent signal 间歇信号 | The **intermittent** rain ruined the picnic. （断断续续的雨毁了野餐。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：sporadic（零星的）· **反义**：continuous（连续的）· **易混**：intermittent vs intermediate（中间的）|
| | violate | /ˈvaɪəleɪt/ | v. 违反，侵犯 | violate the law 违法<br>violate privacy 侵犯隐私 | His actions **violated** the terms of the contract. （他的行为违反了合同条款。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：break（打破）/ infringe（侵犯）· **反义**：obey（遵守）· **易混**：violate vs violent（暴力的）|
| | constitutional | /ˌkɑːnstɪˈtuːʃənl/ | adj. 宪法的；体质上的 | constitutional right 宪法权利<br>constitutional monarchy 君主立宪制 | The court ruled the law **unconstitutional**. （法院裁定该法律违宪。） <br><br>🔍 **反义**：unconstitutional（违宪的）|
| | skyscraper | /ˈskaɪskreɪpər/ | n. 摩天大楼 | towering skyscraper 高耸的摩天大楼 | The city is famous for its **skyscrapers**. （这座城市以摩天大楼闻名。） <br><br>🔍 词源：sky + scraper|
| | allege | /əˈledʒ/ | v. 声称，指控（未经证实） | be alleged to have done 被指控做了 | He **alleges** that the company misled investors. （他声称公司误导了投资者。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：claim（声称）/ assert（断言）· **易混**：allege vs allergy（过敏）|

| | acute | /əˈkjuːt/ | adj. 急性的；敏锐的；严重的 | acute pain 剧痛<br>acute shortage 严重短缺 | She felt an **acute** pain in her abdomen. （她感到腹部剧痛。）<br>He has an **acute** sense of hearing. （他有敏锐的听觉。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：severe（严重的）/ sharp（尖锐的）· **反义**：chronic（慢性的）/ dull（迟钝的）· **易混**：acute vs cute（可爱的）|

| | accountant | /əˈkaʊntənt/ | n. 会计师，会计 | chartered accountant 注册会计师<br>certified public accountant 注册会计师(CPA) | She works as an **accountant** at a large firm. （她在一家大公司做会计。）<br>The **accountant** prepared the tax return. （会计准备了纳税申报表。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：accountant vs account（账户/叙述）vs accountable（应负责的）|
| | interim | /ˈɪntərɪm/ | adj. 临时的，过渡期间的<br>n. 过渡时期 | interim report 中期报告<br>interim government 临时政府<br>in the interim 在此期间 | An **interim** government was formed after the election. （选举后成立了临时政府。）<br>**In the interim**, please continue with your work. （在此期间请继续工作。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：temporary（暂时的）/ provisional（临时的）· **反义**：permanent（永久的）· **易混**：interim vs interim ≠ interior（内部）|
| | antique | /ænˈtiːk/ | n. 古董，古玩<br>adj. 古老的，古董的 | antique shop 古董店<br>antique furniture 古董家具<br>antique collector 古董收藏家 | He collects **antique** clocks. （他收藏古董钟表。）<br>This vase is a valuable **antique**. （这个花瓶是珍贵的古董。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：ancient（古代的）/ vintage（老式的）· **易混**：antique vs unique（独特的）|
| | queer | /kwɪr/ | adj. 奇怪的，古怪的；<br>酷儿的（性少数群体） | a queer feeling 奇怪的感觉<br>queer behaviour 古怪行为 | There was something **queer** about the whole situation. （整个情况有些古怪。）<br>He gave me a **queer** look. （他奇怪地看了我一眼。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：strange（奇怪的）/ odd（古怪的）/ peculiar（奇异的）· **易混**：queer vs queue（排队）vs queen（女王）|
| | boycott | /ˈbɔɪkɑːt/ | v./n. 抵制，排斥；拒绝购买 | boycott a product 抵制产品<br>call for a boycott 呼吁抵制<br>trade boycott 贸易抵制 | Consumers **boycotted** the brand over its labor practices. （消费者因劳工问题抵制该品牌。）<br>The **boycott** cost the company millions. （抵制行动让公司损失了数百万。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：protest（抗议）/ ban（禁止）· **反义**：support（支持）/ endorse（认可）· **词源**：来自人名 Boycott，19世纪爱尔兰土地代理，因受抵制而闻名|
| | pillar | /ˈpɪlər/ | n. 柱子，支柱；<br>栋梁，骨干 | pillar of society 社会栋梁<br>a pillar of …的支柱<br>stone pillar 石柱 | Education is a **pillar** of modern society. （教育是现代社会的支柱。）<br>She has been a **pillar** of strength for her family. （她一直是家庭的力量支柱。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：column（柱子）/ foundation（根基）· **易混**：pillar vs pillow（枕头）vs pill（药丸）|
| | futile | /ˈfjuːtl/ | adj. 徒劳的，无用的 | futile attempt 徒劳的尝试<br>futile effort 白费力<br>it is futile to do 做…是徒劳的 | It is **futile** to argue with him — he never changes his mind. （和他争论是徒劳的——他从不改变主意。）<br>Rescue efforts proved **futile**. （救援努力被证明是徒劳的。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：useless（无用的）/ vain（徒劳的）/ pointless（无意义的）· **反义**：fruitful（有成果的）/ effective（有效的）· **易混**：futile vs feudal（封建的）vs fertile（肥沃的）|
| | tumble | /ˈtʌmbl/ | v. 跌倒，摔倒；暴跌<br>n. 跌倒，暴跌 | tumble down 倒塌<br>tumble to 突然明白<br>take a tumble 摔一跤<br>stock market tumble 股市暴跌 | He **tumbled** down the stairs. （他从楼梯上摔了下来。）<br>Share prices **tumbled** on the news. （消息一出股价暴跌。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：fall（跌倒）/ plunge（暴跌）/ stumble（绊倒）· **易混**：tumble vs rumble（隆隆声）vs humble（谦逊的）|
| | bulb | /bʌlb/ | n. 灯泡；球茎（植物） | light bulb 灯泡<br>flower bulb 花球茎<br>bulb garlic 蒜头 | The **light bulb** needs replacing. （灯泡需要换了。）<br>Tulips grow from **bulbs**. （郁金香从球茎长出来。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：lamp（灯）· **易混**：bulb vs bulk（大量）vs bull（公牛）|
| | lawn | /lɔːn/ | n. 草坪，草地 | mow the lawn 修剪草坪<br>front/back lawn 前/后院草坪<br>lawn chair 草坪躺椅 | He spent the afternoon mowing the **lawn**. （他花了一下午修剪草坪。）<br>The kids are playing on the **lawn**. （孩子们在草坪上玩。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：grass（草地）/ meadow（牧场）· **易混**：lawn vs loan（贷款）vs dawn（黎明）|
| | appoint | /əˈpɔɪnt/ | v. 任命，委派；约定 | appoint sb as/to 任命某人为…<br>appoint a date 约定日期<br>well-appointed 设备齐全的 | She was **appointed** CEO of the company. （她被任命为公司CEO。）<br>We need to **appoint** a time for the next meeting. （我们需要约定下次会议的时间。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：designate（指定）/ assign（分派）· **反义**：dismiss（解雇）· **易混**：appoint vs disappoint（使失望）vs appoint ≠ appointment（约定/任命，名词）|
| | conspiracy | /kənˈspɪrəsi/ | n. 阴谋，共谋 | conspiracy theory 阴谋论<br>conspiracy to murder 共谋谋杀<br>uncover a conspiracy 揭露阴谋 | He was convicted of **conspiracy** to commit fraud. （他被判共谋诈骗罪。）<br>Many people believe in **conspiracy** theories. （很多人相信阴谋论。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：plot（密谋）/ scheme（阴谋）· **易混**：conspiracy vs conspiracy ≠ conspiracy（注意拼写 con+spir+acy）· conspire（动词，密谋）|
| | execute | /ˈeksɪkjuːt/ | v. 处决；执行，实施 | execute a plan 执行计划<br>execute a will 执行遗嘱<br>be executed for 因…被处决 | The plan was **executed** perfectly. （计划执行得非常完美。）<br>He was **executed** for treason. （他因叛国罪被处决。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：carry out（执行）/ put to death（处死）· **易混**：execute vs executive（行政的/高管）vs execution（执行/处决，名词）|
| | studio | /ˈstuːdioʊ/ | n. 工作室；录音室；<br>电影制片厂 | art studio 画室<br>recording studio 录音棚<br>film studio 电影制片厂<br>dance studio 舞蹈室 | The artist works in a small **studio**. （艺术家在一个小工作室里工作。）<br>The band spent weeks in the **studio**. （乐队在录音棚里待了好几周。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：workshop（工作坊）/ atelier（画室）· **易混**：studio vs study（书房/学习）|
| | hitherto | /ˌhɪðərˈtuː/ | adv. 迄今，至今（正式） | hitherto unknown 此前未知的<br>hitherto unpublished 此前未发表的 | The discovery revealed a **hitherto** unknown species. （这个发现揭示了一个此前未知的物种。）<br>**Hitherto**, no one had challenged the theory. （迄今为止，没有人挑战过这个理论。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：so far（至今）/ previously（此前）/ up to now（到现在）· **易混**：hitherto vs hereto（至此，法律用语）|
| | trolley | /ˈtrɑːli/ | n. 手推车；有轨电车<br>（英式） | shopping trolley 购物车<br>trolley bus 无轨电车<br>supermarket trolley 超市手推车 | She pushed the **trolley** down the aisle. （她推着购物车沿着过道走。）<br>The city still operates old **trolley** buses. （这座城市仍在运营老式无轨电车。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：cart（手推车，美式）/ tram（有轨电车）· **易混**：trolley vs trolly（同义变体拼写）vs trolley ≠ trolley（≠ trolley vs jolly 快乐的）|
| | speciality | /ˌspeʃiˈæləti/ | n. 专长，特长；<br>特色菜，特产 | local speciality 当地特产<br>house speciality 餐厅招牌菜<br>speciality coffee 精品咖啡 | Seafood is the **speciality** of this region. （海鲜是这个地区的特产。）<br>Her **speciality** is international law. （她的专长是国际法。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：specialty（美式拼写，同义）/ expertise（专长）/ forte（特长）· **易混**：speciality vs specialty（英式 vs 美式拼写）vs special（特别的）|
| | puppet | /ˈpʌpɪt/ | n. 木偶；傀儡 | puppet show 木偶戏<br>puppet government 傀儡政府<br>puppet master 幕后操纵者<br>hand puppet 手偶 | The children enjoyed the **puppet** show. （孩子们很喜欢木偶戏。）<br>He was just a **puppet** — the real power lay elsewhere. （他只是一个傀儡——实权在别处。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：marionette（牵线木偶）/ figurehead（傀儡领导）· **易混**：puppet vs puppy（小狗）vs puppet ≠ puppet（≠ puppet vs poppet 宝贝）|
| | advent | /ˈædvent/ | n. 来临，到来，出现 | the advent of …的到来<br>with the advent of 随着…的到来 | The **advent** of the internet changed everything. （互联网的到来改变了一切。）<br>With the **advent** of winter, the days grew shorter. （随着冬天的到来，白天变短了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：arrival（到来）/ emergence（出现）/ dawn（开端）· **反义**：departure（离开）/ end（结束）· **易混**：advent vs adventure（冒险）vs advent ≠ Advent（大写指基督降临节）|

| | boot | /buːt/ | n. 靴子；<br>（汽车）后备箱（英）<br>v. 启动（电脑） | a pair of boots 一双靴子<br>boot up 启动（电脑）<br>give sb the boot 解雇某人 | He put on his **boots** and went out in the snow. （他穿上靴子走进雪地里。）<br>The computer is **booting** up. （电脑正在启动。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：shoe（鞋）· **易混**：boot vs booth（小隔间）vs boost（推动）|
| | abolish | /əˈbɑːlɪʃ/ | v. 废除，废止（法律/制度等） | abolish slavery 废除奴隶制<br>abolish the death penalty 废除死刑<br>abolish a law 废除一项法律 | Many countries have **abolished** the death penalty. （许多国家已废除死刑。）<br>The tax was **abolished** last year. （这项税收去年被废除了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：eliminate（消除）/ do away with（废除）· **反义**：establish（建立）/ institute（设立）· **易混**：abolish vs demolish（拆除建筑物）|
| | veto | /ˈviːtoʊ/ | n./v. 否决；否决权 | exercise the veto 行使否决权<br>veto a bill 否决法案<br>power of veto 否决权 | The president **vetoed** the bill. （总统否决了该法案。）<br>China has a **veto** power in the UN Security Council. （中国在联合国安理会拥有否决权。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：reject（拒绝）/ overrule（驳回）· **反义**：approve（批准）/ ratify（批准）· **易混**：veto vs vote（投票）|
| | latent | /ˈleɪtnt/ | adj. 潜在的，潜伏的 | latent talent 潜在的才能<br>latent disease 潜伏性疾病<br>latent period 潜伏期<br>latent heat 潜热 | He discovered his **latent** talent for music. （他发现了自己潜在的音乐才能。）<br>The virus can remain **latent** for years. （病毒可以潜伏多年。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：dormant（休眠的）/ hidden（隐藏的）/ potential（潜在的）· **反义**：manifest（明显的）/ active（活跃的）· **易混**：latent vs patent（专利/明显的）vs latent ≠ latent（≠ latent vs lateral 侧面的）|
| | insult | /ɪnˈsʌlt/ v.<br>/ˈɪnsʌlt/ n. | v./n. 侮辱，冒犯 | insult sb's intelligence 侮辱某人的智商<br>hurl insults at 对…破口大骂<br>add insult to injury 雪上加霜 | He felt **insulted** by her remarks. （他觉得被她的话侮辱了。）<br>To add **insult** to injury, they fired him on his birthday. （雪上加霜的是，他们在他的生日那天解雇了他。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：offend（冒犯）/ humiliate（羞辱）· **反义**：compliment（赞美）/ praise（表扬）· **易混**：insult vs consult（咨询）vs result（结果）|
| | accuse | /əˈkjuːz/ | v. 指控，指责 | accuse sb of (doing) sth 指控某人做某事<br>be accused of 被指控<br>stand accused of 被指控 | He was **accused** of stealing the money. （他被指控偷了钱。）<br>She **accused** him of lying. （她指责他说谎。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：charge（指控，charge sb with）/ blame（责备）· **反义**：defend（辩护）/ exonerate（免除指控）· **易混**：accuse vs excuse（原谅）vs abuse（滥用/虐待）|
| | owing to | /ˈoʊɪŋ tuː/ | prep. 因为，由于 | owing to bad weather 由于天气不好<br>owing to the fact that 由于…的事实 | The flight was delayed **owing to** bad weather. （航班因恶劣天气延误。）<br>**Owing to** a lack of funds, the project was canceled. （由于缺乏资金，项目被取消了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：due to（由于）/ because of（因为）/ on account of（由于）· **易混**：owing to vs due to（同义，due to 更常用于be动词后）· ⚠️ owing to 后接名词/动名词|
| | deteriorate | /dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/ | v. 恶化，变坏；退化 | deteriorate into 恶化为<br>conditions deteriorate 情况恶化<br>deterioration n. 恶化 | His health began to **deteriorate** rapidly. （他的健康状况开始迅速恶化。）<br>The situation has **deteriorated** into chaos. （局势已恶化成混乱。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：worsen（恶化）/ decline（衰退）/ degenerate（退化）· **反义**：improve（改善）/ recover（恢复）· **易混**：deteriorate vs deteriorate ≠ deteriorate（注意拼写：deterior+ate，不是 deterio+rate）|
| | intercourse | /ˈɪntərkɔːrs/ | n. 交流，交往；性交 | social intercourse 社交<br>intercourse between 之间的交流 | Language is essential for social **intercourse**. （语言对社交至关重要。）<br>The internet has transformed human **intercourse**. （互联网改变了人类的交往方式。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：communication（交流）/ interaction（互动）· **易混**：intercourse vs intercourse ≠ intercourse（≠ intercourse vs intercom 对讲机）· 词根：inter-相互 + course 运行 → 人际互动|
| | rotten | /ˈrɑːtn/ | adj. 腐烂的；腐败的；<br>非常糟糕的 | rotten fruit 腐烂的水果<br>rotten weather 糟糕的天气<br>rotten to the core 烂透了<br>rot v. 腐烂 | The apples have gone **rotten**. （苹果已经腐烂了。）<br>I feel **rotten** about missing your wedding. （错过你的婚礼我感到很糟糕。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：decayed（腐烂的）/ spoiled（变质的）/ corrupt（腐败的）· **反义**：fresh（新鲜的）· **易混**：rotten vs cotton（棉花）|
| | innumerable | /ɪˈnuːmərəbl/ | adj. 无数的，数不清的 | innumerable times 无数次<br>innumerable difficulties 无数困难 | There are **innumerable** stars in the sky. （天上有无数的星星。）<br>I've told him **innumerable** times to be careful. （我告诉过他无数次要小心。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：countless（无数的）/ numerous（许多的）/ myriad（无数的）· **反义**：few（少的）· **易混**：innumerable vs innumerable ≠ innumerable（注意：in + numer + able = 不可数的）|
| | blunt | /blʌnt/ | adj. 钝的；直率的<br>v. 使变钝；减弱 | blunt instrument 钝器<br>blunt criticism 直言不讳的批评<br>to be blunt 坦率地说<br>blunt the edge 挫其锋芒 | The knife is too **blunt** to cut the meat. （刀太钝了，切不动肉。）<br>**To be blunt**, I think your plan won't work. （恕我直言，我觉得你的计划行不通。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：frank（坦率的）/ dull（钝的）· **反义**：sharp（锋利的）/ tactful（委婉的）· **易混**：blunt vs blunt ≠ blunt（≠ blunt vs blunder 大错）|
| | deplore | /dɪˈplɔːr/ | v. 谴责，强烈反对；<br>对…感到悲痛 | deplore violence 谴责暴力<br>deplore the decision 对决定表示遗憾<br>deplorable adj. 可悲的 | The UN **deplored** the use of chemical weapons. （联合国谴责使用化学武器。）<br>We **deplore** the loss of innocent lives. （我们对无辜生命的逝去表示悲痛。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：condemn（谴责）/ lament（哀叹）· **反义**：applaud（赞赏）/ praise（赞扬）· **易混**：deplore vs explore（探索）vs implore（恳求）|
| | siren | /ˈsaɪrən/ | n. 汽笛，警报器；<br>（希腊神话）塞壬女妖 | police siren 警笛<br>air-raid siren 防空警报<br>siren song 诱人但危险的事物<br>the siren call of …的诱惑 | We heard the **sirens** of the ambulance. （我们听到了救护车的警笛声。）<br>He couldn't resist the **siren** call of the big city. （他无法抗拒大城市的诱惑。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：alarm（警报器）/ horn（喇叭）· **易混**：siren vs silent（安静的）vs sir（先生）|
| | agitate | /ˈædʒɪteɪt/ | v. 煽动，鼓动；<br>使焦虑不安；搅动 | agitate for 鼓动要求…<br>agitate against 煽动反对<br>agitation n. 煽动；焦虑<br>agitator n. 煽动者 | Workers **agitated** for higher wages. （工人们鼓动要求加薪。）<br>He became **agitated** at the thought of the exam. （一想到考试他就焦躁不安。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：stir up（煽动）/ campaign（运动）· **反义**：calm（使平静）/ soothe（安抚）· **易混**：agitate vs irritate（激怒）vs agitate ≠ agitate（≠ agitate vs aggregate 聚集）|
| | mob | /mɑːb/ | n. 暴民，乌合之众；<br>一群人<br>v. 围攻，蜂拥围住 | lynch mob 私刑暴民<br>mob mentality 从众心理<br>be mobbed by 被…围住<br>flash mob 快闪族 | An angry **mob** gathered outside the courthouse. （一群愤怒的暴民聚集在法院外。）<br>The singer was **mobbed** by fans at the airport. （歌手在机场被粉丝围住了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：crowd（人群）/ gang（帮派）· **易混**：mob vs rob（抢劫）vs mob ≠ mob（≠ mob vs mop 拖把）|
| | retort | /rɪˈtɔːrt/ | v./n. 反驳，回嘴 | retort that 反驳说<br>quick retort 迅速回击<br>sharp retort 尖刻的反驳 | "It's none of your business," she **retorted**. （"不关你的事，"她反呛道。）<br>He couldn't think of a suitable **retort**. （他想不出合适的回击。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：reply sharply（尖锐回应）/ counter（反驳）· **易混**：retort vs resort（度假地/诉诸）vs distort（扭曲）|
| | clutch | /klʌtʃ/ | v. 紧抓，紧握<br>n. 离合器；一窝（蛋）；<br>掌控（clutches） | clutch at 抓住<br>in the clutches of 在…掌控中<br>clutch bag 手拿包 | She **clutched** her bag tightly. （她紧紧抓住她的包。）<br>He fell into the **clutches** of the drug dealers. （他落入了毒贩的魔掌。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：grip（紧握）/ grasp（抓牢）/ seize（抓住）· **易混**：clutch vs crutch（拐杖）vs clutch ≠ clutch（≠ clutch vs catch 抓住）|
| | lofty | /ˈlɔːfti/ | adj. 崇高的，高尚的；<br>高耸的；傲慢的 | lofty ideals 崇高的理想<br>lofty ambitions 雄心壮志<br>lofty mountain peaks 高耸的山峰<br>lofty attitude 傲慢的态度 | She has **lofty** ideals about public service. （她对公共服务有着崇高的理想。）<br>The **lofty** mountain peaks were covered with snow. （高耸的山峰覆盖着白雪。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：noble（崇高的）/ towering（高耸的）· **反义**：humble（谦逊的）/ low（低的）· **易混**：lofty vs loft（阁楼）vs lofty ≠ lofty（≠ lofty vs lotion 乳液）|
| | sentiment | /ˈsentɪmənt/ | n. 情感，情绪；<br>观点，感想 | public sentiment 公众情绪<br>anti-war sentiment 反战情绪<br>I share your sentiment 我赞同你的看法<br>sentimental adj. 多愁善感的 | There is strong **anti-war sentiment** in the country. （国内有强烈的反战情绪。）<br>I share your **sentiment** about the new policy. （我赞同你对新政策的看法。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：feeling（感情）/ emotion（情感）/ opinion（看法）· **易混**：sentiment vs sediment（沉淀物）vs sentiment ≠ sentiment（≠ sentiment vs sentence 句子）|
| | prophet | /ˈprɑːfɪt/ | n. 先知；预言者 | false prophet 假先知<br>prophet of doom 末日预言家<br>prophetic adj. 预言的<br>prophecy n. 预言 | He is regarded as a **prophet** in his field. （他在自己领域被视为先知。）<br>A **prophet** of doom predicted the market crash. （一位末日预言家预言了市场崩盘。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：seer（预言家）/ oracle（神谕）· **易混**：prophet vs profit（利润，同音！）vs prophet ≠ prophet（≠ prophet vs profile 侧面/简介）|
| | magistrate | /ˈmædʒɪstreɪt/ | n. 地方法官，治安法官 | appear before a magistrate 在地方法官面前受审<br>chief magistrate 首席地方法官 | The case was heard by a **magistrate**. （此案由地方法官审理。）<br>He was brought before the **magistrate** for sentencing. （他被带到地方法官面前接受宣判。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：judge（法官）/ justice（大法官）· **易混**：magistrate vs magistrate ≠ magistrate（注意拼写 magis+trate，不是 majis+trate）· 词根：magistr- 主人 → 地方长官|
| | stab | /stæb/ | v./n. 刺，戳；刺痛；<br>尝试 | stab sb to death 刺死某人<br>stab in the back 背后捅刀，背叛<br>take a stab at 尝试做<br>stabbing pain 刺痛 | He felt a sharp **stab** of pain in his chest. （他感到胸口一阵刺痛。）<br>Her betrayal felt like a **stab** in the back. （她的背叛感觉像背后捅了一刀。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：pierce（刺穿）/ thrust（猛刺）· **易混**：stab vs stable（马厩/稳定的）vs slab（厚板）|
| | spiral | /ˈspaɪrəl/ | n. 螺旋<br>v. 螺旋式上升/下降；<br>急剧恶化 | spiral staircase 螺旋楼梯<br>downward spiral 螺旋式下降<br>inflation spiral 通货膨胀螺旋<br>spiral out of control 失控恶化 | The economy is in a dangerous downward **spiral**. （经济处于危险的螺旋式下滑中。）<br>Fuel prices have **spiraled** in recent months. （近几个月油价螺旋式攀升。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：cycle（循环）/ vortex（漩涡）· **易混**：spiral vs spirit（精神）vs spiral ≠ spiral（≠ spiral vs spine 脊柱）|
| | tan | /tæn/ | n. 棕褐色；晒黑的皮肤<br>v. 晒黑；鞣制（皮革）<br>adj. 棕褐色的 | get a tan 晒黑<br>suntan lotion 防晒霜<br>tan leather 鞣制皮革 | She has a beautiful **tan** from the beach holiday. （海滩度假后她晒出了漂亮的棕褐色皮肤。）<br>He spent the afternoon **tanning** by the pool. （他一下午在池边晒太阳。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：suntan（晒黑）/ bronze（古铜色）· **易混**：tan vs tank（坦克/水箱）vs tin（锡罐）|
