| | hemisphere | /ˈhemɪsfɪr/ | n. 半球；大脑半球 | northern/southern hemisphere 北/南半球<br>left/right hemisphere 左/右脑<br>cerebral hemisphere 大脑半球 | Australia is in the southern **hemisphere**. （澳大利亚位于南半球。）<br>The left **hemisphere** of the brain controls language. （大脑左半球控制语言。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：half-sphere（半球）· **易混**：hemisphere vs atmosphere（大气层）· 词根：hemi-半 + sphere 球 → 半球|
| | eject | /iˈdʒekt/ | v. 弹出，排出；驱逐 | eject sb from 把某人赶出<br>eject a CD 弹出光盘<br>ejection n. 弹出；驱逐 | The pilot **ejected** from the crashing plane. （飞行员从坠毁的飞机中弹射出来。）<br>He was **ejected** from the bar for fighting. （他因打架被赶出酒吧。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：expel（开除/驱逐）/ discharge（排出）· **易混**：eject vs inject（注射）vs reject（拒绝）· 词根：e-出 + ject 投 → 投出去|
| | outskirts | /ˈaʊtskɜːrts/ | n. 郊区，市郊（复数） | on the outskirts of 在…的郊区<br>live in the outskirts 住在郊区 | They live on the **outskirts** of London. （他们住在伦敦郊区。）<br>The factory is located in the **outskirts** of the city. （工厂位于市郊。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：suburb（郊区）/ outskirts（城市边缘）· **易混**：outskirts vs outskirts ≠ outskirts（out + skirts = 外围 → 郊区，形象好记！）|
| | tape | /teɪp/ | n. 磁带；胶带<br>v. 录音；用胶带粘 | cassette tape 盒式磁带<br>red tape 官僚习气，繁文缛节<br>tape recorder 磁带录音机 | The interview was recorded on **tape**. （采访被录在磁带上。）<br>We need to cut through the **red tape** to get the permit. （我们需要突破繁文缛节才能拿到许可证。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：record（录音/录像）/ ribbon（带子）· **易混**：tape vs tap（水龙头/轻敲）vs tape ≠ tape（≠ tape vs rape 强奸）|
| | fringe | /frɪndʒ/ | n. 刘海；边缘，外围<br>adj. 边缘的，非主流的 | on the fringe of 在…的边缘<br>fringe benefits 附加福利<br>fringe group 边缘群体 | He lives on the **fringe** of society. （他生活在社会的边缘。）<br>The job comes with attractive **fringe** benefits. （这份工作有诱人的附加福利。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：edge（边缘）/ margin（边缘）/ periphery（外围）· **反义**：center（中心）/ core（核心）· **易混**：fringe vs fridge（冰箱）|
| | lobby | /ˈlɑːbi/ | n. 大厅，大堂；游说团体<br>v. 游说 | hotel lobby 酒店大堂<br>lobby group 游说团体<br>lobby for/against 游说支持/反对 | We waited for them in the hotel **lobby**. （我们在酒店大堂等他们。）<br>Environmental groups **lobbied** for stricter regulations. （环保团体游说要求更严格的法规。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：hall（大厅）/ pressure group（压力团体）· **易混**：lobby vs hobby（爱好）vs lobby ≠ lobby（≠ lobby vs lobbyist 说客）|
| | premise | /ˈpremɪs/ | n. 前提，假设；<br>（pl.）经营场所 | on the premise that 以…为前提<br>basic premise 基本前提<br>false premise 错误前提 | His argument is based on a false **premise**. （他的论点基于错误前提。）<br>The company moved to larger **premises**. （公司搬到了更大的经营场所。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：assumption（假设）/ hypothesis（假说）· **易混**：premise vs promise（承诺）vs premise ≠ premise（≠ premise vs premium 保险费/优质的）|
| | rim | /rɪm/ | n. 边缘，边框；轮辋 | on the rim of 在…的边缘<br>rim of a glass 杯沿<br>rim of a wheel 轮辋 | He filled the glass to the **rim**. （他把杯子倒满了到杯沿。）<br>The **rim** of the crater was visible from a distance. （火山口边缘从远处就能看到。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：edge（边缘）/ brim（帽沿/杯沿）/ border（边界）· **易混**：rim vs brim（rim 硬质边缘，brim 帽/杯口）vs rim ≠ rim（≠ rim vs rib 肋骨）|
| | tentative | /ˈtentətɪv/ | adj. 试探性的；暂时的，<br>初步的 | tentative agreement 初步协议<br>tentative steps 试探性的步伐<br>tentative conclusion 暂定结论 | They reached a **tentative** agreement. （他们达成了初步协议。）<br>She took a few **tentative** steps on the ice. （她在冰上试探性地走了几步。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：provisional（临时的）/ uncertain（不确定的）/ hesitant（犹豫的）· **反义**：definite（确定的）/ firm（坚定的）· **易混**：tentative vs attentive（专心的）|
| | oak | /oʊk/ | n. 橡树；橡木 | oak tree 橡树<br>oak furniture 橡木家具<br>oak barrel 橡木桶 | The table is made of solid **oak**. （桌子由实心橡木制成。）<br>An old **oak** tree stood in the garden. （花园里有一棵老橡树。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：oak vs oath（誓言）vs oak ≠ oak（≠ oak vs soak 浸泡）|
| | robust | /roʊˈbʌst/ | adj. 强健的，健壮的；<br>结实的；强有力的 | robust health 强健的体魄<br>robust economy 强劲的经济<br>robust debate 激烈的辩论 | He has a **robust** physique. （他体格强健。）<br>The economy remains **robust** despite challenges. （尽管面临挑战，经济仍保持强劲。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：sturdy（结实的）/ vigorous（精力充沛的）/ strong（强壮的）· **反义**：frail（脆弱的）/ weak（弱的）· **易混**：robust vs rust（铁锈）vs robust ≠ robust（≠ robust vs robot 机器人）|
| | resent | /rɪˈzent/ | v. 怨恨，憎恨 | resent (doing) sth 怨恨（做）某事<br>bitterly resent 强烈憎恨<br>resentment n. 怨恨 | He **resented** being treated as a child. （他憎恨被当小孩对待。）<br>She **resents** his interference in her affairs. （她怨恨他对她事务的干涉。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：begrudge（嫉妒/不满）/ dislike（不喜欢）· **反义**：accept（接受）/ welcome（欢迎）· **易混**：resent vs recent（最近的）vs consent（同意）|
| | empirical | /ɪmˈpɪrɪkl/ | adj. 经验主义的；<br>以实验/观察为依据的 | empirical evidence 实证<br>empirical research 实证研究<br>empirical formula 经验公式<br>empiricism n. 经验主义 | The theory needs to be supported by **empirical** evidence. （理论需要实证支持。）<br>Scientists rely on **empirical** data, not speculation. （科学家依赖实证数据，而非推测。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：experimental（实验的）/ observational（观察的）· **反义**：theoretical（理论的）· **易混**：empirical vs imperial（帝国的）vs empirical ≠ empirical（≠ empirical vs empathic 共情的）|
| | dean | /diːn/ | n.（大学）院长，系主任；<br>（教会）教长 | dean of students 学生事务主任<br>college dean 学院院长<br>cathedral dean 大教堂教长 | She was appointed **dean** of the law school. （她被任命为法学院院长。）<br>The **dean** announced new academic policies. （院长宣布了新的学术政策。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：director（主任）/ principal（校长）· **易混**：dean vs deal（交易）vs dean ≠ dean（≠ dean vs bean 豆子）|
| | aggravate | /ˈæɡrəveɪt/ | v. 加重，恶化；<br>激怒，惹恼 | aggravate the situation 恶化局势<br>aggravate an injury 加重伤势<br>aggravating circumstances 加重情节<br>aggravation n. 恶化；恼怒 | The cold weather **aggravated** his condition. （寒冷天气加重了他的病情。）<br>Stop **aggravating** your brother! （别再惹你哥哥生气了！）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：worsen（恶化）/ irritate（激怒）/ exacerbate（加剧）· **反义**：alleviate（缓解）/ soothe（安抚）· **易混**：aggravate vs aggregate（聚集）vs aggravate ≠ aggravate（注意拼写：a+ggrav+ate，双写g！）|

| | qualify | /ˈkwɑːlɪfaɪ/ | v. 使有资格；限定；修饰 | qualify for 有…的资格<br>qualify as 取得…资格<br>qualify a statement 修饰陈述<br>qualification n. 资格 | She **qualifies** for the scholarship. （她有资格获得奖学金。）<br>I should **qualify** my earlier remark. （我应该限定一下先前的说法。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：entitle（赋予权利）/ certify（认证）/ modify（修饰）· **反义**：disqualify（取消资格）· **易混**：qualify vs quantify（量化）vs quality（质量）|
| | advocate | /ˈædvəkeɪt/ v.<br>/ˈædvəkət/ n. | v. 提倡，主张<br>n. 倡导者，拥护者；律师 | advocate for 为…倡导<br>strong advocate 坚定的倡导者<br>devil's advocate 故意唱反调的人 | She **advocates** for stricter environmental laws. （她提倡更严格的环境法。）<br>He is a lifelong **advocate** of free education. （他是免费教育的终身倡导者。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：champion（拥护者）/ support（支持）· **反义**：opponent（反对者）/ oppose（反对）· **易混**：advocate vs adversary（对手）|
| | detach | /dɪˈtætʃ/ | v. 拆卸，使分离；派遣 | detach from 从…分离<br>detach oneself from 摆脱<br>detachable adj. 可拆卸的<br>detached adj. 客观的；独立的 | You can **detach** the hood from the jacket. （你可以把兜帽从夹克上拆下来。）<br>She tried to **detach** herself from the controversy. （她试图从争议中抽身。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：separate（分离）/ disconnect（断开）· **反义**：attach（附上）/ connect（连接）· **易混**：detach vs attach（附上，反义对，一起记！）|

| | homogeneous | /ˌhoʊməˈdʒiːniəs/ | adj. 同质的，同类的 | homogeneous group 同质群体<br>homogeneous mixture 均匀混合物<br>homogeneity n. 同质性 | The population of the village is fairly **homogeneous**. （这个村子的人口相当同质化。）<br>A **homogeneous** mixture has a uniform composition. （均匀混合物各处成分一致。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：uniform（一致的）/ identical（相同的）· **反义**：heterogeneous（异质的）/ diverse（多样的）· **解构**：homo-同 + gene 种类 + ous → 同类的|
| | gracious | /ˈɡreɪʃəs/ | adj. 亲切的，仁慈的；<br>优雅的 | gracious host 亲切的主人<br>gracious smile 慈祥的微笑<br>good gracious 天哪！（感叹语） | She is a **gracious** host who makes everyone feel welcome. （她是亲切的主人，让每个人都感到受欢迎。）<br>**Good gracious**, I didn't expect to see you here! （天哪，没想到在这里见到你！）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：kind（和蔼的）/ courteous（有礼貌的）/ elegant（优雅的）· **反义**：rude（粗鲁的）/ ungracious（不亲切的）· **易混**：gracious vs graceful（优美的，侧重姿态）|

| | exploit | /ɪkˈsplɔɪt/ v.<br>/ˈeksplɔɪt/ n. | v. 剥削；利用，开发<br>n. 英勇事迹，壮举 | exploit workers 剥削工人<br>exploit resources 开发资源<br>exploit a loophole 利用漏洞<br>daring exploits 英勇事迹 | The company was accused of **exploiting** child labor. （该公司被指控剥削童工。）<br>He told us about his wartime **exploits**. （他给我们讲了他的战时壮举。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：utilize（利用）/ tap into（开发）· **易混**：exploit vs explore（探索）vs exploit ≠ exploit（注意重音：动词在第二音节，名词在第一音节）|
| | saucer | /ˈsɔːsər/ | n. 茶碟，茶托 | cup and saucer 杯碟<br>flying saucer 飞碟 | She set the teacup on the **saucer**. （她把茶杯放在茶碟上。）<br>People claimed to have seen a flying **saucer**. （人们声称看到了飞碟。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：saucer vs sauce（酱汁，同根词！saucer 原意是"放酱汁的碟"）vs saucer ≠ saucer（≠ saucer vs saunter 闲逛）|

| | conspicuous | /kənˈspɪkjuəs/ | adj. 显眼的，引人注目的 | conspicuous absence 显眼的缺席<br>conspicuous consumption 炫耀性消费<br>make oneself conspicuous 引人注目<br>conspicuously adv. 显眼地 | Her bright red coat was highly **conspicuous**. （她那亮红色的大衣非常显眼。）<br>He was **conspicuous** by his absence at the meeting. （他在会议上的缺席非常显眼。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：prominent（突出的）/ noticeable（引人注意的）/ striking（显眼的）· **反义**：inconspicuous（不显眼的）· **易混**：conspicuous vs continuous（连续的）|
| | worship | /ˈwɜːrʃɪp/ | n./v. 崇拜；敬奉；做礼拜 | freedom of worship 信仰自由<br>place of worship 礼拜场所<br>hero worship 英雄崇拜<br>worshipper n. 崇拜者 | The church is open for **worship** every Sunday. （教堂每周日开放做礼拜。）<br>She **worships** her elder brother. （她崇拜她的哥哥。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：adore（崇拜）/ revere（尊敬）· **反义**：despise（鄙视）/ disdain（蔑视）· **易混**：worship vs warship（战舰，同音！）|
| | democracy | /dɪˈmɑːkrəsi/ | n. 民主；民主制度 | parliamentary democracy 议会民主<br>democracy movement 民主运动<br>democratic adj. 民主的<br>democrat n. 民主主义者 | Freedom of speech is the cornerstone of **democracy**. （言论自由是民主的基石。）<br>The country is trying to build a stable **democracy**. （该国试图建立稳定的民主制度。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：republic（共和国）/ self-government（自治）· **反义**：dictatorship（独裁）/ autocracy（专制）· **解构**：demo-人民 + cracy-统治 → 人民统治|

| | arbitrary | /ˈɑːrbɪtreri/ | adj. 任意的，武断的；<br>专横的 | arbitrary decision 武断的决定<br>arbitrary rule 专横的统治<br>arbitrary choice 任意选择<br>arbitrarily adv. 武断地 | The choice seems completely **arbitrary**. （这个选择看起来完全是任意的。）<br>The dictator exercised **arbitrary** power over the country. （独裁者对国家行使专横权力。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：random（随机的）/ capricious（反复无常的）· **反义**：rational（理性的）/ justified（有道理的）· **易混**：arbitrary vs arbitration（仲裁）|
| | exclusive | /ɪkˈskluːsɪv/ | adj. 独有的，专属的；<br>排外的；高档的<br>n. 独家新闻 | exclusive right 专有权<br>mutually exclusive 相互排斥的<br>exclusive interview 独家采访<br>exclusively adv. 专门地 | The club is very **exclusive** — only members can enter. （这个俱乐部非常排外——只有会员能进。）<br>The newspaper published an **exclusive** interview with the president. （报纸刊登了对总统的独家采访。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：sole（唯一的）/ select（精选的）/ restricted（受限制的）· **反义**：inclusive（包容的）/ public（公开的）· **易混**：exclusive vs excessive（过度的）vs exclude（排除，动词）|
| | fright | /fraɪt/ | n. 惊吓，恐惧 | stage fright 怯场<br>give sb a fright 吓某人一跳<br>in fright 惊恐地<br>frighten v. 使恐惧 | You gave me such a **fright**! （你吓了我一大跳！）<br>She suffers from stage **fright** before every performance. （她每次演出前都会怯场。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：fear（恐惧）/ terror（恐怖）/ scare（惊吓）· **反义**：calmness（镇定）/ courage（勇气）· **易混**：fright vs freight（货物，同音！）vs fright ≠ fright（≠ fright vs fight 战斗）|
| | overwhelm | /ˌoʊvərˈwelm/ | v. 压倒，使不知所措；<br>淹没 | be overwhelmed by 被…压倒<br>overwhelm with 使充满（情感）<br>overwhelming adj. 压倒性的<br>overwhelming majority 压倒性多数 | She was **overwhelmed** with grief at the news. （听到消息后她被悲伤淹没。）<br>The sheer amount of work **overwhelmed** him. （巨大的工作量让他不知所措。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：overcome（克服/使受不了）/ crush（压垮）/ deluge（淹没）· **易混**：overwhelm vs overflow（溢出）vs overwhelm ≠ overwhelm（≠ overwhelm vs overwork 过度工作）|
| | speculate | /ˈspekjuleɪt/ | v. 推测，猜测；投机 | speculate about/on 猜测…<br>speculate in stocks 炒股<br>speculation n. 推测；投机<br>speculator n. 投机者 | We can only **speculate** about the cause of the crash. （我们只能推测坠机原因。）<br>He lost all his money **speculating** on the stock market. （他炒股亏光了所有的钱。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：conjecture（推测）/ guess（猜测）/ gamble（赌博）· **易混**：speculate vs spectacular（壮观的）· 词根：spec- 看 → 用脑子看 → 推测|
| | sovereign | /ˈsɑːvrən/ | n. 君主，元首<br>adj. 至高无上的；<br>有主权的 | sovereign state 主权国家<br>sovereign power 最高权力<br>sovereignty n. 主权 | Each state has **sovereign** rights over its territory. （每个国家对其领土拥有主权。）<br>The **sovereign** addressed the nation. （元首向全国发表讲话。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：ruler（统治者）/ monarch（君主）/ autonomous（自治的）· **易混**：sovereign vs sovereignty（主权，名词）vs sovereign ≠ sovereign（注意发音 /ˈsɑːvrən/，g不发音！）|
| | pope | /poʊp/ | n. 教皇，教宗 | Pope Francis 教皇方济各<br>the election of a new pope 新教皇选举 | The **Pope** addressed the crowd in St. Peter's Square. （教皇在圣彼得广场向人群发表讲话。）<br>He was elected **Pope** in 2013. （他于2013年当选教皇。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：pope vs pop（流行/弹出）vs pope ≠ pope（≠ pope vs pole 杆/极点）· 大写 Pope 特指罗马天主教教皇|
| | radical | /ˈrædɪkl/ | adj. 激进的；根本的<br>n. 激进分子 | radical change 根本性变革<br>radical ideas 激进的想法<br>radical reform 彻底改革<br>radically adv. 根本地 | The policy needs **radical** reform. （政策需要彻底改革。）<br>He was considered a **radical** in his youth. （他年轻时被认为是激进分子。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：extreme（极端的）/ revolutionary（革命性的）/ fundamental（根本的）· **反义**：conservative（保守的）/ moderate（温和的）· **易混**：radical vs radiant（光芒四射的）vs radical ≠ radical（≠ radical vs radical 自由基/根号）|
| | anecdote | /ˈænɪkdoʊt/ | n. 轶事，趣闻 | personal anecdote 个人轶事<br>tell an anecdote 讲一个趣闻<br>anecdotal evidence 轶事证据 | He told us an amusing **anecdote** about his travels. （他给我们讲了一个关于他旅行的有趣轶事。）<br>The book is full of entertaining **anecdotes**. （这本书充满了有趣的轶事。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：story（故事）/ tale（传说）· **易混**：anecdote vs antidote（解毒剂，读音极像！anec- vs anti-）· 词根：an-未 + ec-出 + dote-给 → 未公开的小故事|
| | redeem | /rɪˈdiːm/ | v. 赎回；挽回，补救；<br>兑换 | redeem oneself 挽回声誉<br>redeem a promise 兑现承诺<br>redeem a voucher 兑换优惠券<br>redemption n. 救赎 | He tried to **redeem** himself by apologizing. （他试图通过道歉来挽回自己。）<br>You can **redeem** the coupon at any store. （你可以在任何商店兑换这张优惠券。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：save（拯救）/ compensate（弥补）/ exchange（兑换）· **易混**：redeem vs deem（认为）vs redeem ≠ redeem（≠ redeem vs regain 重新获得）|
| | oblige | /əˈblaɪdʒ/ | v. 迫使；帮忙；<br>使感激 | be obliged to do 不得不做<br>be obliged to sb 感激某人<br>much obliged 非常感谢<br>obligation n. 义务 | The law **obliges** companies to disclose their finances. （法律强制公司披露财务状况。）<br>I'm much **obliged** to you for your help. （非常感谢你的帮助。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：compel（迫使）/ force（强迫）/ require（要求）· **易混**：oblige vs obligate（有义务，同源）vs oblige ≠ oblige（≠ oblige vs oblique 斜的）|
| | storey | /ˈstɔːri/ | n. 楼层<br>（美式拼写：story） | two-storey building 两层建筑<br>multi-storey car park 多层停车场<br>top storey 顶层 | They live in a three-**storey** house. （他们住在一栋三层楼的房子里。）<br>The building has six **storeys**. （这栋楼有六层。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：floor（楼层）/ level（层）· **易混**：storey vs story（故事，英式拼写区分，美式story两者通用）|
| | precedent | /ˈpresɪdənt/ | n. 先例，前例 | set a precedent 开创先例<br>without precedent 史无前例<br>legal precedent 法律先例<br>precede v. 先于 | This ruling sets a dangerous **precedent**. （这项裁决开创了危险的先例。）<br>There is no **precedent** for such a massive recall. （如此大规模的召回史无前例。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：example（范例）/ model（典范）· **易混**：precedent vs president（总统，差一个字母！）vs precedent ≠ precedent（≠ precedent vs precedence 优先权）|
| | immense | /ɪˈmens/ | adj. 巨大的，极大的 | immense importance 极大的重要性<br>immense pressure 巨大的压力<br>immense wealth 巨额财富<br>immensely adv. 极其 | The project requires an **immense** amount of effort. （项目需要巨大的努力。）<br>She felt an **immense** sense of relief. （她感到极大的解脱。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：enormous（巨大的）/ tremendous（极大的）/ vast（广阔的）· **反义**：tiny（微小的）/ minute（微小的）· **易混**：immense vs immerse（沉浸）vs immense ≠ immense（≠ immense vs intense 强烈的）|

| | rear | /rɪr/ | n. 后部，背后<br>adj. 后部的<br>v. 抚养；饲养；用后腿站立 | at the rear of 在…的后部<br>rear door 后门<br>rear children 抚养孩子<br>rear up 后腿站立 | The garden is at the **rear** of the house. （花园在房子的后面。）<br>She **reared** three children on her own. （她独自抚养了三个孩子。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：back（后面）/ raise（抚养）/ breed（饲养）· **反义**：front（前面）· **易混**：rear vs rare（稀有的）vs rear ≠ rear（≠ rear vs earn 赚）|

| | grind | /ɡraɪnd/ | v. 磨碎；磨光；苦干<br>n. 苦差事 | grind coffee beans 磨咖啡豆<br>grind to a halt 慢慢停下来<br>daily grind 日常苦差事<br>grindstone n. 磨石 | She **grinds** fresh coffee every morning. （她每天早上现磨咖啡。）<br>The traffic **ground** to a halt on the highway. （高速公路上的车流慢慢停了下来。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：crush（碾碎）/ mill（研磨）/ drudgery（苦工）· **易混**：grind vs grand（宏伟的）vs grind ≠ grind（≠ grind vs grin 咧嘴笑）· ⚠️ 过去式/过去分词 ground|
| | tide | /taɪd/ | n. 潮汐；潮流，趋势 | high/low tide 涨/退潮<br>tide over 帮助渡过（难关）<br>turn the tide 扭转局势<br>tidal wave 海啸 | The **tide** is coming in. （涨潮了。）<br>Public opinion has turned the **tide** against the policy. （舆论扭转了对该政策的支持趋势。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：current（水流/趋势）/ trend（趋势）/ wave（浪潮）· **易混**：tide vs tidy（整洁的）vs tide ≠ tide（≠ tide vs tide 一字多义：潮汐 + 趋势）|
| | blur | /blɜːr/ | n. 模糊不清<br>v. 使模糊，变得模糊 | blur the line 模糊界限<br>in a blur 模糊地<br>blurred vision 视力模糊<br>blurry adj. 模糊的 | The days all seemed to merge into a **blur**. （那些日子似乎都模糊成了一片。）<br>Tears **blurred** her vision. （泪水模糊了她的视线。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：obscure（使模糊）/ smudge（弄脏）· **反义**：clarify（澄清）/ sharpen（使清晰）· **易混**：blur vs blue（蓝色）vs blur ≠ blur（≠ blur vs slur 含糊的发音）|
| | kin | /kɪn/ | n. 亲属，家人（总称）<br>adj. 有亲戚关系的 | next of kin 直系亲属，最近亲属<br>kith and kin 亲戚朋友<br>be kin to 与…有亲属关系<br>kinship n. 亲属关系 | We must inform the next of **kin** before releasing the name. （公布名字前必须先通知直系亲属。）<br>She felt a strong connection with her distant **kin**. （她与远房亲属有种强烈的联系。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：relatives（亲戚）/ family（家人）/ folk（家人）· **反义**：stranger（陌生人）· **易混**：kin vs king（国王）vs kin ≠ kin（≠ kin vs keen 热衷的）|

| | scrape | /skreɪp/ | v. 刮，擦；勉强通过<br>n. 刮擦声；困境 | scrape through 勉强通过<br>scrape together 勉强凑齐<br>scrape the surface 触及表面<br>in a scrape 陷入困境 | He barely **scraped** through the exam. （他勉强通过了考试。）<br>I **scraped** the ice off the windshield. （我把挡风玻璃上的冰刮掉了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：scratch（抓，擦）/ rub（擦）/ scuff（磨损）· **易混**：scrape vs scrap（废料，差一个字母！）vs scrape ≠ scrape（≠ scrape vs grape 葡萄）|
| | overlap | /ˌoʊvərˈlæp/ v.<br>/ˈoʊvərlæp/ n. | v. 重叠，部分相同<br>n. 重叠部分 | overlap with 与…重叠<br>overlap in …方面的重叠<br>overlapping adj. 重叠的 | The two subjects **overlap** in many ways. （这两个学科在很多方面有重叠。）<br>There is considerable **overlap** between their responsibilities. （他们的职责有相当大的重叠。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：coincide（重合）/ intersect（相交）· **易混**：overlap vs overland（陆上的）· over + lap = 之上 + 交叠 → 重叠|
| | concession | /kənˈseʃn/ | n. 让步；特许权；<br>优惠 | make a concession 作出让步<br>without concession 毫不让步<br>tax concession 税收优惠<br>concede v. 让步；承认 | Both sides made **concessions** to reach the agreement. （双方都作了让步以达成协议。）<br>Students get a **concession** on bus fares. （学生享有公交车费优惠。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：compromise（妥协）/ allowance（允许）/ discount（折扣）· **反义**：refusal（拒绝）/ insistence（坚持）· **易混**：concession vs concession ≠ concession（≠ concession vs confession 忏悔）|
| | despatch | /dɪˈspætʃ/ | v./n. 派遣；发送；迅速完成<br>（= dispatch） | despatch troops 派遣部队<br>with despatch 迅速地<br>despatch a letter 发送信件 | The government **despatched** aid to the disaster area. （政府向灾区派遣了救援。）<br>He finished the task with great **despatch**. （他以极快的速度完成了任务。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：send（发送）/ expedite（加速处理）· **易混**：despatch vs dispatch（同义，dispatch更常用美式拼写）|

| | analogy | /əˈnælədʒi/ | n. 类比，比喻 | draw an analogy 作类比<br>by analogy with 通过类比<br>close analogy 很类似的类比<br>analogous adj. 类似的 | He drew an **analogy** between the brain and a computer. （他把人脑和电脑作类比。）<br>**By analogy**, we can predict the new product's success. （通过类比，我们可以预测新产品的成功。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：comparison（比较）/ similarity（相似）/ parallel（相似之处）· **易混**：analogy vs analogue（类似物）vs analogy ≠ analogy（≠ analogy vs apology 道歉）|
| | exotic | /ɪɡˈzɑːtɪk/ | adj. 异国情调的，奇异的；<br>外来的 | exotic fruit 异国水果<br>exotic destination 异国目的地<br>exotic species 外来物种 | The market sells **exotic** spices from around the world. （市场出售来自世界各地的异国香料。）<br>She loves traveling to **exotic** locations. （她喜欢去有异国情调的地方旅行。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：foreign（外国的）/ unusual（不寻常的）/ tropical（热带的）· **反义**：local（本地的）/ native（本土的）· **易混**：exotic vs erotic（色情的，差一个字母！⚠️）vs exotic ≠ exotic（≠ exotic vs exit 出口）|
| | extinguish | /ɪkˈstɪŋɡwɪʃ/ | v. 熄灭；消灭，使破灭 | extinguish a fire 灭火<br>extinguish hope 使希望破灭<br>extinguish a debt 清偿债务<br>extinguisher n. 灭火器 | Firefighters quickly **extinguished** the blaze. （消防员迅速扑灭了大火。）<br>The news **extinguished** all hope of their return. （消息粉碎了他们归来的所有希望。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：put out（熄灭）/ quench（熄灭）/ obliterate（消灭）· **反义**：ignite（点燃）/ kindle（点燃）· **易混**：extinguish vs distinguish（区分）vs extinguish ≠ extinguish（≠ extinguish vs extinct 灭绝的）|
| | avert | /əˈvɜːrt/ | v. 避免，防止；转移（目光） | avert a crisis 避免危机<br>avert disaster 避免灾难<br>avert one's eyes 转移目光 | The quick thinking of the pilot **averted** a disaster. （飞行员的迅速反应避免了一场灾难。）<br>She **averted** her eyes from the horrific scene. （她从恐怖的场景上移开了目光。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：prevent（防止）/ avoid（避免）/ divert（转移）· **反义**：cause（引起）/ bring about（导致）· **易混**：avert vs advertise（广告）vs avert ≠ avert（≠ avert vs ever 曾经）|

| | ecology | /iˈkɑːlədʒi/ | n. 生态学；生态 | marine ecology 海洋生态<br>ecology movement 生态运动<br>ecological adj. 生态的<br>ecologist n. 生态学家 | The oil spill caused serious damage to the local **ecology**. （石油泄漏对当地生态造成了严重破坏。）<br>**Ecology** helps us understand how living things interact. （生态学帮助我们理解生物如何相互作用。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：environment（环境）/ ecosystem（生态系统）· **易混**：ecology vs biology（生物学）· 词根：eco-家 + logy-学 → 研究家园的学问 → 生态学|

| | devise | /dɪˈvaɪz/ | v. 设计，发明；想出，策划 | devise a plan 制订计划<br>devise a method 想出方法<br>devise a strategy 制定策略<br>device n. 装置，设备 | They **devised** a clever plan to escape. （他们想出了一个巧妙的逃跑计划。）<br>Scientists are **devising** new ways to combat the disease. （科学家正在设计对抗该疾病的新方法。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：design（设计）/ invent（发明）/ formulate（制定）· **易混**：devise vs device（装置，名词，发音不同！devise /dɪˈvaɪz/ vs device /dɪˈvaɪs/）· 注意拼写：动词devise用s，名词device用c|

| | imply | /ɪmˈplaɪ/ | v. 暗示，暗指；意味着 | imply that 暗示…<br>what are you implying? 你什么意思？<br>implication n. 含义，暗示 | His silence **implied** consent. （他的沉默暗示了同意。）<br>What exactly are you **implying**? （你到底在暗示什么？）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：suggest（暗示）/ hint（暗示）/ indicate（表明）· **反义**：state（明确说明）/ declare（声明）· **易混**：imply vs infer（推断，说话者imply，听者infer！）|
| | pile | /paɪl/ | n. 一堆，一摞；大量<br>v. 堆积，堆放 | a pile of 一堆<br>pile up 堆积<br>pile on 堆上；施加 | There is a **pile** of laundry on the floor. （地板上有一堆待洗衣物。）<br>The work just keeps **piling** up. （工作越堆越多。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：heap（杂乱堆）/ stack（整齐摞）· **易混**：pile vs pill（药丸）vs pile ≠ pile（≠ pile vs pale 苍白的）|
| | render | /ˈrendər/ | v. 使变得；提供，给予；<br>翻译；渲染 | render sb/sth + adj. 使…变得<br>render services 提供服务<br>render an account 提交报告<br>rendering n. 渲染；翻译 | The accident **rendered** him unable to work. （事故使他无法工作。）<br>We **rendered** first aid to the injured. （我们给伤者进行了急救。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：make（使成为）/ provide（提供）/ translate（翻译）· **易混**：render vs rent（租赁）vs render ≠ render（≠ render vs tender 温柔的）|
| | yield | /jiːld/ | v. 产出；屈服，让步<br>n. 产量，产出 | yield results 产生结果<br>yield to 屈服于<br>high yield 高产<br>yield sign 让行标志 | The investigation **yielded** important evidence. （调查产生了重要证据。）<br>He refused to **yield** to pressure. （他拒绝向压力屈服。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：produce（产出）/ surrender（屈服）/ output（产量）· **反义**：resist（抵抗）/ withstand（抵抗）· **易混**：yield vs yeld（拼写不要错！）|
| | immerse | /ɪˈmɜːrs/ | v. 使沉浸，使浸没；<br>使专心于 | immerse oneself in 专心于<br>be immersed in 沉浸于<br>immersion n. 沉浸 | She **immersed** herself in her studies. （她全身心投入学习。）<br>He was completely **immersed** in the book. （他完全沉浸在书中。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：absorb（使全神贯注）/ submerge（淹没）· **易混**：immerse vs immense（巨大的，拼写极像！）vs immerse ≠ immerse（≠ immerse vs amass 积累）|
| | sensation | /senˈseɪʃn/ | n. 感觉，知觉；轰动 | create a sensation 引起轰动<br>burning sensation 灼热感<br>sensory adj. 感觉的<br>sense n./v. 感觉 | The news caused a **sensation** around the world. （这条新闻在全世界引起轰动。）<br>She felt a strange tingling **sensation** in her fingers. （她手指感到一阵奇怪的刺痛感。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：feeling（感觉）/ stir（轰动）· **易混**：sensation vs sensational（耸人听闻的）vs sensation ≠ sensation（≠ sensation vs sentence 句子）|
| | endeavor | /ɪnˈdevər/ | n./v. 努力，尽力<br>（英：endeavour） | scientific endeavor 科学探索<br>endeavor to do 努力做<br>despite one's best endeavors 尽管尽了最大努力 | They **endeavored** to complete the project on time. （他们努力按时完成项目。）<br>Science is a human **endeavor** to understand nature. （科学是人类理解自然的努力。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：strive（努力）/ attempt（尝试）/ effort（努力，名词）· **易混**：endeavor vs endeavor ≠ endeavor（美式拼写 endeavor，英式 endeavour）|
| | prone | /proʊn/ | adj. 易于…的；有…倾向的；<br>俯卧的 | be prone to (doing) sth 容易受…影响<br>accident-prone 易出事故的<br>prone position 俯卧位 | He is **prone** to catching colds in winter. （他冬天容易感冒。）<br>The area is **prone** to flooding during heavy rain. （该地区暴雨期间容易淹水。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：susceptible（易受影响的）/ inclined（倾向于）· **反义**：resistant（抵抗的）/ immune（免疫的）· **易混**：prone vs probe（探查）vs prone ≠ prone（≠ prone vs pronoun 代词）|
| | detector | /dɪˈtektər/ | n. 检测器，探测器 | metal detector 金属探测器<br>lie detector 测谎仪<br>smoke detector 烟雾报警器<br>detect v. 探测，发现 | The **smoke detector** went off in the middle of the night. （烟雾报警器半夜响了。）<br>Airport security uses metal **detectors** to screen passengers. （机场安检用金属探测器检查乘客。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：sensor（传感器）/ scanner（扫描仪）· **易混**：detector vs detective（侦探）vs detector ≠ detector（≠ detector vs detector ≠ protect 保护）|
| | slum | /slʌm/ | n. 贫民窟，棚户区 | slum area 贫民区<br>live in a slum 住在贫民窟<br>slum conditions 贫民窟条件 | He grew up in the **slums** of the city. （他在城市的贫民窟长大。）<br>The government plans to demolish the **slums**. （政府计划拆除贫民窟。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：ghetto（贫民区）· **反义**：affluent neighborhood（富裕社区）· **易混**：slum vs slam（砰地关上）vs slum ≠ slum（≠ slum vs plum 李子）|
| | affiliate (复现) | /əˈfɪlieɪt/ v. /əˈfɪliət/ n. | v. 使隶属于<br>n. 附属机构 | affiliate with 隶属于 | The hospital is **affiliated** with the university. （该医院隶属于大学。）|
| | sore (复现) | /sɔːr/ | adj. 疼痛的；恼火的<br>n. 痛处 | sore throat 喉咙痛<br>sore point 痛点 | I have a **sore** throat. （我喉咙痛。）|
| | wreck (复现) | /rek/ | n. 残骸；v. 毁掉 | ship wreck 沉船<br>nervous wreck 精神崩溃 | The storm **wrecked** several boats. （暴风雨毁了几艘船。）|
| | envisage (复现) | /ɪnˈvɪzɪdʒ/ | v. 设想，想象；展望 | envisage doing sth 设想做某事 | She **envisaged** a future where everyone has access to education. （她设想人人都能接受教育的未来。）|
| | diplomatic (复现) | /ˌdɪpləˈmætɪk/ | adj. 外交的；有手腕的 | diplomatic relations 外交关系 | She gave a very **diplomatic** reply. （她给了非常得体的回答。）|
| | illiterate (复现) | /ɪˈlɪtərət/ | adj. 不识字，文盲；外行的 | functionally illiterate 功能性文盲<br>computer illiterate 电脑盲 | Many children in remote areas remain **illiterate**. （偏远地区许多孩子仍是文盲。）|
| | memorandum (复现) | /ˌmeməˈrændəm/ | n. 备忘录 | internal memorandum 内部备忘录 | The CEO sent a **memorandum** to all staff. （CEO发了备忘录给全员。）|
| | surplus (复现) | /ˈsɜːrpləs/ | n./adj. 过剩，盈余 | trade surplus 贸易顺差<br>surplus value 剩余价值 | The country has a large trade **surplus**. （该国有巨额贸易顺差。）|
| | summit (复现) | /ˈsʌmɪt/ | n. 山顶；峰会 | summit meeting 峰会<br>reach the summit 登顶 | They finally reached the **summit**. （他们终于登顶了。）|
| | elapse (复现) | /ɪˈlæps/ | v.（时间）流逝 | time elapses 时间流逝 | Several months **elapsed** before he replied. （几个月过去了他才回复。）|
| | handicap (复现) | /ˈhændikæp/ | n./v. 障碍；妨碍 | mental handicap 智力障碍 | Lack of funding is a serious **handicap**. （资金不足是严重障碍。）|
| | deduce (复现) | /dɪˈduːs/ | v. 推断，演绎 | deduce from 从…推断 | We can **deduce** from the evidence that he was there. （我们从证据推断他在场。）|
| | heap (复现) | /hiːp/ | n. 一堆；大量<br>v. 堆积 | a heap of 一堆；大量<br>heap praise on 对…大加赞扬 | There was a **heap** of dirty clothes. （有一堆脏衣服。）|
| | litter (复现) | /ˈlɪtər/ | n. 垃圾；一窝幼崽<br>v. 乱扔 | no littering 禁止乱扔<br>littered with 满是 | The floor was **littered** with papers. （地板上散落着纸片。）|
| | discretion (复现) | /dɪˈskreʃn/ | n. 谨慎；自行决定权 | at one's discretion 自行决定 | Use your own **discretion**. （自行斟酌决定。）|
| | merit (复现) | /ˈmerɪt/ | n. 优点，价值；v. 值得 | have merit 有价值<br>on its own merits 根据本身情况 | The plan has considerable **merit**. （这个计划很有价值。）|
| | compact (复现) | /ˈkɑːmpækt/ adj. /kəmˈpækt/ v. | adj. 紧凑的<br>v. 压缩 | compact car 紧凑型车<br>compact disc 光盘 | The apartment is small but **compact**. （公寓虽小但紧凑。）|
| | prejudice (复现) | /ˈpredʒədɪs/ | n. 偏见<br>v. 使有偏见 | racial prejudice 种族偏见<br>prejudice against 对…的偏见 | We must fight against **prejudice**. （我们必须反对偏见。）|
| | contaminate (复现) | /kənˈtæmɪneɪt/ | v. 污染，弄脏 | contaminate water 污染水<br>contamination n. 污染 | The water was **contaminated** with chemicals. （水被化学物质污染了。）|
| | drawback (复现) | /ˈdrɔːbæk/ | n. 缺点，弊端 | major drawback 主要缺点 | The only **drawback** is the cost. （唯一的缺点是成本。）|
| | resolute (复现) | /ˈrezəluːt/ | adj. 坚定的，坚决的 | resolute determination 坚定决心 | She remained **resolute** in her decision. （她对决定坚定不移。）|
| | diversion (复现) | /daɪˈvɜːrʒn/ | n. 转移；消遣 | create a diversion 声东击西<br>traffic diversion 交通绕行 | Reading is a pleasant **diversion**. （阅读是愉快的消遣。）|
| | gloomy (复现) | /ˈɡluːmi/ | adj. 阴暗的；沮丧的 | gloomy weather 阴沉的天气<br>gloomy outlook 悲观前景 | He felt **gloomy** about his job prospects. （他对就业前景感到悲观。）|
| | duplicate (复现) | /ˈduːplɪkeɪt/ v. /ˈduːplɪkət/ n./adj. | v. 复制<br>n. 副本<br>adj. 复制的 | in duplicate 一式两份<br>duplicate a key 配钥匙 | Please submit the form in **duplicate**. （请提交表格一式两份。）|
| | wool (复现) | /wʊl/ | n. 羊毛；毛线 | wool sweater 羊毛衫<br>pull the wool over sb's eyes 蒙骗 | This scarf is made of pure **wool**. （这条围巾是纯羊毛的。）|
| | discourse (复现) | /ˈdɪskɔːrs/ | n. 话语，论述；演讲 | public discourse 公众话语<br>academic discourse 学术论述 | The internet has transformed public **discourse**. （互联网改变了公众话语。）|
| | tape (复现) | /teɪp/ | n. 磁带；胶带<br>v. 录音 | red tape 繁文缛节<br>cassette tape 盒式磁带 | We need to cut through the **red tape**. （我们需要突破繁文缛节。）|
| | accumulate | /əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/ | v. 积累，积聚 | accumulate wealth 积累财富<br>accumulate evidence 收集证据<br>accumulation n. 积累 | Over the years, he **accumulated** a large collection of books. （多年来，他积累了大量的藏书。）<br>Dust quickly **accumulates** in an empty room. （空房间很快积灰。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：amass（积累大量）/ gather（收集）/ build up（积累）· **反义**：disperse（分散）/ dissipate（消散）· **易混**：accumulate vs accommodate（容纳）|
| | topic | /ˈtɑːpɪk/ | n. 话题，主题 | hot topic 热门话题<br>topic of conversation 谈话主题<br>change the topic 换话题 | Climate change is a hot **topic** these days. （气候变化是当下的热门话题。）<br>Let's change the **topic** — this one is too sensitive. （换个话题吧——这个太敏感了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：subject（主题）/ theme（主题）/ issue（议题）· **易混**：topic vs tropic（回归线/热带，差一个字母！）|
| | extinct | /ɪkˈstɪŋkt/ | adj. 灭绝的；绝迹的；<br>（火山）熄灭的 | become extinct 灭绝<br>extinct species 灭绝的物种<br>extinct volcano 死火山<br>extinction n. 灭绝 | Dinosaurs have been **extinct** for millions of years. （恐龙已灭绝数百万年。）<br>Many languages are becoming **extinct**. （许多语言正在消亡。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：vanished（消失的）/ dead（死亡的）· **反义**：existing（存在的）/ thriving（繁荣的）· **易混**：extinct vs extinguish（使灭绝，动词）vs extinct ≠ extinct（≠ extinct vs distinct 明显的）|
| | eccentric | /ɪkˈsentrɪk/ | adj. 古怪的，反常的<br>n. 古怪的人 | eccentric behavior 古怪行为<br>eccentric old man 古怪老人<br>eccentricity n. 古怪 | The old professor was known for his **eccentric** habits. （那位老教授以古怪的习惯而闻名。）<br>People thought he was an **eccentric**, but he was just ahead of his time. （人们认为他古怪，但他只是超前于时代。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：odd（奇怪的）/ quirky（古怪的）/ unconventional（非传统的）· **反义**：conventional（传统的）/ normal（正常的）· **词根**：ec-出 + centr-中心 → 偏离中心的 → 古怪的|
| | lottery | /ˈlɑːtəri/ | n. 彩票，抽奖 | win the lottery 中彩票<br>lottery ticket 彩票<br>national lottery 国家彩票 | He won a million dollars in the **lottery**. （他买彩票中了100万美元。）<br>Getting that job was like winning the **lottery**. （得到那份工作就像中了彩票。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：raffle（抽奖）/ sweepstakes（抽奖）· **易混**：lottery vs pottery（陶器）vs lottery ≠ lottery（≠ lottery vs lot 大量）|
| | credential | /krəˈdenʃl/ | n. 凭证，证书（常用pl.） | academic credentials 学历<br>present credentials 出示凭证<br>teaching credential 教学资格证书 | She has excellent academic **credentials**. （她有出色的学历。）<br>He checked the applicant's **credentials** carefully. （他仔细核对了申请者的资质。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：qualification（资格）/ certificate（证书）/ diploma（文凭）· **易混**：credential vs credible（可信的）vs credential ≠ credential（≠ credential vs essential 必要的）|
| | assault | /əˈsɔːlt/ | n./v. 攻击，袭击；侵犯 | sexual assault 性侵犯<br>assault on 对…的攻击<br>assault rifle 突击步枪<br>under assault 受到攻击 | He was charged with **assault**. （他被控袭击罪。）<br>The castle was **under assault** for three days. （城堡被围攻了三天。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：attack（攻击）/ raid（突袭）· **反义**：defend（防御）/ protect（保护）· **易混**：assault vs insult（侮辱）vs assault ≠ assault（≠ assault vs result 结果）|
| | lest | /lest/ | conj. 以免，唯恐 | lest sb should do 以免某人做某事<br>lest we forget 以免我们遗忘 | She spoke quietly **lest** she wake the baby. （她轻声说话以免吵醒婴儿。）<br>They hurried **lest** they should miss the train. （他们急匆匆的，唯恐错过火车。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：for fear that（唯恐）/ in case（以防）· **易混**：lest vs less（更少）vs lest ≠ lest（≠ lest vs list 列表）· ⚠️ lest 后常用 should do 或虚拟语气（动词原形）|
| | flee | /fliː/ | v. 逃跑，逃离 | flee from 从…逃跑<br>flee the country 逃离该国<br>flee for one's life 逃命 | Refugees **fled** the war-torn region. （难民逃离了战乱地区。）<br>He grabbed his bag and **fled** the building. （他抓起包逃离了大楼。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：escape（逃跑）/ run away（跑掉）· **反义**：stay（留下）/ remain（保持）· **易混**：flee vs flea（跳蚤，同音！）vs flee ≠ flee（≠ flee vs free 自由）· ⚠️ 过去式/过去分词 fled|
| | synthesis | /ˈsɪnθəsɪs/ | n. 综合，合成 | synthesis of …的综合<br>protein synthesis 蛋白质合成<br>in synthesis 综合来看<br>synthesize v. 合成 | The report is a **synthesis** of various research findings. （报告是各种研究发现的综合。）<br>Photosynthesis is the **synthesis** of organic compounds from carbon dioxide and water. （光合作用是从二氧化碳和水合成有机化合物。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：combination（组合）/ integration（整合）· **反义**：analysis（分析）/ separation（分离）· **易混**：synthesis vs thesis（论文）vs synthesis ≠ synthesis（≠ synthesis vs synthetic 人造的）· 词根：syn-共同 + thesis 放置 → 放在一起 → 综合|
| | appall | /əˈpɔːl/ | v. 使震惊，使惊骇<br>（英：appal） | be appalled by/at 被…震惊<br>appalling conditions 骇人的条件<br>appallingly adv. 骇人地 | The public was **appalled** by the level of corruption. （公众对腐败程度感到震惊。）<br>The **appalling** living conditions shocked the visitors. （骇人的居住条件震惊了来访者。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：shock（使震惊）/ horrify（使惊骇）/ dismay（使惊愕）· **反义**：delight（使高兴）· **易混**：appall vs appeal（呼吁/吸引）vs appall ≠ appall（≠ appall vs apparel 服装）|
| | chamber | /ˈtʃeɪmbər/ | n. 房间；议院；<br>腔，室 | chamber of commerce 商会<br>heart chamber 心腔<br>Upper/Lower Chamber 上/下议院 | The bill was approved by the lower **chamber**. （法案由下议院批准。）<br>The patient has fluid in the lung **chambers**. （患者肺部有积液。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：room（房间）/ hall（厅）/ cavity（腔）· **易混**：chamber vs chamber ≠ chamber（≠ chamber vs chimney 烟囱）|
| | convict | /kənˈvɪkt/ v.<br>/ˈkɑːnvɪkt/ n. | v. 定罪，判罪<br>n. 囚犯，罪犯 | convict sb of 判某人有…罪<br>convicted criminal 已定罪的罪犯<br>conviction n. 定罪；坚信 | He was **convicted** of murder. （他被判谋杀罪成立。）<br>The escaped **convict** was caught within 24 hours. （逃犯在24小时内被抓获。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：sentence（判刑）/ prisoner（囚犯）· **反义**：acquit（宣告无罪）· **易混**：convict vs convince（说服）vs convict ≠ convict（≠ convict vs convert 转变）|
| | warehouse | /ˈwerhaʊs/ | n. 仓库，货栈<br>v. 仓储 | warehouse worker 仓库工人<br>data warehouse 数据仓库<br>warehousing n. 仓储 | The goods are stored in a large **warehouse**. （货物存放在一个大仓库里。）<br>Amazon operates massive **warehouses** around the world. （亚马逊在全球运营大型仓库。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：storage（仓库）/ depot（仓库）· **易混**：warehouse vs warehouse ≠ warehouse（ware + house = 货物的房子 → 仓库，好记！）|
| | salvation | /sælˈveɪʃn/ | n. 拯救，救赎；<br>解救者 | seek salvation 寻求救赎<br>salvation lies in 出路在于<br>salvation army 救世军 | Many people see education as their **salvation** from poverty. （许多人视教育为摆脱贫困的出路。）<br>He owes his life to the **salvation** of the firefighters. （他感谢消防员的救命之恩。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：rescue（救援）/ redemption（救赎）/ deliverance（解救）· **反义**：destruction（毁灭）/ ruin（毁灭）· **易混**：salvation vs salvage（打捞/挽救）vs salvation ≠ salvation（≠ salvation vs vacation 假期）|
| | assert | /əˈsɜːrt/ | v. 断言，声称；维护（权利） | assert that 断言…<br>assert one's rights 维护权利<br>assert oneself 彰显自己<br>assertion n. 断言 | He **asserted** that he was innocent. （他坚称自己是无辜的。）<br>She learned to **assert** herself in meetings. （她学会了在会议上彰显自己。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：declare（声明）/ claim（声称）/ maintain（坚称）· **反义**：deny（否认）· **易混**：assert vs assert ≠ assert（≠ assert vs asset 资产）vs assert ≠ assert（≠ assert vs assist 帮助）|
| | intrinsic | /ɪnˈtrɪnsɪk/ | adj. 固有的，内在的，<br>本质的 | intrinsic value 内在价值<br>intrinsic to …所固有的<br>intrinsically adv. 本质上 | Honesty is an **intrinsic** part of his character. （诚实是他性格中固有的一部分。）<br>The **intrinsic** value of education goes beyond career benefits. （教育的内在价值超越了职业利益。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：inherent（固有的）/ innate（天生的）/ essential（本质的）· **反义**：extrinsic（外在的）/ acquired（后天获得的）· **易混**：intrinsic vs internal（内部的）|
| | glamour | /ˈɡlæmər/ | n. 魅力，吸引力；<br>奢华 | glamour and glitter 光彩与魅力<br>glamour industry 时尚美容业<br>glamorous adj. 迷人的 | She was attracted by the **glamour** of show business. （她被演艺圈的奢华魅力所吸引。）<br>Hollywood is known for its **glamour** and fame. （好莱坞以其光彩与声名闻名。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：charm（魅力）/ allure（吸引力）/ prestige（声望）· **反义**：dullness（乏味）· **易混**：glamour vs grammar（语法）vs glamour ≠ glamour（英式拼写 glamour，美式 glamor）|
| | lubricate | /ˈluːbrɪkeɪt/ | v. 润滑，加润滑油；<br>使顺畅 | lubricate the engine 给发动机上油<br>lubricate the wheels 给车轮上油<br>lubricant n. 润滑油 | You should **lubricate** the machine regularly. （你应该定期给机器上油。）<br>Alcohol can **lubricate** social interactions. （酒精可以让社交更顺畅。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：oil（给…上油）/ grease（涂油脂）· **易混**：lubricate vs lubricant（润滑油，名词）|
| | exempt | /ɪɡˈzempt/ | adj. 被免除的，豁免的<br>v. 免除，豁免 | be exempt from 被免除…<br>tax-exempt 免税的<br>exempt sb from 免除某人…<br>exemption n. 免除 | Students are **exempt** from paying the fee. （学生免交这项费用。）<br>The goods are **exempted** from import duties. （这些货物免征进口关税。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：free from（免于）/ excuse（免除）/ immune（免疫的）· **反义**：liable（负有责任的）/ subject to（受…约束）· **易混**：exempt vs example（例子）vs exempt ≠ exempt（≠ exempt vs excerpt 摘录）|
| | layoff | /ˈleɪɔːf/ | n. 解雇，裁员；<br>停工期间 | mass layoffs 大规模裁员<br>announce layoffs 宣布裁员<br>temporary layoff 临时下岗 | The company announced massive **layoffs** last month. （公司上个月宣布了大规模裁员。）<br>He was affected by the **layoffs** at the factory. （他受到了工厂裁员的影响。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：dismissal（解雇）/ redundancy（裁员，英式）· **反义**：hiring（招聘）· **易混**：layoff vs payoff（回报/付清）· lay + off = 把人放下 → 解雇|
| | grave | /ɡreɪv/ | n. 坟墓<br>adj. 严重的，严肃的 | dig one's own grave 自掘坟墓<br>grave danger 严重危险<br>grave mistake 严重错误<br>graveyard n. 墓地 | The situation is **grave** and requires immediate action. （形势严峻，需要立即行动。）<br>He visited his father's **grave** every year. （他每年都去父亲的墓前祭扫。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：serious（严重的）/ tomb（坟墓）/ severe（严峻的）· **反义**：trivial（微不足道的）· **易混**：grave vs brave（勇敢）vs grave ≠ grave（≠ grave vs gravity 重力/严重性）·一词两义：坟墓 + 严肃/严重|
| | tariff | /ˈtærɪf/ | n. 关税；价目表 | import tariff 进口关税<br>raise tariffs 提高关税<br>tariff barrier 关税壁垒<br>tariff rate 关税率 | The government raised **tariffs** on imported steel. （政府提高了进口钢铁的关税。）<br>Tariff barriers protect domestic industries from foreign competition. （关税壁垒保护国内产业免受外国竞争。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：duty（税）/ tax（税）/ levy（征税）· **易混**：tariff vs tariff ≠ tariff（≠ tariff vs terrain 地形）|
| | shatter | /ˈʃætər/ | v. 粉碎，砸碎；<br>使破灭 | shatter dreams 粉碎梦想<br>shatter confidence 击碎信心<br>shatter into pieces 碎成片<br>shattered adj. 心碎的 | The window was **shattered** by the explosion. （窗户被爆炸震碎了。）<br>Her hopes were **shattered** when she heard the news. （听到消息后她的希望破灭了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：smash（打碎）/ crush（粉碎）/ destroy（毁灭）· **反义**：repair（修复）/ restore（恢复）· **易混**：shatter vs scatter（散开）vs shatter ≠ shatter（≠ shatter vs chatter 闲聊）|

| | whirl | /wɜːrl/ | v./n. 旋转；忙乱，眩晕 | in a whirl 一片忙乱<br>give it a whirl 试试看<br>whirlwind n. 旋风 | The leaves **whirled** in the autumn wind. （树叶在秋风中旋转飞舞。）<br>My head was **whirling** with so much information. （信息太多，我的头都晕了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：spin（快速旋转）/ swirl（打旋）· **易混**：whirl vs wheel（轮子）vs whirl ≠ whirl（≠ whirl vs while 当…时）|

| | inverse | /ˌɪnˈvɜːrs/ | adj. 相反的，反向的<br>n. 反面，倒数 | inverse relationship 反比关系<br>inverse proportion 反比例<br>inversely adv. 相反地 | There is an **inverse** relationship between supply and price. （供给和价格之间存在反比关系。）<br>The **inverse** of 2 is 1/2. （2的倒数是1/2。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：reverse（相反的）/ opposite（相反的）· **反义**：direct（直接的）· **易混**：inverse vs reverse（同义对，inverse偏数学/逻辑）vs inverse ≠ inverse（≠ inverse vs invert 倒转）|

| | orthodox | /ˈɔːrθədɑːks/ | adj. 正统的，传统的；<br>正教的 | orthodox view 正统观点<br>orthodox medicine 传统医学<br>Orthodox Church 东正教<br>unorthodox adj. 非正统的 | She holds fairly **orthodox** views on education. （她对教育持有相当正统的观点。）<br>His methods are far from **orthodox**, but they work. （他的方法远非正统，但很有效。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：conventional（传统的）/ traditional（传统的）· **反义**：unorthodox（非正统的）/ unconventional（非传统的）· **词根**：ortho-正 + dox 观点 → 正统观点|

| | defect | /ˈdiːfekt/ n.<br>/dɪˈfekt/ v. | n. 缺陷，缺点<br>v. 叛逃，背叛 | birth defect 先天缺陷<br>manufacturing defect 制造缺陷<br>defect to 叛逃到… | The product was recalled due to a **defect**. （产品因缺陷被召回。）<br>The scientist **defected** to the West. （那位科学家叛逃到了西方。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：flaw（缺陷）/ fault（缺点）/ desert（叛逃）· **反义**：perfection（完美）· **易混**：defect vs defect ≠ defect（注意重音：名词在首，动词在尾）vs defect ≠ defect（≠ defect vs detect 发现）|
| | decisive | /dɪˈsaɪsɪv/ | adj. 决定性的；果断的 | decisive victory 决定性的胜利<br>decisive action 果断行动<br>be decisive about 对…果断 | We need **decisive** leadership in times of crisis. （危机时刻我们需要果断的领导。）<br>The battle was a **decisive** turning point in the war. （这场战役是战争的决定性转折点。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：crucial（关键的）/ resolute（坚决的）· **反义**：indecisive（犹豫不决的）· **易混**：decisive vs decide（决定）vs decisive ≠ decisive（≠ decisive vs divisive 分裂的）|
| | conception | /kənˈsepʃn/ | n. 概念，观念；<br>构想，设想；受孕 | have no conception of 对…毫无概念<br>from conception to completion 从构想到完成<br>conceptual adj. 概念上的 | He has no **conception** of how difficult this task is. （他对这项任务的难度毫无概念。）<br>The project took two years from **conception** to launch. （项目从构想到发布花了两年。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：idea（想法）/ notion（观念）/ understanding（理解）· **易混**：conception vs concept（概念，同族词）vs conception ≠ conception（≠ conception vs perception 感知）|
| | inward | /ˈɪnwərd/ | adj. 向内的，内部的；<br>内心的<br>adv. 向内 （inwards） | inward feelings 内心感受<br>inward investment 对内投资<br>face inward 面朝内 | She turned her attention **inward** and reflected on her life. （她把注意力转向内心，反思人生。）<br>The door opens **inward**, not outward. （门朝内开，不是朝外。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：inner（内部的）/ internal（内在的）· **反义**：outward（向外的）/ external（外在的）· **易混**：inward vs inward ≠ inward（≠ inward vs toward 朝向）|

| | weep (复现) | /wiːp/ | v. 哭泣，流泪 | weep over/for 为…哭泣<br>weep with joy 喜极而泣 | She **wept** when she heard the sad news. （她听到悲伤消息时哭了起来。）|
| | snap (复现) | /snæp/ | v. 折断；厉声说；拍照 | snap at sb 厉声斥责<br>snap a photo 拍照 | The branch **snapped** in the wind. （树枝在风中折断了。）|
| | apparatus (复现) | /ˌæpəˈrætəs/ | n. 仪器，设备；机构 | state apparatus 国家机器 | The lab has state-of-the-art **apparatus**. （实验室有最先进的设备。）|
| | exaggerate (复现) | /ɪɡˈzædʒəreɪt/ | v. 夸张，夸大 | exaggerate the truth 夸大事实 | He tends to **exaggerate** his achievements. （他倾向于夸大成就。）|
| | pact (复现) | /pækt/ | n. 协定，条约，契约 | sign a pact 签署协议<br>peace pact 和平条约 | The two countries signed a trade **pact**. （两国签署了贸易协定。）|

| | considerable (复现) | /kənˈsɪdərəbl/ | adj. 相当大的，大量的 | considerable amount 大量<br>considerable progress 显著进步 | The project made **considerable** progress. （项目取得显著进展。）|

| | contagious (复现) | /kənˈteɪdʒəs/ | adj. 传染的；有感染力的 | contagious disease 传染病<br>contagious laughter 有感染力的笑声 | Yawning is **contagious**. （打哈欠会传染的。）|
| | eradicate (复现) | /ɪˈrædɪkeɪt/ | v. 根除，消灭 | eradicate poverty 消除贫困<br>eradicate disease 消灭疾病 | Smallpox has been completely **eradicated**. （天花已被彻底消灭。）|
| | lobby (复现) | /ˈlɑːbi/ | n. 大厅；游说团体<br>v. 游说 | hotel lobby 酒店大堂<br>lobby for/against 游说支持/反对 | We waited in the hotel **lobby**. （我们在酒店大堂等。）|
| | bail | /beɪl/ | n. 保释金；保释<br>v. 保释；舀水 | post bail 缴纳保释金<br>bail sb out 保释某人；解困<br>on bail 被保释 | He was released on **bail** pending trial. （他被保释等待审判。）<br>The government **bailed** out the failing bank. （政府救助了那家濒临倒闭的银行。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：bond（保证金）/ rescue（救助）· **易混**：bail vs bale（大捆）vs bail ≠ bail（≠ bail vs fail 失败）|
| | recede | /rɪˈsiːd/ | v. 后退，减弱；<br>（头发）变秃 | recede into the distance 退到远处<br>receding hairline 后退的发际线<br>recede from 从…后退 | The flood waters gradually **receded**. （洪水逐渐退去。）<br>His hair started **receding** in his twenties. （他二十多岁时发际线开始后退。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：retreat（撤退）/ withdraw（撤退）/ ebb（退潮）· **反义**：advance（前进）/ approach（接近）· **易混**：recede vs precede（先于）vs recede ≠ recede（≠ recede vs recess 休会期）|
| | amiable | /ˈeɪmiəbl/ | adj. 和蔼可亲的，友善的 | amiable personality 友善的性格<br>amiable smile 亲切的微笑 | He is an **amiable** old man, always ready to help. （他是位和蔼可亲的老人，总是乐于助人。）<br>They had an **amiable** conversation over coffee. （他们边喝咖啡边友好交谈。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：friendly（友好的）/ pleasant（愉快的）/ good-natured（脾气好的）· **反义**：hostile（敌对的）/ unfriendly（不友好的）· **易混**：amiable vs amicable（和睦的，amicable 多指关系）vs amiable ≠ amiable（≠ amiable vs amend 修订）|
| | sprout | /spraʊt/ | v. 发芽，抽条<br>n. 新芽，嫩芽 | bean sprout 豆芽<br>sprout up 迅速成长，涌现 | The seeds are beginning to **sprout**. （种子开始发芽了。）<br>New high-rise buildings have **sprouted** up all over the city. （新高层建筑在全城到处涌现。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：germinate（发芽）/ bud（发芽）/ shoot（抽枝）· **易混**：sprout vs spout（喷出）vs sprout ≠ sprout（≠ sprout vs sprout ≠ sprout）|
| | snobbish | /ˈsnɑːbɪʃ/ | adj. 势利的，自命不凡的 | snobbish attitude 势利的态度<br>snobbish behavior 势利行为<br>snob n. 势利眼 | She is too **snobbish** to mix with ordinary people. （她太势利了，不愿和普通人来往。）<br>His **snobbish** remarks offended everyone. （他势利的言论冒犯了所有人。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：arrogant（傲慢的）/ pretentious（自命不凡的）· **反义**：humble（谦逊的）/ down-to-earth（接地气的）· **易混**：snobbish vs snob（势利眼，名词）|
| | royalty | /ˈrɔɪəlti/ | n. 王室成员；版税 | royalty payment 版税支付<br>royal family 王室<br>in royalties 以版税形式 | The author earned millions in **royalties** from her bestseller. （作者从畅销书中赚了数百万版税。）<br>The **royalty** attended the state banquet. （王室成员出席了国宴。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：crown（王权）/ fee（费用）· **易混**：royalty vs royal（王室的）vs royalty ≠ royalty（≠ royalty vs reality 现实）|
| | transcend | /trænˈsend/ | v. 超越，超出 | transcend boundaries 超越界限<br>transcend differences 超越分歧<br>transcendental adj. 超越的 | Art can **transcend** cultural and linguistic barriers. （艺术可以超越文化和语言障碍。）<br>The beauty of nature **transcends** human understanding. （自然之美超越了人类的理解。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：surpass（超越）/ exceed（超过）/ go beyond（超出）· **易混**：transcend vs descend（下降）vs transcend ≠ transcend（≠ transcend vs trend 趋势）· 词根：trans-超越 + scend-爬 → 爬过去 → 超越|
| | subsidy | /ˈsʌbsədi/ | n. 补贴，补助金 | government subsidy 政府补贴<br>agricultural subsidy 农业补贴<br>subsidize v. 资助 | Farmers receive **subsidies** from the government. （农民从政府获得补贴。）<br>The **subsidy** helps families afford childcare. （这笔补贴帮助家庭负担得起托儿服务。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：grant（拨款）/ allowance（津贴）/ funding（资助）· **易混**：subsidy vs subsidiary（子公司/辅助的）vs subsidy ≠ subsidy（≠ subsidy vs subside 减退）|
| | ponder | /ˈpɑːndər/ | v. 沉思，仔细考虑 | ponder over/on 仔细思考…<br>ponder what to do 思考该做什么 | She **pondered** the question for a long time. （她长时间思考这个问题。）<br>He sat alone, **pondering** the meaning of life. （他独自坐着，思索人生的意义。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：contemplate（沉思）/ reflect on（反思）/ think over（仔细考虑）· **易混**：ponder vs wonder（好奇）vs ponder ≠ ponder（≠ ponder vs powder 粉末）|
| | deter | /dɪˈtɜːr/ | v. 阻止，威慑 | deter sb from doing 阻止某人做<br>deter crime 遏制犯罪<br>deterrent n. 威慑因素 | The high price **deterred** many potential buyers. （高价阻止了许多潜在买家。）<br>Fear of failure should not **deter** you from trying. （对失败的恐惧不应阻止你尝试。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：discourage（劝阻）/ prevent（阻止）/ dissuade（劝阻）· **反义**：encourage（鼓励）/ persuade（说服）· **易混**：deter vs detain（拘留）vs deter ≠ deter（≠ deter vs defer 推迟）|
| | malignant | /məˈlɪɡnənt/ | adj. 恶性的；恶意的 | malignant tumor 恶性肿瘤<br>malignant growth 恶性增长<br>malignancy n. 恶性 | The test revealed a **malignant** tumor. （检测显示了一个恶性肿瘤。）<br>His **malignant** intentions were finally exposed. （他恶毒的意图终于被揭露了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：cancerous（癌性的）/ malevolent（恶意的）· **反义**：benign（良性的）/ harmless（无害的）· **易混**：malignant vs malign（诽谤/恶意的）· 词根：mal-坏 + ign-产生 → 产生坏东西 → 恶性|
| | nationality | /ˌnæʃəˈnæləti/ | n. 国籍；民族 | dual nationality 双重国籍<br>minority nationality 少数民族 | What is your **nationality**? （你是什么国籍？）<br>China has 56 different **nationalities**. （中国有56个不同民族。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：citizenship（公民身份）/ ethnicity（种族）· **易混**：nationality vs national（国家的）vs nationality ≠ nationality（≠ nationality vs nationalism 民族主义）|
| | decent | /ˈdiːsnt/ | adj. 体面的，得体的；<br>相当好的 | decent job 体面的工作<br>decent living 体面的生活<br>decent person 正派人 | Everyone deserves a **decent** standard of living. （每个人都应享有体面的生活水平。）<br>He made a **decent** amount of money from the deal. （他从这笔交易中赚了相当可观的收入。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：respectable（体面的）/ proper（得体的）/ adequate（足够的）· **反义**：indecent（不雅的）/ shameful（可耻的）· **易混**：decent vs descent（下降）vs decimal（小数，已在⚡）|
| | predominant | /prɪˈdɑːmɪnənt/ | adj. 主要的，占优势的 | predominant role 主要角色<br>predominant view 主流观点<br>predominantly adv. 主要地 | English is the **predominant** language of international business. （英语是国际商务的主要语言。）<br>The **predominant** color in the painting is blue. （画作的主色调是蓝色。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：dominant（主导的）/ prevailing（盛行的）/ chief（主要的）· **反义**：minor（次要的）/ subordinate（从属的）· **易混**：predominant vs dominate（支配）vs predominant ≠ predominant（≠ predominant vs prominent 突出的）|
| | acquaintance | /əˈkweɪntəns/ | n. 熟人；认识，了解 | make the acquaintance of 结识…<br>casual acquaintance 泛泛之交<br>acquaint v. 使认识 | She is just a casual **acquaintance**, not a close friend. （她只是个泛泛之交，不是亲密朋友。）<br>I have some **acquaintance** with French literature. （我对法国文学略知一二。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：contact（熟人，联系人）/ familiarity（熟悉）· **反义**：stranger（陌生人）· **易混**：acquaintance vs acquaintance ≠ acquaintance（注意拼写 ac+quaint+ance）|
| | admission | /ədˈmɪʃn/ | n. 准许进入；入场费；<br>承认 | gain admission to 获准进入<br>admission fee 入场费<br>by one's own admission 据某人自己承认 | He gained **admission** to Harvard University. （他被哈佛大学录取了。）<br>By his own **admission**, he made a mistake. （据他自己承认，他犯了错误。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：entry（进入）/ confession（承认）/ access（准入）· **反义**：denial（否认）/ exclusion（排除）· **易混**：admission vs admit（承认/准许，动词）vs admission ≠ admission（≠ admission vs omission 省略）|
| | rhetoric | /ˈretərɪk/ | n. 修辞，雄辩术；<br>华丽辞藻（常贬义） | political rhetoric 政治辞令<br>empty rhetoric 空洞的辞藻<br>rhetorical question 反问句 | His speech was just empty **rhetoric** with no real plan. （他的演讲只是空洞的辞藻，没有实质计划。）<br>Don't be fooled by his clever **rhetoric**. （别被他巧妙的辞令所迷惑。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：eloquence（雄辩）/ bombast（华丽空话）· **易混**：rhetoric vs rhetorical（修辞的）vs rhetoric ≠ rhetoric（注意拼写 rhet+oric，不是 rethor+ic）|
| | lodge | /lɑːdʒ/ | n. 小屋；门房<br>v. 正式提出；寄宿；嵌入 | ski lodge 滑雪度假小屋<br>lodge a complaint 提出投诉<br>lodge with 寄宿在 | The bullet **lodged** in the wall. （子弹嵌入了墙里。）<br>He decided to **lodge** an appeal against the ruling. （他决定对裁决提出上诉。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：cabin（小屋）/ file（提交）/ embed（嵌入）· **易混**：lodge vs dodge（躲避）vs lodge ≠ lodge（≠ lodge vs ledge 窗台）|
| | constituent | /kənˈstɪtʃuənt/ | n. 选民；成分，构成要素<br>adj. 组成的 | constituent parts 组成部分<br>constituent assembly 制宪会议 | Hydrogen and oxygen are the **constituent** elements of water. （氢和氧是水的构成元素。）<br>The MP listened to the concerns of his **constituents**. （议员听取了他选区选民的意见。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：component（组成部分）/ element（元素）/ voter（选民）· **易混**：constituent vs constitution（宪法）vs constituent ≠ constituent（≠ constituent vs consist 由…组成）|
| | deviate | /ˈdiːvieɪt/ | v. 偏离，背离 | deviate from 偏离，背离<br>deviate from the norm 偏离常规<br>deviation n. 偏离 | Don't **deviate** from the original plan. （不要偏离原计划。）<br>His behavior **deviates** significantly from social norms. （他的行为显著偏离了社会规范。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：stray（偏离）/ diverge（分歧）/ depart（脱离）· **反义**：conform（遵守）/ adhere to（坚持）· **易混**：deviate vs device（装置）vs deviate ≠ deviate（≠ deviate vs devote 奉献）· 词根：de-离开 + via-路 → 偏离道路|
| | symmetry | /ˈsɪmətri/ | n. 对称，匀称 | perfect symmetry 完美对称<br>bilateral symmetry 两侧对称<br>symmetrical adj. 对称的 | The garden design emphasizes balance and **symmetry**. （花园设计强调平衡与对称。）<br>Her face has a natural **symmetry** that is pleasing to the eye. （她的脸有着自然的对称美，令人赏心悦目。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：balance（平衡）/ harmony（和谐）· **反义**：asymmetry（不对称）· **易混**：symmetry vs cemetery（墓地，拼写像！）vs symmetry ≠ symmetry（≠ symmetry vs symphony 交响乐）· 词根：sym-共同 + metry-测量 → 测量相同 → 对称|
| | plough | /plaʊ/ | n. 犁<br>v. 犁地；奋力前进<br>（美式：plow） | plough through 费力地通过<br>plough ahead 奋力前进<br>snow plough 扫雪机 | The farmer **ploughed** the field before planting. （农民在播种前犁了地。）<br>She **ploughed** through the pile of paperwork. （她费力地处理完那堆文件。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：till（耕作）/ push through（努力通过）· **易混**：plough vs cough（咳嗽）vs plough ≠ plough（美式拼写 plow）· 发音 /plaʊ/|
| | lure | /lʊr/ | n. 诱惑，吸引力<br>v. 引诱，吸引 | the lure of …的诱惑<br>lure sb into (doing) 引诱某人做<br>lure sb away from 引诱某人离开 | He was **lured** into a trap by the promise of easy money. （他被轻松赚钱的承诺引诱进了陷阱。）<br>The **lure** of city life attracted many young people. （城市生活的诱惑吸引了许多年轻人。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：tempt（引诱）/ bait（诱饵）/ attract（吸引）· **易混**：lure vs lure ≠ lure（≠ lure vs cure 治愈）vs lure ≠ lure（≠ lure vs lure ≠ lure）|

| | negligible | /ˈneɡlɪdʒəbl/ | adj. 微不足道的，<br>可以忽略不计的 | negligible amount 微不足道的量<br>negligible effect 可忽略的影响<br>negligible risk 极小的风险 | The cost is **negligible** compared to the benefits. （与收益相比，成本可以忽略不计。）<br>The difference between the two results is **negligible**. （两个结果之间的差异微乎其微。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：insignificant（微不足道的）/ trivial（琐碎的）/ minimal（最小的）· **反义**：significant（重大的）/ substantial（大量的）· **易混**：negligible vs neglect（忽视）vs negligible ≠ negligible（≠ negligible vs negligent 疏忽的）|
| | seal | /siːl/ | n. 印章；封条；海豹<br>v. 密封；盖章；确定 | seal a deal 敲定协议<br>official seal 公章<br>seal off 封锁 | They shook hands to **seal** the deal. （他们握手敲定了协议。）<br>The police **sealed** off the area after the explosion. （爆炸后警方封锁了该区域。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：stamp（盖章）/ close tightly（密封）/ finalize（敲定）· **易混**：seal vs sell（卖）vs seal ≠ seal（≠ seal vs cease 停止）· 一词多义：密封/印章/海豹|
| | obscure | /əbˈskjʊr/ | adj. 模糊的；鲜为人知的<br>v. 使模糊，遮蔽 | obscure meaning 模糊的含义<br>obscure figure 无名小人物<br>obscure the truth 掩盖真相 | The origins of the tradition remain **obscure**. （这一传统的起源仍然不明。）<br>The clouds **obscured** the sun. （云遮住了太阳。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：vague（模糊的）/ unknown（鲜为人知的）/ conceal（遮蔽）· **反义**：clear（清晰的）/ famous（著名的）/ reveal（揭示）· **易混**：obscure vs secure（安全的）vs obscure ≠ obscure（≠ obscure vs obscure ≠ obscure）|
| | irony | /ˈaɪrəni/ | n. 讽刺，反讽；<br>具有讽刺意味的事 | the irony is that 讽刺的是…<br>bitter irony 苦涩的讽刺<br>dramatic irony 戏剧性反讽<br>ironic adj. 讽刺的 | The **irony** is that he became a teacher after hating school as a child. （讽刺的是，他小时候讨厌上学，长大后却当了老师。）<br>She spoke with gentle **irony** about his "brilliant" plan. （她带着一丝讽刺说起他的"高明"计划。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：sarcasm（挖苦）/ satire（讽刺）· **易混**：irony vs iron（铁）vs irony ≠ irony（≠ irony vs ivory 象牙）|

| | lane (复现) | /leɪn/ | n. 车道；小路，小巷 | bus lane 公交车道<br>country lane 乡间小路 | Stay in the right **lane**. （保持在右车道。）|

| | brace (复现) | /breɪs/ | v. 使防备；支撑<br>n. 支架；牙箍 | brace oneself for 为…做好准备 | We **braced** ourselves for the storm. （我们为暴风雨做好了准备。）|
| | debut (复现) | /deɪˈbjuː/ | n./v. 首次亮相，初次登台 | make one's debut 首次亮相<br>debut album 首张专辑 | The band made their **debut** last year. （乐队去年首次亮相。）|
| | swarm (复现) | /swɔːrm/ | n. 群；v. 蜂拥 | a swarm of 一群<br>swarm with 挤满 | The beach was **swarming** with tourists. （海滩上挤满了游客。）|
| | clause (复现) | /klɔːz/ | n. 从句；条款 | main clause 主句<br>contract clause 合同条款 | A complex sentence has a main **clause** and a subordinate **clause**. （复合句有主句和从句。）|
| | ignorant (复现) | /ˈɪɡnərənt/ | adj. 无知的；不知道的 | be ignorant of 不知道<br>ignorant behavior 无知行为 | He was **ignorant** of the rules. （他不知道规则。）|
| | repertoire | /ˈrepərˌtwɑːr/ | n. 全部剧目；所有技能 | play in one's repertoire 某人能表演的节目<br>extensive repertoire 丰富的剧目/技能库 | The pianist has a wide **repertoire** of classical pieces. （这位钢琴家的古典曲目库很广。）<br>He added a new technique to his **repertoire**. （他的技能库中增加了一项新技术。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：collection（收藏）/ range（范围）/ skillset（技能组合）· **易混**：repertoire vs reportoire（常见拼写错误！正确为 r-e-p-e-r-t-o-i-r-e）|
| | tackle | /ˈtækl/ | v. 处理，应对；<br>（足球）抢断<br>n. 用具，装备 | tackle a problem 处理问题<br>tackle a challenge 应对挑战<br>fishing tackle 钓具<br>block and tackle 滑轮组 | The government must **tackle** the problem of unemployment. （政府必须处理失业问题。）<br>He **tackled** the defender and scored. （他抢断后卫并得分。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：address（处理）/ deal with（应对）/ handle（处理）· **易混**：tackle vs ticket（票）vs tackle ≠ tackle（≠ tackle vs ankle 脚踝）|
| | obstruction | /əbˈstrʌkʃn/ | n. 阻碍，障碍物；阻挠 | obstruction of justice 妨碍司法<br>remove an obstruction 清除障碍<br>obstruct v. 阻碍 | The fallen tree caused an **obstruction** on the road. （倒下的树在路上造成了障碍。）<br>The witness was charged with **obstruction** of justice. （证人被指控妨碍司法。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：obstacle（障碍）/ barrier（屏障）/ hindrance（妨害）· **反义**：assistance（帮助）/ clearance（清除）· **易混**：obstruction vs construction（建筑）vs obstruction ≠ obstruction（≠ obstruction vs destruction 破坏）|
| | elastic | /ɪˈlæstɪk/ | adj. 有弹性的；灵活的<br>n. 橡皮筋，松紧带 | elastic band 橡皮筋<br>elastic fabric 弹性面料<br>elastic demand 弹性需求 | The **elastic** waistband makes the trousers comfortable. （弹性腰带使裤子穿着舒适。）<br>The rules are quite **elastic** and can be adapted. （规则很灵活，可以调整。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：flexible（灵活的）/ stretchy（可拉伸的）/ rubbery（橡胶般的）· **反义**：rigid（僵硬的）/ stiff（硬的）· **易混**：elastic vs plastic（塑料的）vs elastic ≠ elastic（≠ elastic vs drastic 激烈的）|
| | hardship | /ˈhɑːrdʃɪp/ | n. 困难，艰苦 | economic hardship 经济困难<br>face hardship 面对艰难<br>suffer hardship 吃苦 | Many people suffered great **hardship** during the war. （许多人在战争期间遭受了巨大的苦难。）<br>The family endured financial **hardship** for years. （这个家庭多年承受经济困难。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：difficulty（困难）/ adversity（逆境）/ suffering（苦难）· **反义**：ease（舒适）/ comfort（安逸）· **易混**：hardship vs hard（硬的/难的）vs hardship ≠ hardship（≠ hardship vs friendship 友谊）|
| | breed | /briːd/ | v. 繁殖；饲养；<br>培育；导致<br>n. 品种，种类 | purebred 纯种的<br>breed dogs 养狗<br>breed resentment 引起怨恨<br>breeding n. 饲养；教养 | Poverty **breeds** crime and violence. （贫穷滋生犯罪和暴力。）<br>What **breed** of dog do you own? （你养的是什么品种的狗？）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：raise（饲养）/ reproduce（繁殖）/ cause（导致）/ variety（品种）· **易混**：breed vs bleed（流血）vs breed ≠ breed（≠ breed vs bread 面包）· 过去式/分词 bred|
| | hijack | /ˈhaɪdʒæk/ | v./n. 劫持（交通工具）；操纵 | hijack a plane 劫机<br>hijack the conversation 主导谈话<br>hijacking n. 劫持事件 | The terrorists **hijacked** the plane. （恐怖分子劫持了飞机。）<br>Critics accused him of **hijacking** the agenda for his own ends. （批评者指责他操纵议程为己谋利。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：kidnap（绑架）/ seize（夺取）· **易混**：hijack vs hitch（搭便车）vs hijack ≠ hijack（≠ hijack vs highjack 常见拼写错误→hijack正确）|
| | scheme | /skiːm/ | n. 计划，方案；阴谋<br>v. 图谋，策划 | a scheme to do 做…的方案/阴谋<br>pension scheme 养老金计划<br>scheming adj. 诡计多端的 | The company launched a new training **scheme**. （公司推出了新的培训计划。）<br>They **schemed** against their rivals. （他们密谋对付竞争对手。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：plan（计划）/ plot（阴谋）/ conspiracy（共谋）· **易混**：scheme vs schema（纲要/模式）vs scheme ≠ scheme（≠ scheme vs theme 主题）|
| | germ | /dʒɜːrm/ | n. 微生物，细菌；<br>萌芽，起源 | disease-causing germ 致病菌<br>germ of an idea 想法的萌芽<br>germ warfare 细菌战 | Wash your hands to kill **germs**. （洗手杀菌。）<br>The **germ** of the plan was discussed over coffee. （这个计划的初步构想是在喝咖啡时讨论的。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：bacterium（细菌）/ virus（病毒）/ seed（种子/萌芽）· **易混**：germ vs term（术语）vs germ ≠ germ（≠ germ vs germany 德国）|
| | manifest | /ˈmænɪfest/ | adj. 明显的，显而易见的<br>v. 显示，表明 | manifest itself 显现<br>manifest success 明显的成功<br>manifesto n. 宣言 | The symptoms first **manifested** themselves a week after exposure. （暴露一周后症状才显现。）<br>His dislike was **manifest** in his tone of voice. （他的厌恶在语气中表露无遗。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：apparent（明显的）/ evident（明显的）/ demonstrate（显示）· **反义**：latent（潜伏的）/ hidden（隐藏的）· **易混**：manifest vs manual（手册/手工的）vs manifest ≠ manifest（≠ manifest vs manipulate 操纵）|

| | pitch | /pɪtʃ/ | n. 音高；球场；程度；<br>v. 投掷；定调；搭帐篷 | pitch a tent 搭帐篷<br>sales pitch 推销说辞<br>pitch in 协力<br>at fever pitch 处于极度兴奋状态 | The fans were at fever **pitch** before the match. （赛前球迷热情高涨。）<br>We **pitched** our tent by the river. （我们在河边搭了帐篷。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：toss（投掷）/ throw（扔）/ camp（扎营）· **易混**：pitch vs ditch（沟/抛弃）vs pitch ≠ pitch（≠ pitch vs pitcher 罐子/投手）|
| | horror | /ˈhɔːrər/ | n. 恐怖，震惊；<br>憎恶 | in horror 惊恐地<br>horror movie 恐怖电影<br>to one's horror 令某人恐怖的是<br>horrify v. 使恐怖 | To his **horror**, he saw the car crash. （令他惊恐的是，他看到汽车撞了。）<br>She watched in **horror** as the building collapsed. （她惊恐地看着大楼倒塌。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：terror（恐怖）/ dread（恐惧）/ fright（惊吓）· **反义**：calmness（镇定）· **易混**：horror vs horrible（可怕的）vs honor（荣誉）|
| | culprit | /ˈkʌlprɪt/ | n. 罪犯，犯人；<br>引起问题的原因 | find the culprit 找到罪犯<br>main culprit 罪魁祸首 | The police are trying to find the **culprit**. （警方正努力寻找罪犯。）<br>Stress is the main **culprit** behind many health problems. （压力是许多健康问题的主要元凶。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：offender（罪犯）/ perpetrator（作案者）/ cause（原因）· **易混**：culprit vs culprit ≠ culprit（注意拼写 cul+prit）|
| | principal | /ˈprɪnsəpl/ | n. 校长；本金；委托人<br>adj. 主要的，首要的 | principal of a school 校长<br>principal amount 本金<br>principal focus 主要关注点<br>principal concern 首要关注 | The **principal** announced the new policy at the meeting. （校长在会上宣布了新政策。）<br>Her **principal** concern is the safety of the children. （她最关心的是孩子们的安全。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：headmaster（校长）/ main（主要的）/ primary（首要的）· **易混**：principal vs principle（原则，同音异义拼写不同！考研高频陷阱⚠️）|
| | edible | /ˈedəbl/ | adj. 可以吃的，可食用的 | edible mushrooms 可食用蘑菇<br>edible oil 食用油<br>barely edible 勉强能吃的 | These mushrooms are not **edible** — they're poisonous. （这些蘑菇不能吃——它们有毒。）<br>The food was barely **edible** but we were starving. （食物勉强能吃，但我们饿坏了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：eatable（可食用的）/ consumable（可消费的）· **反义**：inedible（不可食用的）/ poisonous（有毒的）· **易混**：edible vs eligible（有资格的）vs edible ≠ edible（≠ edible vs credible 可信的）|
| | colonel | /ˈkɜːrnl/ | n. 上校 | colonel command 上校指挥<br>Lieutenant Colonel 中校<br>Colonel Sanders 山德士上校 | He retired as a **colonel** in the army. （他以陆军上校的身份退役。）<br>**Colonel** Sanders founded KFC. （山德士上校创立了肯德基。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：captain（上尉）/ general（将军）· **易混**：colonel vs colonel ≠ colonel（注意发音/ˈkɜːrnl/，不读/koˈloʊnel/！l不发音！）|
| | scandal | /ˈskændl/ | n. 丑闻，丑事；<br>流言蜚语 | political scandal 政治丑闻<br>cause a scandal 引起丑闻<br>be involved in a scandal 卷入丑闻<br>scandalous adj. 可耻的 | The **scandal** ruined his political career. （丑闻毁了他的政治生涯。）<br>The newspaper is full of **scandal** and gossip. （报纸上满是丑闻和八卦。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：disgrace（耻辱）/ outrage（丑行）· **易混**：scandal vs scandal ≠ scandal（≠ scandal vs scan 扫描）|
| | humble | /ˈhʌmbl/ | adj. 谦逊的，谦虚的；<br>卑微的<br>v. 使谦卑 | humble person 谦逊的人<br>humble beginnings 卑微的出身<br>eat humble pie 低头认错<br>humility n. 谦逊 | Despite his wealth, he remains **humble**. （尽管他富有，却依然谦逊。）<br>He started his career from **humble** beginnings. （他从卑微的出身开始职业生涯。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：modest（谦虚的）/ meek（温顺的）· **反义**：arrogant（傲慢的）/ proud（骄傲的）· **易混**：humble vs tremble（颤抖）vs humble ≠ humble（≠ humble vs stumble 绊倒）|
| | supervise | /ˈsuːpərvaɪz/ | v. 监督，管理；指导 | supervise the work 监督工作<br>supervise employees 管理员工<br>supervision n. 监督<br>supervisor n. 主管 | She **supervises** a team of 20 people. （她管理一个20人的团队。）<br>The construction must be **supervised** by a qualified engineer. （施工必须由合格工程师监督。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：oversee（监督）/ manage（管理）/ direct（指导）· **易混**：supervise vs superwise（✗ 常见拼写错误）vs supervise ≠ supervise（≠ supervise vs supreme 至高无上的）|
| | favor | /ˈfeɪvər/ | n. 好意，恩惠；支持<br>v. 偏爱，偏袒；有利于<br>（英：favour） | do sb a favor 帮某人一个忙<br>in favor of 赞成<br>favorable adj. 有利的<br>favorite adj. 最喜欢的 | Could you do me a **favor**? （你能帮我个忙吗？）<br>The majority voted **in favor** of the proposal. （多数人投票赞成该提议。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：kindness（好意）/ support（支持）· **反义**：disfavor（不赞成）· **易混**：favor vs flavor（风味/味道，多一个l）vs favor ≠ favor（≠ favor vs fever 发烧）|
| | empire | /ˈempaɪər/ | n. 帝国；大企业 | Roman Empire 罗马帝国<br>business empire 商业帝国<br>empire building 帝国建设 | The **empire** stretched across three continents. （帝国横跨三大洲。）<br>He built a media **empire** from scratch. （他白手起家建立了一个媒体帝国。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：kingdom（王国）/ realm（王国/领域）· **易混**：empire vs umpire（裁判）vs empire ≠ empire（≠ empire vs expire 到期）|
| | despise | /dɪˈspaɪz/ | v. 鄙视，厌恶 | despise sb for (doing) sth 因…鄙视某人<br>despise violence 厌恶暴力<br>despicable adj. 可鄙的 | She **despises** people who lie. （她鄙视说谎的人。）<br>He **despised** himself for being so weak. （他因自己的软弱而鄙视自己。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：loathe（厌恶）/ hate（恨）/ scorn（蔑视）· **反义**：admire（钦佩）/ respect（尊敬）· **易混**：despise vs despite（尽管）vs despise ≠ despise（≠ despise vs dispute 争端）|

| | authentic | /ɔːˈθentɪk/ | adj. 真正的，正宗的；可靠的 | authentic food 正宗食物<br>authentic document 真实文件<br>authenticity n. 真实性 | The restaurant serves **authentic** Italian cuisine. （这家餐厅供应正宗的意大利菜。）<br>The signature was verified as **authentic**. （签名被验证为真实。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：genuine（真正的）/ real（真实的）· **反义**：fake（假的）/ counterfeit（伪造的）· **易混**：authentic vs author（作者）|
| | subscribe | /səbˈskraɪb/ | v. 订阅，订购；<br>同意，赞同 | subscribe to a magazine 订阅杂志<br>subscribe to a view 赞同观点<br>subscriber n. 订阅者<br>subscription n. 订阅 | I **subscribe** to several online platforms. （我订阅了几个在线平台。）<br>I don't **subscribe** to the view that money equals success. （我不认同金钱等于成功的观点。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：sign up（注册）/ support（支持）/ agree with（同意）· **易混**：subscribe vs describe（描述）vs ascribe（归因于）· 词根：sub-下 + scribe-写 → 在下面签名 → 订阅/同意|
| | contemporary | /kənˈtempəreri/ | adj. 当代的，现代的；<br>同时代的<br>n. 同代人 | contemporary art 当代艺术<br>contemporary literature 当代文学<br>contemporary of 与…同时代的人 | She is one of the finest **contemporary** artists. （她是最优秀的当代艺术家之一。）<br>He was a **contemporary** of Shakespeare. （他与莎士比亚是同时代人。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：modern（现代的）/ current（当前的）/ present-day（当代的）· **反义**：ancient（古代的）/ outdated（过时的）· **易混**：contemporary vs temporary（暂时的）vs contemporary ≠ contemporary（≠ contempt 蔑视）|
| | flaw | /flɔː/ | n. 缺点，缺陷，瑕疵 | character flaw 性格缺陷<br>design flaw 设计缺陷<br>flawless adj. 完美无缺的 | The diamond has a tiny **flaw**. （这颗钻石有一个微小瑕疵。）<br>There's a **flaw** in your argument. （你的论点有一个漏洞。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：defect（缺陷）/ fault（缺点）/ imperfection（不完美）· **反义**：perfection（完美）· **易混**：flaw vs flow（流动）vs flaw ≠ flaw（≠ flaw vs lawn 草坪）|
| | scarce | /skers/ | adj. 稀少的，不足的 | scarce resources 稀缺资源<br>make oneself scarce 躲开，溜走<br>scarcity n. 稀缺 | Water becomes **scarce** during the summer months. （夏季水资源短缺。）<br>Job opportunities are **scarce** in this area. （这个地区就业机会稀少。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：rare（稀有的）/ insufficient（不足的）· **反义**：plentiful（充足的）/ abundant（大量的）· **易混**：scarce vs scare（惊吓）vs scarce ≠ scarce（≠ scarce vs scarcely 几乎不）|
| | gut | /ɡʌt/ | n. 肠，内脏；<br>直觉（pl.）<br>v. 取出内脏；毁坏内部 | gut feeling 直觉<br>have the guts to do 有勇气做<br>gut-wrenching 令人揪心的 | I have a **gut** feeling that something is wrong. （我直觉感到出问题了。）<br>He didn't have the **guts** to confront her. （他没有勇气面对她。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：intuition（直觉）/ courage（勇气）/ innards（内脏）· **易混**：gut vs gut ≠ gut（≠ gut vs gust 一阵狂风）|

| | comprehensive | /ˌkɑːmprɪˈhensɪv/ | adj. 全面的，综合的；<br>详尽的 | comprehensive review 全面审查<br>comprehensive plan 综合计划<br>comprehensive coverage 全面覆盖<br>comprehension n. 理解力 | The report provides a **comprehensive** analysis of the market. （该报告对市场做了全面分析。）<br>She has a **comprehensive** understanding of the subject. （她对这个学科有全面的理解。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：complete（完整的）/ thorough（彻底的）/ all-round（全面的）· **反义**：partial（部分的）/ incomplete（不完整的）· **易混**：comprehensive vs comprehensible（可理解的）· 词根：com-全部 + prehens-抓 + -ive → 全部抓住→全面的|
| | dynamic | /daɪˈnæmɪk/ | adj. 动态的；充满活力的；<br>动力的<br>n. 动力；动力学 | dynamic economy 充满活力的经济<br>dynamic personality 活泼的个性<br>dynamic system 动态系统<br>dynamics n. 动力学；动态 | The company has a **dynamic** young team. （这家公司拥有一支充满活力的年轻团队。）<br>Prices are **dynamic** and change constantly. （价格是动态的，不断变化。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：energetic（精力充沛的）/ active（活跃的）/ vibrant（有活力的）· **反义**：static（静止的）/ passive（被动的）· **易混**：dynamic vs dynamite（炸药）· 词根：dynam-力量 + -ic → 有力量的→充满活力的|
| | perceive | /pərˈsiːv/ | v. 感知，察觉；<br>认为，理解 | perceive sth as 把…看作<br>perceive the difference 察觉差异<br>perception n. 感知；看法<br>perceptive adj. 感知敏锐的 | I **perceived** a change in his attitude. （我察觉到他的态度有变化。）<br>How do the public **perceive** the new policy? （公众如何看待这项新政策？）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：notice（注意到）/ observe（观察）/ discern（辨别）· **易混**：perceive vs receive（收到）vs deceive（欺骗）· 词根：per-完全 + ceive-抓住 → 完全抓住→感知|
| | embark | /ɪmˈbɑːrk/ | v. 上船，登机；<br>着手，开始 | embark on 开始，从事<br>embark on a career 开始职业生涯<br>embark on a journey 启程<br>embarkation n. 登船/登机 | She **embarked** on a new career in medicine. （她开始了新的从医职业。）<br>The passengers **embarked** at dawn. （乘客在黎明时登船。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：begin（开始）/ undertake（着手）/ set out（出发）· **反义**：disembark（下船）/ abandon（放弃）· **易混**：embark vs embark ≠ embark（≠ embank 堤岸）· 词根：em-上 + bark（船）→ 上船→开始|
| | intuition | /ˌɪntuˈɪʃn/ | n. 直觉，直觉力 | gut intuition 直觉<br>by intuition 凭直觉<br>intuitive adj. 直觉的<br>intuitively adv. 直觉地 | She had an **intuition** that something was wrong. （她直觉感到出问题了。）<br>**Intuition** told him to trust the stranger. （直觉告诉他要相信这个陌生人。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：instinct（本能）/ hunch（预感）/ gut feeling（直觉）· **易混**：intuition vs institution（机构）vs intuition ≠ intuition（≠ tuition 学费，少 in- 前缀）|
| | hatred | /ˈheɪtrɪd/ | n. 仇恨，憎恨 | deep hatred 深仇<br>hatred of/for 对…的仇恨<br>racial hatred 种族仇恨<br>hate v. 恨 | His **hatred** for injustice drove him to act. （对不公的憎恨驱使他采取行动。）<br>The war was fueled by racial **hatred**. （战争因种族仇恨而加剧。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：hostility（敌意）/ animosity（仇恨）/ loathing（厌恶）· **反义**：love（爱）/ affection（感情）· **易混**：hatred vs hate（恨，动词/名词）· 后缀：-red（表状态，如 hatred）|
| | triumph | /ˈtraɪʌmf/ | n. 胜利，成功；狂喜<br>v. 获胜，成功 | triumph over 战胜<br>in triumph 得意洋洋地<br>triumphant adj. 胜利的，得意洋洋的<br>trump card 王牌 | The team returned **in triumph** after winning the championship. （球队夺冠后凯旋而归。）<br>Justice **triumphed** over evil. （正义战胜了邪恶。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：victory（胜利）/ success（成功）/ conquest（征服）· **反义**：defeat（失败）/ failure（失败）· **易混**：triumph vs trumpet（喇叭）· 词根：tri-三 + umph → 三次欢呼→胜利|
| | cosmic | /ˈkɑːzmɪk/ | adj. 宇宙的；巨大的；<br>极其重要的 | cosmic rays 宇宙射线<br>cosmic dust 宇宙尘埃<br>cosmic event 宇宙事件<br>cosmos n. 宇宙 | Scientists study **cosmic** radiation from deep space. （科学家研究来自深空的宇宙辐射。）<br>The discovery had **cosmic** implications for physics. （这一发现对物理学有重大意义。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：universal（宇宙的）/ astronomical（天文的，巨大的）· **易混**：cosmic vs cosmetic（化妆品的，只差一个字母！）· 词根：cosmos（宇宙）+ -ic |
| | ore | /ɔːr/ | n. 矿石，矿砂 | iron ore 铁矿石<br>copper ore 铜矿石<br>gold ore 金矿石<br>ore deposit 矿床 | The region is rich in iron **ore**. （该地区富含铁矿石。）<br>**Ore** is mined from deep underground. （矿石从地下深处开采。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：mineral（矿物）/ metal ore（金属矿）· **易混**：ore vs core（核心，多一个c）vs ore ≠ ore（≠ oar 桨，同音！）|

| | compound | /ˈkɑːmpaʊnd/ n.<br>/kəmˈpaʊnd/ v. | n. 化合物；复合物；<br>大院<br>v. 使恶化；复合 | chemical compound 化合物<br>compound interest 复利<br>compound the problem 使问题恶化<br>compound sentence 复合句 | Water is a **compound** of hydrogen and oxygen. （水是氢和氧的化合物。）<br>The delays **compounded** the existing problems. （延误使现有问题更加恶化。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：mixture（混合物）/ aggravate（使恶化）/ worsen（恶化）· **反义**：simplify（简化）/ improve（改善）· **易混**：compound vs component（成分）vs composition（构成）· 名词重音在前，动词重音在后 |
| | grim | /ɡrɪm/ | adj. 严峻的，冷酷的；<br>阴沉的；令人沮丧的 | grim reality 严酷现实<br>grim news 坏消息<br>grim determination 坚毅的决心<br>grim face 冷峻的脸 | The economic outlook is **grim**. （经济前景严峻。）<br>He told us the **grim** news of the accident. （他告诉了我们事故的噩耗。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：gloomy（阴沉的）/ harsh（严酷的）/ stern（严厉的）· **反义**：cheerful（乐观的）/ bright（光明的）· **易混**：grim vs grin（咧嘴笑）vs grim ≠ grim（≠ grim vs grime 污垢）|
| | articulate | /ɑːrˈtɪkjuleɪt/ v.<br>/ɑːrˈtɪkjələt/ adj. | v. 清晰地表达<br>adj. 口齿清晰的，善于表达的 | articulate one's ideas 表达想法<br>articulate speech 清晰的演讲<br>articulate spokesperson 善于表达的发言人<br>articulation n. 表达 | She **articulated** her concerns clearly. （她清楚地表达了她的担忧。）<br>He is an **articulate** young man. （他是个善于表达的年轻人。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：express（表达）/ voice（表达）/ eloquent（雄辩的）· **反义**：inarticulate（不善表达的）· **易混**：articulate vs article（文章）vs articulate ≠ articulate（≠ articulate vs circular 圆形的）|
| | explicit | /ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/ | adj. 明确的，清楚的；<br>直率的；露骨的 | explicit instructions 明确指示<br>be explicit about 对…明确<br>explicit content 露骨内容<br>explicitly adv. 明确地 | The teacher gave **explicit** instructions for the assignment. （老师对作业给出了明确的指示。）<br>He was **explicit** about his intentions. （他明确表明了自己的意图。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：clear（清楚的）/ definite（明确的）/ specific（具体的）· **反义**：implicit（含蓄的）/ vague（模糊的）· **易混**：explicit vs implicit（含蓄的，反义对！）vs exploit（利用）|
| | infrared | /ˌɪnfrəˈred/ | n./adj. 红外线（的） | infrared rays 红外线<br>infrared sensor 红外传感器<br>infrared radiation 红外辐射 | **Infrared** cameras can see in the dark. （红外摄像机能在黑暗中看清物体。）<br>The remote control uses **infrared** signals. （遥控器使用红外信号。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：ultraviolet（紫外线的，对应词）· **易混**：infrared vs infra（拉丁语"下"）+ red（红）· 红外 = 光谱中红光之下，紫外线 = 紫光之上 |
| | timid | /ˈtɪmɪd/ | adj. 胆小的，羞怯的 | timid about 对…胆怯<br>timid child 胆小的孩子<br>timidity n. 胆小，怯懦<br>timidly adv. 胆怯地 | The **timid** girl hesitated to speak up. （那个胆小的女孩犹豫着不敢发言。）<br>He was too **timid** to complain. （他太胆怯，不敢抱怨。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：shy（害羞的）/ fearful（害怕的）/ cowardly（怯懦的）· **反义**：bold（大胆的）/ brave（勇敢的）/ confident（自信的）· **易混**：timid vs tepid（温吞的/微温的）vs intimidated（受恐吓的）· 词根：tim-害怕 |
| | abide | /əˈbaɪd/ | v. 忍受，容忍；遵守；<br>停留（旧） | abide by 遵守<br>abide by the rules 遵守规则<br>abide by the law 守法<br>can't abide 无法忍受 | All members must **abide by** the regulations. （所有成员都必须遵守规定。）<br>I can't **abide** such rudeness. （我无法忍受这种粗鲁。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：obey（遵守）/ comply with（遵从）/ tolerate（容忍）· **反义**：violate（违反）/ break（违背）· **易混**：abide vs bide（等待）vs abode（住所）· abide by 遵守，是考研高频搭配 |
| | velvet | /ˈvelvɪt/ | n. 天鹅绒，丝绒<br>adj. 天鹅绒的；柔软的 | velvet dress 天鹅绒连衣裙<br>velvet touch 柔软的触感<br>velvet voice 柔和的嗓音 | The queen wore a **velvet** gown. （女王穿着天鹅绒长袍。）<br>His voice was as smooth as **velvet**. （他的嗓音像天鹅绒一样柔和。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：fabric（织物）/ silk（丝绸）· **易混**：velvet vs velveeta（奶酪品牌，注意区分）· velvet glove 表面温和的手段 |
| | puff | /pʌf/ | v. 喷出，喘气；<br>吹<br>n. 一阵（烟/风）；喘息 | puff out 喷出<br>puff up 肿胀；膨胀<br>puff of smoke 一缕烟<br>puffed adj. 喘气的 | He **puffed** on his pipe. （他抽着烟斗。）<br>She was **puffing** after the long run. （长跑后她气喘吁吁。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：pant（喘气）/ gasp（喘气）/ blow（吹）· **易混**：puff vs puffin（海鹦）vs puff ≠ puff（≠ puff vs pour 倾倒）|
| | discourage | /dɪsˈkɜːrɪdʒ/ | v. 使泄气，使灰心；<br>劝阻，阻止 | discourage sb from doing 劝阻某人做<br>be discouraged by 因…而气馁<br>discouraging adj. 令人气馁的<br>discouragement n. 气馁 | Don't let failure **discourage** you. （不要让失败使你气馁。）<br>The bad weather **discouraged** us from going out. （坏天气使我们打消了出去的念头。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：dishearten（使灰心）/ deter（阻止）/ dissuade（劝阻）· **反义**：encourage（鼓励）/ inspire（激励）· **易混**：discourage vs encourage（鼓励，反义对！dis-表否定）· 结构：dis（不）+ courage（勇气）→ 使失去勇气 |
| | punctual | /ˈpʌŋktʃuəl/ | adj. 准时的，守时的 | be punctual for 准时参加<br>punctual arrival 准时到达<br>punctuality n. 准时<br>punctually adv. 准时地 | She is always **punctual** for meetings. （她开会总是准时。）<br>The train arrived **punctually** at 6:30. （火车准点于6:30到达。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：on time（准时）/ prompt（及时的）· **反义**：late（迟到的）/ tardy（迟到的）· **易混**：punctual vs punctured（被刺穿的）· 词根：punct-点（如 punctual 准"点"）|
| | plot | /plɑːt/ | n. 情节；阴谋；小块土地<br>v. 密谋；绘制 | plot of the novel 小说情节<br>plot against 密谋反对<br>plot a graph 绘制图表<br>a plot of land 一块地 | The **plot** of the movie was complicated. （这部电影的情节很复杂。）<br>They **plotted** to overthrow the government. （他们密谋推翻政府。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：scheme（阴谋）/ conspiracy（阴谋）/ storyline（故事情节）· **易混**：plot vs pilot（飞行员/试点）vs plod（沉重地走）· 一词多义：情节/阴谋/土地/图表，考研高频多义词 |
| | lucrative | /ˈluːkrətɪv/ | adj. 赚钱的，利润丰厚的 | lucrative business 赚钱的生意<br>lucrative market 利润丰厚的市场<br>lucrative contract 高利润合同<br>lucrativeness n. 获利 | He landed a **lucrative** job in finance. （他找到了一份金融界的高薪工作。）<br>The company signed a **lucrative** deal with a foreign firm. （公司与一家外国公司签署了一笔利润丰厚的交易。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：profitable（有利可图的）/ rewarding（有回报的）· **反义**：unprofitable（无利可图的）· **易混**：lucrative vs lucid（清晰的）vs lucrative ≠ lucrative（≠ lucrative vs luxury 奢侈）|
| | dissipate | /ˈdɪsɪpeɪt/ | v. 消散，驱散；<br>挥霍，浪费 | dissipate the fog 驱散雾气<br>dissipate doubts 消除疑虑<br>dissipate energy 浪费精力<br>dissipation n. 消散；挥霍 | The morning fog quickly **dissipated**. （晨雾很快消散了。）<br>He **dissipated** his inheritance on gambling. （他把继承的遗产挥霍在赌博上。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：disperse（驱散）/ scatter（散开）/ squander（挥霍）· **反义**：accumulate（积累）/ gather（聚集）· **易混**：dissipate vs desperate（绝望的）vs dissipate ≠ dissipate（≠ dissipate vs designate 指定）|
| | consolidate | /kənˈsɑːlɪdeɪt/ | v. 巩固，加强；合并 | consolidate power 巩固权力<br>consolidate one's position 巩固地位<br>consolidate the debt 合并债务<br>consolidation n. 巩固；合并 | The company **consolidated** its position in the market. （公司巩固了在市场上的地位。）<br>The two schools were **consolidated** into one. （两所学校合并成了一所。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：strengthen（加强）/ merge（合并）/ solidify（巩固）· **反义**：weaken（削弱）/ divide（分裂）· **易混**：consolidate vs console（安慰）vs solid（固体，词根）· 词根：con-共同 + solid-坚固 + -ate → 使共同坚固→巩固 |
| | beverage | /ˈbevərɪdʒ/ | n. 饮料 | alcoholic beverages 酒精饮料<br>hot beverage 热饮<br>beverage industry 饮料行业<br>soft drink 软饮料（同义） | The bar serves a variety of **beverages**. （这家酒吧供应各种饮料。）<br>Would you like a cold **beverage**? （想来点冷饮吗？）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：drink（饮料）/ refreshment（饮品）· **易混**：beverage vs beverage ≠ beverage（≠ beverage vs average 平均）· 正式用语，drink 更口语化 |

| | immune | /ɪˈmjuːn/ | adj. 免疫的；<br>不受影响的；免除的 | immune system 免疫系统<br>be immune to 对…免疫；不受…影响<br>immune from 免除…的<br>immunity n. 免疫力 | Vaccination makes you **immune** to the disease. （接种疫苗使你对这种疾病免疫。）<br>No one is **immune** to criticism. （没有人能免受批评。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：resistant（抵抗的）/ protected（受保护的）· **反义**：susceptible（易受影响的）/ vulnerable（脆弱的）· **易混**：immune vs immune ≠ immune（≠ immune vs impure 不纯的）· 词根：im-不 + mun-服务 → 不需服务→免疫 |

| | confer | /kənˈfɜːr/ | v. 授予，赋予；<br>商讨，协商 | confer sth on/upon sb 授予某人某物<br>confer a degree 授予学位<br>confer with sb 与某人商议 | The university **conferred** an honorary degree on her. （大学授予她荣誉学位。）<br>The manager **conferred** with his team before deciding. （经理在决定前与团队商议。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：grant（授予）/ award（颁发）/ consult（商议）· **易混**：confer vs prefer（更喜欢）vs refer（参考）· 词根：con-共同 + fer-带 → 带来一起→商议；授予 |

| | impose | /ɪmˈpoʊz/ | v. 强加；征收；<br>推行（规定） | impose sth on sb 把…强加于某人<br>impose a tax on 对…征税<br>impose a ban on 对…实行禁令<br>imposing adj. 壮观的，威严的 | The government **imposed** a new tax on imports. （政府对进口商品征收新税。）<br>Don't **impose** your views on others. （别把你的观点强加给别人。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：enforce（执行）/ levy（征收）/ force（强加）· **易混**：impose vs oppose（反对）vs propose（提议）· 词根：im-上 + pos-放 → 放在上面→强加 |
| | durable | /ˈdʊrəbl/ | adj. 耐用的，持久的；<br>耐久的 | durable goods 耐用品<br>durable material 耐用材料<br>durability n. 耐用性<br>duration n. 持续时间 | These shoes are made of **durable** leather. （这些鞋由耐用的皮革制成。）<br>The treaty created a **durable** peace. （该条约缔造了持久和平。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：lasting（持久的）/ long-lasting（耐用的）/ sturdy（坚固的）· **反义**：fragile（易碎的）/ temporary（短暂的）· **易混**：durable vs duration（持续时间）vs during（在…期间）· 词根：dur-持续 + -able → 能持续的→耐用的 |
| | contrast | /ˈkɑːntræst/ n.<br>/kənˈtræst/ v. | n. 对比，对照；反差<br>v. 对比，对照；形成对比 | in contrast 相比之下<br>in contrast to/with 与…形成对比<br>by contrast 对比之下<br>contrast A with B 把A与B对比 | **In contrast** to his brother, he is very outgoing. （与他哥哥相比，他很外向。）<br>The artist **contrasted** light with shadow. （艺术家把光与影作对比。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：comparison（比较）/ difference（差异）· **易混**：contrast vs compare（compare 侧重比较相同点，contrast 侧重对比不同点）vs contract（合同）· 词根：contra-相对 + st-站 → 相对而立→对比 |

| | overlook | /ˌoʊvərˈlʊk/ | v. 忽视，忽略；<br>俯瞰，眺望；<br>宽恕 | overlook a mistake 忽视错误<br>overlook the city 俯瞰城市<br>overlook sb's fault 宽恕某人的过错<br>overlooked adj. 被忽视的 | We **overlooked** an important detail. （我们忽略了一个重要细节。）<br>The hotel **overlooks** the sea. （这家酒店俯瞰大海。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：ignore（忽视）/ miss（错过）/ look down on（俯瞰）· **易混**：overlook vs oversee（监督）vs overlook ≠ overlook（≠ overlook vs outline 轮廓）· 一词多义：忽视 / 俯瞰 / 宽恕 |
| | recruit | /rɪˈkruːt/ | v. 招募，征募<br>n. 新兵，新成员 | recruit staff 招聘员工<br>recruit soldiers 招募士兵<br>new recruit 新成员<br>recruitment n. 招聘 | The company is **recruiting** new engineers. （公司正在招聘新工程师。）<br>The army **recruited** thousands of soldiers. （军队招募了数千名士兵。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：enlist（征募）/ hire（雇用）/ enroll（招收）· **易混**：recruit vs recruit ≠ recruit（≠ recruit vs routine 常规）· 词根：re-再 + cru-生长 → 使重新生长→补充新成员 |
| | infant | /ˈɪnfənt/ | n. 婴儿，幼儿<br>adj. 婴儿的；初期的 | infant mortality 婴儿死亡率<br>infant formula 婴儿配方奶粉<br>infant industry 新兴产业<br>infancy n. 婴儿期；初期 | The **infant** was sleeping peacefully. （婴儿睡得很安稳。）<br>The project is still **in its infancy**. （这个项目仍处于初期阶段。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：baby（婴儿）/ newborn（新生儿）· **反义**：adult（成年人）· **易混**：infant vs infantry（步兵）vs infant ≠ infant（≠ infant vs infallible 永无过失的）· 词根：in-不 + fant-说 → 不会说话的→婴儿 |

| | anguish | /ˈæŋɡwɪʃ/ | n. 极度痛苦，苦恼<br>v. 使极度痛苦 | in anguish 极度痛苦中<br>mental anguish 精神痛苦<br>anguished adj. 痛苦的 | She cried out **in anguish** at the news. （听到消息她痛苦地哭喊出来。）<br>The loss caused him deep **anguish**. （这一损失使他深感痛苦。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：agony（极度痛苦）/ torment（折磨）/ suffering（苦难）· **反义**：joy（快乐）/ delight（欣喜）· **易混**：anguish vs language（语言）vs anguish ≠ anguish（≠ anguish vs anxious 焦虑的） |
| | automobile | /ˈɔːtəməbiːl/ | n. 汽车（正式） | automobile industry 汽车工业<br>automobile accident 车祸<br>automaker 汽车制造商<br>automotive adj. 汽车的 | The **automobile** industry is a pillar of the economy. （汽车工业是经济支柱。）<br>He works for a major **automobile** manufacturer. （他在一家大型汽车制造商工作。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：car（汽车，口语）/ vehicle（车辆）/ motorcar（汽车，英式旧）· **易混**：automobile vs automatic（自动的）vs automobile ≠ automobile（≠ automobile vs automotive 汽车的）· 词根：auto-自己 + mobile-移动 → 自己移动的交通工具 |
| | perpetual | /pərˈpetʃuəl/ | adj. 永久的，永恒的；<br>无休止的 | perpetual motion 永动<br>perpetual contract 永久合同<br>perpetual fear 无休止的恐惧<br>perpetually adv. 永久地 | The two countries were in a **perpetual** state of conflict. （两国处于无休止的冲突状态。）<br>She lives in **perpetual** fear of failure. （她生活在永久的失败恐惧中。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：eternal（永恒的）/ everlasting（永久的）/ endless（无尽的）· **反义**：temporary（暂时的）/ transient（短暂的）· **易混**：perpetual vs perpetual ≠ perpetual（≠ perpetual vs personal 个人的）· 词根：per-贯穿 + pet-追求 + -ual → 一直追求→永久的 |
